## File: app/api/cron/notifications/route.ts
````typescript
import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { flushPending } from '../../../../lib/notifications/service';
import { producers } from '../../../../lib/notifications/producers';

// Constant-time comparison of the bearer token against CRON_SECRET
function isAuthorized(req: NextRequest): boolean {
  const secret = process.env.CRON_SECRET;
  if (!secret) return false;
  const header = req.headers.get('authorization') ?? '';
  const token = header.startsWith('Bearer ') ? header.slice(7) : '';
  const a = createHash('sha256').update(token).digest();
  const b = createHash('sha256').update(secret).digest();
  return timingSafeEqual(a, b);
}

async function handle(req: NextRequest) {
  if (!isAuthorized(req)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const now = new Date();
  const produced: Record<string, number | string> = {};
  for (const producer of producers) {
    try {
      produced[producer.name] = (await producer.run(now)).created;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(`[notifications] producer ${producer.name} failed`, e);
      produced[producer.name] = 'error';
    }
  }

  const flushed = await flushPending();
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, produced, flushed: flushed.processed });
}

// GET for Vercel Cron / simple pingers, POST for everything else
export const GET = handle;
export const POST = handle;
````

## File: app/goals/page.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { Suspense, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import dynamic from 'next/dynamic';
import { Network, Target, ListChecks } from 'lucide-react';
import AppHeader from '../../components/AppHeader';
import GoalDialog from '../../components/goals/GoalDialog';
import GoalsTab from '../../components/goals/GoalsTab';
import TasksTab from '../../components/goals/TasksTab';
import GoalsProvider, { useGoals, type GoalsTab as TabId } from './GoalsProvider';

// React Flow measures the DOM, so it only renders in the browser
const GraphTab = dynamic(() => import('../../components/goals/GraphTab'), {
  ssr: false,
  loading: () => <div className="h-[62vh] min-h-[380px] rounded-2xl border border-[#222222] animate-pulse bg-neutral-950" />,
});

const TAB_META: { id: TabId; label: string; icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }> }[] = [
  { id: 'goals', label: 'Goals', icon: Target },
  { id: 'tasks', label: 'Tasks', icon: ListChecks },
  { id: 'graph', label: 'Graph', icon: Network },
];

function GoalsView() {
  const { tab, setTab, goals, tasks, loadError, refresh } = useGoals();

  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState('');
  const [currentDate, setCurrentDate] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const update = () => {
      const now = new Date();
      setCurrentTime(now.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: true }));
      setCurrentDate(now.toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }));
    };
    update();
    const interval = setInterval(update, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  const counts: Record<TabId, number | null> = { goals: goals.length, tasks: tasks.length, graph: null };

  return (
    <div className="bg-black text-white font-sans antialiased min-h-screen selection:bg-white selection:text-black">
      <AppHeader currentDate={currentDate} currentTime={currentTime} />

      <main className="w-full pt-6 md:pt-8 pb-16 min-h-[calc(100vh-4rem)]">
        <div className="w-full max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6">
          <div className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 sm:p-6 md:p-8 relative border border-[#222222] shadow-2xl">
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-5 border-b border-[#222222]">
              <h1 className="font-headline text-2xl text-white tracking-tight font-semibold">Goals</h1>

              <div role="tablist" aria-label="Goal views" className="flex p-1 rounded-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 gap-1">
                {TAB_META.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon }) => (
                  <button
                    key={id}
                    role="tab"
                    aria-selected={tab === id}
                    onClick={() => setTab(id)}
                    className={`flex-1 sm:flex-none flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 sm:px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium transition-all ${
                      tab === id ? 'bg-white text-black font-semibold shadow-sm' : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {label}
                    {counts[id] !== null && <span className={`font-mono text-[10px] ${tab === id ? 'text-neutral-600' : 'text-neutral-600'}`}>{counts[id]}</span>}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {loadError && (
              <div role="alert" className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-950 px-4 py-3 text-xs text-neutral-300">
                {loadError}
                <button onClick={refresh} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all">Retry</button>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="pt-6" role="tabpanel">
              {tab === 'goals' && <GoalsTab />}
              {tab === 'tasks' && <TasksTab />}
              {tab === 'graph' && <GraphTab />}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      <GoalDialog />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function GoalsPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div className="min-h-screen bg-black" />}>
      <GoalsProvider>
        <GoalsView />
      </GoalsProvider>
    </Suspense>
  );
}
````

## File: app/notifications/actions.ts
````typescript
"use server";

import { prisma } from '../../lib/prisma';
import { getSession } from '../../lib/auth';
import { notify } from '../../lib/notifications/service';
import { getSettings, isTypeEnabled } from '../../lib/notifications/preferences';
import { isValidTimezone } from '../../lib/notifications/quietHours';
import {
  NOTIFICATION_TYPES,
  USER_TOGGLEABLE_TYPES,
  isNotificationType,
  type NotificationType,
} from '../../lib/notifications/types';

async function requireUserId(): Promise<string> {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session || typeof session.userId !== 'string') throw new Error('Unauthorized');
  return session.userId;
}

export interface NotificationState {
  enabled: boolean;
  timezone: string;
  quietStart: number | null;
  quietEnd: number | null;
  deviceCount: number;
  types: { type: NotificationType; label: string; description: string; enabled: boolean }[];
}

export async function getNotificationState(): Promise<NotificationState> {
  const userId = await requireUserId();
  const [settings, deviceCount] = await Promise.all([
    getSettings(userId),
    prisma.pushSubscription.count({ where: { userId } }),
  ]);
  return {
    enabled: settings.enabled,
    timezone: settings.timezone,
    quietStart: settings.quietStart,
    quietEnd: settings.quietEnd,
    deviceCount,
    types: USER_TOGGLEABLE_TYPES.map((type) => ({
      type,
      label: NOTIFICATION_TYPES[type].label,
      description: NOTIFICATION_TYPES[type].description,
      enabled: isTypeEnabled({ ...settings, enabled: true }, type),
    })),
  };
}

export interface PushSubscriptionInput {
  endpoint: string;
  keys: { p256dh: string; auth: string };
}

export async function subscribePush(sub: PushSubscriptionInput, userAgent?: string) {
  const userId = await requireUserId();

  const valid =
    typeof sub?.endpoint === 'string' &&
    sub.endpoint.length <= 2048 &&
    sub.endpoint.startsWith('https://') &&
    typeof sub.keys?.p256dh === 'string' &&
    typeof sub.keys?.auth === 'string' &&
    sub.keys.p256dh.length <= 256 &&
    sub.keys.auth.length <= 256;
  if (!valid) return { ok: false as const, error: 'Invalid subscription' };

  // An endpoint identifies one browser; if it was registered by another account, move it to this one
  await prisma.pushSubscription.upsert({
    where: { endpoint: sub.endpoint },
    create: {
      userId,
      endpoint: sub.endpoint,
      p256dh: sub.keys.p256dh,
      auth: sub.keys.auth,
      userAgent: userAgent?.slice(0, 300),
    },
    update: { userId, p256dh: sub.keys.p256dh, auth: sub.keys.auth, userAgent: userAgent?.slice(0, 300) },
  });
  return { ok: true as const };
}

export async function unsubscribePush(endpoint: string) {
  const userId = await requireUserId();
  await prisma.pushSubscription.deleteMany({ where: { endpoint, userId } });
  return { ok: true as const };
}

export interface NotificationSettingsInput {
  enabled: boolean;
  timezone: string;
  quietStart: number | null;
  quietEnd: number | null;
  typePrefs: Record<string, boolean>;
}

const isMinute = (v: unknown): v is number => Number.isInteger(v) && (v as number) >= 0 && (v as number) < 1440;

export async function updateNotificationSettings(input: NotificationSettingsInput) {
  const userId = await requireUserId();

  if (!isValidTimezone(input.timezone)) return { ok: false as const, error: 'Invalid timezone' };
  const quietOn = input.quietStart !== null && input.quietEnd !== null;
  if (quietOn && (!isMinute(input.quietStart) || !isMinute(input.quietEnd))) {
    return { ok: false as const, error: 'Invalid quiet hours' };
  }

  const typePrefs: Record<string, boolean> = {};
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(input.typePrefs ?? {})) {
    if (isNotificationType(key) && USER_TOGGLEABLE_TYPES.includes(key) && typeof value === 'boolean') {
      typePrefs[key] = value;
    }
  }

  const data = {
    enabled: Boolean(input.enabled),
    timezone: input.timezone,
    quietStart: quietOn ? input.quietStart : null,
    quietEnd: quietOn ? input.quietEnd : null,
    typePrefs,
  };
  await prisma.notificationSettings.upsert({
    where: { userId },
    create: { userId, ...data },
    update: data,
  });
  return { ok: true as const };
}

export async function sendTestNotification() {
  const userId = await requireUserId();
  const result = await notify({
    userId,
    type: 'TEST',
    dedupeKey: `test:${userId}:${Date.now()}`,
    title: 'Notifications are working',
    body: 'You will get reminders here even when the app is closed.',
    url: '/',
    expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 2 * 60_000),
  });

  const messages: Record<string, string> = {
    SENT: 'Test notification sent.',
    SKIPPED:
      result.reason === 'no_devices'
        ? 'No device is subscribed yet. Enable notifications on this device first.'
        : result.reason === 'disabled'
          ? 'Notifications are turned off in your settings.'
          : 'Notification was skipped.',
    RETRY: 'Could not reach the push service, will retry shortly.',
    FAILED: `Delivery failed: ${result.reason ?? 'unknown error'}`,
    DEFERRED: 'Notification is held until quiet hours end.',
    DUPLICATE: 'Notification was already handled.',
  };
  return { ok: result.status === 'SENT', message: messages[result.status] ?? 'Done.' };
}
````

## File: app/validation/actions.ts
````typescript
"use server";

import { prisma } from '../../lib/prisma';
import { getSession } from '../../lib/auth';
import { isValidTimezone } from '../../lib/notifications/quietHours';
import { DEFAULT_REMINDER_TIMES, findDueGoals, getValidationConfig } from '../../lib/goalValidation/settings';
import { resumeDueGoals } from '../../lib/goalValidation/resume';
import {
  MAX_REMINDER_TIMES,
  type ValidationOverview,
  type ValidationSettingsInput,
  type ValidationState,
} from '../../types/validation';

async function requireUserId(): Promise<string> {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session || typeof session.userId !== 'string') throw new Error('Unauthorized');
  return session.userId;
}

const isMinute = (v: unknown): v is number => Number.isInteger(v) && (v as number) >= 0 && (v as number) < 1440;

export async function getValidationState(): Promise<ValidationState> {
  const userId = await requireUserId();
  const [row, pushDeviceCount] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.validationSettings.findUnique({ where: { userId } }),
    prisma.pushSubscription.count({ where: { userId } }),
  ]);
  return {
    enabled: row?.enabled ?? false,
    timezone: row?.timezone ?? 'UTC',
    dayStartMin: row?.dayStartMin ?? 420,
    reminderTimes: row?.reminderTimes ?? DEFAULT_REMINDER_TIMES,
    pushDeviceCount,
  };
}

export async function saveValidationSettings(
  input: ValidationSettingsInput
): Promise<{ ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }> {
  const userId = await requireUserId();

  if (typeof input?.timezone !== 'string' || !isValidTimezone(input.timezone)) return { ok: false, error: 'Invalid timezone.' };
  if (!isMinute(input.dayStartMin)) return { ok: false, error: 'Invalid day start time.' };
  if (!Array.isArray(input.reminderTimes) || !input.reminderTimes.every(isMinute)) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Invalid reminder times.' };
  }
  const reminderTimes = [...new Set(input.reminderTimes)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (reminderTimes.length < 1 || reminderTimes.length > MAX_REMINDER_TIMES) {
    return { ok: false, error: `Choose between 1 and ${MAX_REMINDER_TIMES} different reminder times.` };
  }

  const data = { enabled: Boolean(input.enabled), timezone: input.timezone, dayStartMin: input.dayStartMin, reminderTimes };
  await prisma.validationSettings.upsert({ where: { userId }, create: { userId, ...data }, update: data });
  return { ok: true };
}

// Goals that need validating right now (drives the header badge)
export async function getValidationOverview(): Promise<ValidationOverview> {
  const userId = await requireUserId();
  const now = new Date();
  await resumeDueGoals(now, userId);

  const cfg = await getValidationConfig(prisma, userId);
  if (!cfg.enabled) return { enabled: false, dueCount: 0, due: [] };

  const due = await findDueGoals(prisma, userId, cfg, now);
  return {
    enabled: true,
    dueCount: due.length,
    due: due.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, name: d.name, rootName: d.rootName })),
  };
}
````

## File: components/goals/dialog/DeleteGoalConfirm.tsx
````typescript
import React from 'react';
import { TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react';

interface DeleteGoalConfirmProps {
  goalName: string;
  subGoals: number;
  tasks: number;
  plans: number;
  busy: boolean;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onConfirm: () => void;
}

const plural = (n: number, one: string, many: string) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;

export default function DeleteGoalConfirm({ goalName, subGoals, tasks, plans, busy, onCancel, onConfirm }: DeleteGoalConfirmProps) {
  return (
    <div role="alertdialog" aria-label="Confirm goal deletion" className="rounded-2xl border border-neutral-600 bg-neutral-950 p-4 sm:p-5">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <TriangleAlert className="w-5 h-5 text-white shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="text-sm text-neutral-300 space-y-2">
          <p className="font-semibold text-white">Delete “{goalName}”?</p>
          <ul className="list-disc pl-4 space-y-1 text-xs text-neutral-400">
            <li>{plural(subGoals, 'sub-goal becomes', 'sub-goals become')} top-level (kept, and can be re-attached to another goal).</li>
            <li>{plural(tasks, 'task becomes', 'tasks become')} unattached (kept, with their progress).</li>
            <li>{plural(plans, 'plan is', 'plans are')} deleted permanently.</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
        <button onClick={onCancel} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-colors">Cancel</button>
        <button onClick={onConfirm} disabled={busy} className="px-5 py-2 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50">
          {busy ? 'Deleting…' : 'Delete goal'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/dialog/HistorySection.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { History } from 'lucide-react';
import type { GoalDetail } from '../../../types/goals';
import { formatDateTime } from './ModalShell';

type Entry =
  | { kind: 'validation'; at: string; id: string; direct: boolean; via: string | null; text: string }
  | { kind: 'pause'; at: string; id: string; reason: string; resumeAt: string; resumedAt: string | null; resumeKind: string | null; cascaded: number };

const RESUME_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  AUTO: 'resumed automatically',
  MANUAL: 'resumed early',
  COMPLETED: 'ended by completing the goal',
  DELETED: 'ended by deleting a parent',
};

export default function HistorySection({ detail }: { detail: GoalDetail }) {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

  const entries = useMemo<Entry[]>(() => {
    const list: Entry[] = [
      ...detail.validations.map<Entry>(v => ({ kind: 'validation', at: v.createdAt, id: v.id, direct: v.direct, via: v.viaGoalName, text: v.text })),
      ...detail.pauses.map<Entry>(p => ({ kind: 'pause', at: p.pausedAt, id: p.id, reason: p.reason, resumeAt: p.resumeAt, resumedAt: p.resumedAt, resumeKind: p.resumeKind, cascaded: p.cascadedCount })),
    ];
    return list.sort((a, b) => b.at.localeCompare(a.at));
  }, [detail.validations, detail.pauses]);

  if (entries.length === 0) return null;

  const toggle = (id: string) =>
    setExpanded(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
      else next.add(id);
      return next;
    });

  return (
    <details className="group">
      <summary className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400 hover:text-white">
        <History className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> History <span className="text-neutral-600">{entries.length}</span>
      </summary>
      <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2">
        {entries.map(e => {
          const open = expanded.has(e.id);
          const body = e.kind === 'validation' ? e.text : e.reason;
          return (
            <li key={e.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#222222] bg-[#0f0f0f] px-3 py-2.5">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-500">
                <span className="text-neutral-300">
                  {e.kind === 'validation' ? (e.direct ? 'Validated' : `Validated via ${e.via ?? 'a sub-goal'}`) : 'Paused'}
                </span>
                <span>{formatDateTime(e.at)}</span>
              </div>
              <p className={`mt-1.5 text-xs text-neutral-300 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words ${open ? '' : 'line-clamp-3'}`}>{body}</p>
              {body.length > 160 && (
                <button onClick={() => toggle(e.id)} className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-neutral-500 hover:text-white">{open ? 'Show less' : 'Show more'}</button>
              )}
              {e.kind === 'pause' && (
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-500">
                  Planned resume {formatDateTime(e.resumeAt)}
                  {e.resumedAt ? ` · ${RESUME_LABEL[e.resumeKind ?? ''] ?? 'resumed'} ${formatDateTime(e.resumedAt)}` : ' · paused now'}
                  {e.cascaded > 0 ? ` · ${e.cascaded} sub-goal${e.cascaded === 1 ? '' : 's'} paused too` : ''}
                </p>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </details>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/dialog/ModalShell.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';

export const formatDateTime = (iso: string) =>
  new Date(iso).toLocaleString([], { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' });

// Small modal that sits above the goal dialog. Portalled so the dialog's transform cannot trap its fixed positioning.
export default function ModalShell({
  title,
  onClose,
  children,
}: {
  title: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return createPortal(
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 p-4"
      onMouseDown={e => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose(); }}
    >
      <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={title} className="w-full max-w-lg max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain rounded-2xl border border-[#222222] bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <h2 className="font-headline text-sm font-bold text-white">{title}</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close" className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:border-neutral-600 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors">
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/dialog/PauseBanner.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { Pause } from 'lucide-react';
import type { GoalDetail } from '../../../types/goals';
import { formatDateTime } from './ModalShell';

interface PauseBannerProps {
  detail: GoalDetail;
  busy: boolean;
  onResume: () => void;
  onChangeDate: () => void;
  onOpenRoot: (id: string) => void;
}

export default function PauseBanner({ detail, busy, onResume, onChangeDate, onOpenRoot }: PauseBannerProps) {
  if (detail.status !== 'PAUSED') return null;

  // Paused together with another goal: only that goal can be resumed
  if (detail.pausedByGoalId) {
    return (
      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-neutral-700 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-neutral-300">
        <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 text-neutral-500" />
        <span className="min-w-0">
          Paused with{' '}
          <button onClick={() => onOpenRoot(detail.pausedByGoalId!)} className="font-semibold text-white underline underline-offset-2 hover:no-underline">
            {detail.pausedByGoalName ?? 'another goal'}
          </button>
          {detail.pausedUntil && <> · resumes {formatDateTime(detail.pausedUntil)}</>}
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-neutral-700 px-3 py-2.5 space-y-2">
      <p className="flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-neutral-300">
        <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-neutral-500" />
        <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
          Paused{detail.pausedUntil && <> until <span className="font-mono text-white">{formatDateTime(detail.pausedUntil)}</span></>}
          {detail.pauseReason && <> — <span className="text-neutral-400">{detail.pauseReason}</span></>}
        </span>
      </p>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <button disabled={busy} onClick={onResume} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50">Resume now</button>
        <button disabled={busy} onClick={onChangeDate} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white hover:border-white active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50">Change date</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

## File: components/goals/dialog/PauseGoalDialog.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { pauseGoal, updatePause } from '../../../app/goals/actions';
import { LONG_TEXT_MAX, MAX_PAUSE_DAYS, MIN_PAUSE_LEAD_MIN, PAUSE_MIN_WORDS, countWords } from '../../../types/validation';
import ModalShell from './ModalShell';

interface PauseGoalDialogProps {
  goalId: string;
  goalName: string;
  mode: 'create' | 'edit';
  currentResumeAt?: string | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onDone: (message: string) => void;
  onError: (message: string) => void;
}

// <input type="datetime-local"> wants local "YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm"
const toLocalInput = (d: Date) => {
  const p = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}T${p(d.getHours())}:${p(d.getMinutes())}`;
};

const atNineIn = (days: number) => {
  const d = new Date();
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + days);
  d.setHours(9, 0, 0, 0);
  return toLocalInput(d);
};

const CHIPS = [
  { label: 'Tomorrow 9:00', days: 1 },
  { label: '3 days', days: 3 },
  { label: '1 week', days: 7 },
  { label: '2 weeks', days: 14 },
  { label: '4 weeks', days: 28 },
];

export default function PauseGoalDialog({ goalId, goalName, mode, currentResumeAt, onClose, onDone, onError }: PauseGoalDialogProps) {
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
  const [resumeAt, setResumeAt] = useState(currentResumeAt ? toLocalInput(new Date(currentResumeAt)) : '');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // Fixed when the dialog opens; the server re-checks the same bounds
  const [{ min, max }] = useState(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    return {
      min: toLocalInput(new Date(now + MIN_PAUSE_LEAD_MIN * 60_000)),
      max: toLocalInput(new Date(now + MAX_PAUSE_DAYS * 86_400_000)),
    };
  });

  const words = countWords(reason);
  const needReason = mode === 'create';
  const ready = Boolean(resumeAt) && (!needReason || words >= PAUSE_MIN_WORDS);

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!ready || busy) return;
    const when = new Date(resumeAt);
    if (Number.isNaN(when.getTime()) || resumeAt < min || resumeAt > max) {
      setError(`Choose a time between ${MIN_PAUSE_LEAD_MIN} minutes and ${MAX_PAUSE_DAYS} days from now.`);
      return;
    }
    setError('');
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      if (mode === 'create') {
        const res = await pauseGoal(goalId, { reason, resumeAt: when.toISOString() });
        if (res.ok === false) return onError(res.error);
        const n = res.data.cascadedCount;
        onDone(n > 0 ? `Goal paused · ${n} sub-goal${n === 1 ? '' : 's'} paused too` : 'Goal paused');
      } else {
        const res = await updatePause(goalId, { resumeAt: when.toISOString() });
        if (res.ok === false) return onError(res.error);
        onDone('Resume date updated');
      }
    } catch {
      onError('Could not update the pause.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <ModalShell title={mode === 'create' ? `Pause “${goalName}”` : 'Change resume date'} onClose={onClose}>
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-3">
        {mode === 'create' && (
          <>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400">Explain why you are pausing. In-progress sub-goals will pause too.</p>
            <textarea
              autoFocus
              value={reason}
              onChange={e => setReason(e.target.value)}
              maxLength={LONG_TEXT_MAX}
              rows={5}
              aria-label="Reason for pausing"
              className="w-full bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] focus:border-white rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-white placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none transition-all resize-y"
            />
            <p className={`text-[11px] font-mono ${words >= PAUSE_MIN_WORDS ? 'text-white' : 'text-neutral-500'}`}>{words} / {PAUSE_MIN_WORDS} words</p>
          </>
        )}

        <div className="space-y-2">
          <label className="block text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500" htmlFor="pause-resume-at">Resume on</label>
          <input
            id="pause-resume-at"
            type="datetime-local"
            value={resumeAt}
            min={min}
            max={max}
            onChange={e => setResumeAt(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-black border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-white text-xs font-mono outline-none focus:border-white"
          />
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {CHIPS.map(c => (
              <button key={c.days} type="button" onClick={() => setResumeAt(atNineIn(c.days))} className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 text-[11px] text-neutral-300 hover:text-white hover:border-neutral-600 transition-colors">
                {c.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">At most {MAX_PAUSE_DAYS} days. The goal then resumes automatically and needs validating.</p>
        </div>

        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-neutral-300">{error}</p>}
        <button type="submit" disabled={!ready || busy} className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-40 active:scale-95 transition-all">
          {busy ? 'Saving…' : mode === 'create' ? 'Pause goal' : 'Save date'}
        </button>
      </form>
    </ModalShell>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/dialog/PlansSection.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FileText, Plus, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { createPlan } from '../../../app/goals/actions';
import { useGoals } from '../../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import type { GoalDetail } from '../../../types/goals';

interface PlansSectionProps {
  detail: GoalDetail;
  onOpenPlan: (planId: string) => void;
}

function firstLine(body: string): string {
  const line = body.split('\n').map(l => l.replace(/^[#>\-*\s]+/, '').trim()).find(l => l.length > 0);
  return line ?? 'Empty plan';
}

export default function PlansSection({ detail, onOpenPlan }: PlansSectionProps) {
  const { notify, refresh } = useGoals();
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);

  const handleCreate = async () => {
    if (creating) return;
    setCreating(true);
    try {
      const res = await createPlan(detail.id);
      if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
      await refresh();
      onOpenPlan(res.data.id);
    } catch {
      notify('Could not create the plan.');
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="plans-heading">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
        <h3 id="plans-heading" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400">
          <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Plans <span className="text-neutral-600">{detail.plans.length}</span>
        </h3>
        <button onClick={handleCreate} disabled={creating} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white hover:border-white active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50">
          <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> New plan
        </button>
      </div>

      {detail.plans.length === 0 ? (
        <button onClick={handleCreate} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-dashed border-neutral-800 py-6 text-xs text-neutral-500 hover:border-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-300 transition-colors">
          No plans yet. Write your first one.
        </button>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-2">
          {detail.plans.map(p => (
            <li key={p.id}>
              <button onClick={() => onOpenPlan(p.id)} className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[#222222] bg-[#0f0f0f] px-4 py-3 text-left hover:border-neutral-600 transition-colors group">
                <span className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="block text-sm font-medium text-white truncate">{p.title}</span>
                  <span className="block text-xs text-neutral-500 truncate mt-0.5">{firstLine(p.body)}</span>
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-600">
                  {new Date(p.updatedAt).toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}
                  <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-600 group-hover:text-white transition-colors" />
                </span>
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

## File: components/goals/dialog/ValidateGoalDialog.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { validateGoal } from '../../../app/goals/actions';
import { LONG_TEXT_MAX, VALIDATION_MIN_WORDS, countWords } from '../../../types/validation';
import ModalShell from './ModalShell';

interface ValidateGoalDialogProps {
  goalId: string;
  goalName: string;
  ancestors: { id: string; name: string }[]; // root first
  onClose: () => void;
  onValidated: (validatedCount: number) => void;
  onError: (message: string) => void;
}

export default function ValidateGoalDialog({ goalId, goalName, ancestors, onClose, onValidated, onError }: ValidateGoalDialogProps) {
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const words = countWords(text);
  const enough = words >= VALIDATION_MIN_WORDS;

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!enough || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await validateGoal(goalId, text);
      if (res.ok === false) return onError(res.error);
      onValidated(res.data.validatedCount);
    } catch {
      onError('Could not validate the goal.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <ModalShell title={`Validate “${goalName}”`} onClose={onClose}>
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-3">
        <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400">
          Reflect on this goal: what moved forward, what is blocking you, and what comes next.
        </p>
        <textarea
          autoFocus
          value={text}
          onChange={e => setText(e.target.value)}
          maxLength={LONG_TEXT_MAX}
          rows={8}
          aria-label="Validation text"
          className="w-full bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] focus:border-white rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-white placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none transition-all resize-y"
        />
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-mono">
          <span className={enough ? 'text-white' : 'text-neutral-500'}>{words} / {VALIDATION_MIN_WORDS} words</span>
          {ancestors.length > 0 && (
            <span className="text-neutral-500 truncate ml-3">Also validates: {[...ancestors].reverse().map(a => a.name).join(' › ')}</span>
          )}
        </div>
        <button type="submit" disabled={!enough || busy} className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-40 active:scale-95 transition-all">
          {busy ? 'Validating…' : 'Validate'}
        </button>
      </form>
    </ModalShell>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/dialog/ValidationBar.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { PERIOD_OPTIONS } from '../../../types/validation';
import type { GoalDetail } from '../../../types/goals';
import { formatDateTime } from './ModalShell';

interface ValidationBarProps {
  detail: GoalDetail;
  onValidate: () => void;
  onPeriodChange: (days: number) => void;
}

// Shown only while the validation feature is on and the goal is in progress
export default function ValidationBar({ detail, onValidate, onPeriodChange }: ValidationBarProps) {
  if (!detail.validationEnabled || detail.status !== 'IN_PROGRESS') return null;

  const periods = PERIOD_OPTIONS.includes(detail.validationPeriodDays as (typeof PERIOD_OPTIONS)[number])
    ? [...PERIOD_OPTIONS]
    : [...PERIOD_OPTIONS, detail.validationPeriodDays].sort((a, b) => a - b);

  return (
    <div className={`mt-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5 ${detail.needsValidation ? 'border-white bg-[#141414]' : 'border-[#222222] bg-[#0f0f0f]'}`}>
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 text-xs">
        {detail.needsValidation ? (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold text-white">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white animate-pulse" /> Needs validation
          </span>
        ) : (
          <span className="text-neutral-300">
            Validated{detail.validUntil ? <> · valid until <span className="font-mono text-white">{formatDateTime(detail.validUntil)}</span></> : null}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 justify-between sm:justify-end">
        <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
          Validate every
          <select
            value={detail.validationPeriodDays}
            onChange={e => onPeriodChange(Number(e.target.value))}
            aria-label="Validation period in days"
            className="bg-black border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-1.5 py-1 text-white text-[11px] font-mono outline-none focus:border-white"
          >
            {periods.map(d => <option key={d} value={d}>{d} {d === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
        <button
          onClick={onValidate}
          className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold active:scale-95 transition-all ${detail.needsValidation ? 'bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-200' : 'border border-neutral-700 text-white hover:border-white'}`}
        >
          {detail.needsValidation ? 'Validate' : 'Validate again'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

````typescript
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Search } from 'lucide-react';
import type { GoalSummary } from '../../types/goals';
import StatusBadge from './StatusBadge';

interface GoalPickerProps {
  goals: GoalSummary[];
  onPick: (goal: GoalSummary) => void;
  placeholder?: string;
  emptyText?: string;
  autoFocus?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

export default function GoalPicker({ goals, onPick, placeholder = 'Search goals…', emptyText = 'No goals available.', autoFocus = true, className = '' }: GoalPickerProps) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return q ? goals.filter(g => g.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) : goals;
  }, [goals, query]);

  return (
    <div className={`rounded-xl border border-[#262626] bg-[#101010] ${className}`}>
      <div className="relative border-b border-[#1f1f1f]">
        <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-500" />
        <input
          autoFocus={autoFocus}
          value={query}
          onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          aria-label={placeholder}
          className="w-full bg-transparent pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none font-mono"
        />
      </div>
      <ul className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto p-1">
        {filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <li className="px-3 py-4 text-center text-xs text-neutral-600">{query ? 'No matches.' : emptyText}</li>
        ) : (
          filtered.map(g => (
            <li key={g.id}>
              <button
                onClick={() => onPick(g)}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-left hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors"
              >
                <span className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="block text-xs text-white truncate">{g.name}</span>
                  {g.parentName && <span className="block text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-500 truncate">↳ under {g.parentName}</span>}
                </span>
                <StatusBadge status={g.status} />
              </button>
            </li>
          ))
        )}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

## File: components/goals/Markdown.tsx
````typescript
import React from 'react';

// Small markdown renderer that builds React elements (never raw HTML), so user text cannot inject markup.

const SAFE_LINK = /^(https?:\/\/|mailto:)/i;

function renderInline(text: string, keyPrefix: string): React.ReactNode[] {
  const nodes: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  const pattern = /(`[^`]+`)|(\*\*[^*]+\*\*)|(\*[^*]+\*)|(\[[^\]]+\]\([^)\s]+\))/g;
  let last = 0;
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
  let i = 0;

  while ((match = pattern.exec(text)) !== null) {
    if (match.index > last) nodes.push(text.slice(last, match.index));
    const token = match[0];
    const key = `${keyPrefix}-${i++}`;

    if (token.startsWith('`')) {
      nodes.push(<code key={key} className="px-1 py-0.5 rounded bg-neutral-800 text-neutral-200 font-mono text-[0.85em]">{token.slice(1, -1)}</code>);
    } else if (token.startsWith('**')) {
      nodes.push(<strong key={key} className="font-semibold text-white">{token.slice(2, -2)}</strong>);
    } else if (token.startsWith('*')) {
      nodes.push(<em key={key}>{token.slice(1, -1)}</em>);
    } else {
      const m = /^\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)\s]+)\)$/.exec(token)!;
      if (SAFE_LINK.test(m[2])) {
        nodes.push(<a key={key} href={m[2]} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="underline underline-offset-2 text-white hover:text-neutral-300">{m[1]}</a>);
      } else {
        nodes.push(token);
      }
    }
    last = match.index + token.length;
  }
  if (last < text.length) nodes.push(text.slice(last));
  return nodes;
}

export default function Markdown({ source }: { source: string }) {
  const lines = source.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').split('\n');
  const blocks: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  let i = 0;
  let k = 0;

  while (i < lines.length) {
    const line = lines[i];

    if (line.trim() === '') { i++; continue; }

    if (line.startsWith('```')) {
      const code: string[] = [];
      i++;
      while (i < lines.length && !lines[i].startsWith('```')) code.push(lines[i++]);
      i++;
      blocks.push(<pre key={k++} className="rounded-lg bg-black border border-neutral-800 p-3 overflow-x-auto text-xs font-mono text-neutral-300">{code.join('\n')}</pre>);
      continue;
    }

    const heading = /^(#{1,3})\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
    if (heading) {
      const level = heading[1].length;
      const cls = level === 1 ? 'text-xl font-semibold' : level === 2 ? 'text-lg font-semibold' : 'text-base font-semibold';
      blocks.push(<div key={k++} role="heading" aria-level={level} className={`font-headline text-white ${cls}`}>{renderInline(heading[2], `h${k}`)}</div>);
      i++;
      continue;
    }

    if (/^\s*([-*])\s+/.test(line)) {
      const items: string[] = [];
      while (i < lines.length && /^\s*([-*])\s+/.test(lines[i])) items.push(lines[i++].replace(/^\s*([-*])\s+/, ''));
      blocks.push(<ul key={k++} className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1">{items.map((t, n) => <li key={n}>{renderInline(t, `u${k}-${n}`)}</li>)}</ul>);
      continue;
    }

    if (/^\s*\d+\.\s+/.test(line)) {
      const items: string[] = [];
      while (i < lines.length && /^\s*\d+\.\s+/.test(lines[i])) items.push(lines[i++].replace(/^\s*\d+\.\s+/, ''));
      blocks.push(<ol key={k++} className="list-decimal pl-5 space-y-1">{items.map((t, n) => <li key={n}>{renderInline(t, `o${k}-${n}`)}</li>)}</ol>);
      continue;
    }

    if (line.startsWith('>')) {
      const quote: string[] = [];
      while (i < lines.length && lines[i].startsWith('>')) quote.push(lines[i++].replace(/^>\s?/, ''));
      blocks.push(<blockquote key={k++} className="border-l-2 border-neutral-600 pl-3 text-neutral-400 italic">{renderInline(quote.join(' '), `q${k}`)}</blockquote>);
      continue;
    }

    if (/^(-{3,}|\*{3,})$/.test(line.trim())) {
      blocks.push(<hr key={k++} className="border-neutral-800" />);
      i++;
      continue;
    }

    const para: string[] = [];
    while (i < lines.length && lines[i].trim() !== '' && !/^(#{1,3}\s|```|>|\s*([-*]|\d+\.)\s+)/.test(lines[i])) para.push(lines[i++]);
    if (para.length === 0) para.push(lines[i++]);
    blocks.push(<p key={k++}>{renderInline(para.join(' '), `p${k}`)}</p>);
  }

  if (blocks.length === 0) return <p className="text-neutral-600 italic">Nothing to preview yet.</p>;
  return <div className="space-y-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-neutral-300 break-words">{blocks}</div>;
}
````

## File: components/goals/Sparkline.tsx
````typescript
import React from 'react';

interface SparklineProps {
  values: number[];
  className?: string;
}

// 14 tiny bars; today is the last one and rendered solid white
export default function Sparkline({ values, className = '' }: SparklineProps) {
  const max = Math.max(...values, 0);
  return (
    <div className={`flex items-end gap-[3px] h-6 ${className}`} aria-hidden="true">
      {values.map((v, i) => {
        const h = max === 0 ? 8 : Math.max(8, Math.round((v / max) * 100));
        const isToday = i === values.length - 1;
        return (
          <span
            key={i}
            style={{ height: `${h}%` }}
            className={`w-[5px] rounded-sm transition-all duration-300 ${
              v === 0 ? 'bg-neutral-800' : isToday ? 'bg-white' : 'bg-neutral-500'
            }`}
          />
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
````

````typescript
import React from 'react';
import { Check } from 'lucide-react';
import type { GoalStatus } from '../../types/goals';

export const STATUS_LABEL: Record<GoalStatus, string> = {
  IN_PROGRESS: 'In progress',
  PAUSED: 'Paused',
  COMPLETED: 'Completed',
};

export default function StatusBadge({ status, className = '' }: { status: GoalStatus; className?: string }) {
  const base = 'inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider whitespace-nowrap';

  if (status === 'COMPLETED') {
    return (
      <span className={`${base} bg-white text-black ${className}`}>
        <Check className="w-3 h-3 stroke-[3]" /> {STATUS_LABEL[status]}
      </span>
    );
  }
  if (status === 'PAUSED') {
    return (
      <span className={`${base} border border-dashed border-neutral-600 text-neutral-500 ${className}`}>
        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-500" /> {STATUS_LABEL[status]}
      </span>
    );
  }
  return (
    <span className={`${base} border border-neutral-500 text-neutral-200 ${className}`}>
      <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white" /> {STATUS_LABEL[status]}
    </span>
  );
}

## File: components/validation/ValidationSettings.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { Plus, ShieldCheck, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { getValidationOverview, getValidationState, saveValidationSettings } from '../../app/validation/actions';
import { MAX_REMINDER_TIMES, VALIDATION_CHANGED_EVENT, VALIDATION_SETTINGS_EVENT, type ValidationOverview } from '../../types/validation';

const toTime = (min: number) => `${String(Math.floor(min / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(min % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;

const toMinutes = (value: string): number | null => {
  const m = /^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(value);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) * 60 + Number(m[2]) : null;
};

const REFRESH_MS = 5 * 60_000;

export default function ValidationSettings() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [overview, setOverview] = useState<ValidationOverview | null>(null);

  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [dayStart, setDayStart] = useState('07:00');
  const [times, setTimes] = useState<string[]>(['21:00']);
  const [deviceCount, setDeviceCount] = useState(0);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');

  const loadOverview = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setOverview(await getValidationOverview());
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[validation] could not load overview', e);
    }
  }, []);

  // Badge: on mount, every few minutes, and whenever goals change anywhere in the app
  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    void loadOverview();
    const timer = setInterval(loadOverview, REFRESH_MS);
    window.addEventListener(VALIDATION_CHANGED_EVENT, loadOverview);
    return () => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      window.removeEventListener(VALIDATION_CHANGED_EVENT, loadOverview);
    };
  }, [loadOverview]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    getValidationState()
      .then((s) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setEnabled(s.enabled);
        setDayStart(toTime(s.dayStartMin));
        setTimes(s.reminderTimes.map(toTime));
        setDeviceCount(s.pushDeviceCount);
      })
      .catch((e) => {
        console.error('[validation] could not load settings', e);
        if (!cancelled) setMessage(`Could not load settings: ${(e as Error).message}`);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === 'Escape' && setOpen(false);
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [open]);

  const save = async () => {
    const dayStartMin = toMinutes(dayStart);
    const reminderTimes = times.map(toMinutes);
    if (dayStartMin === null || reminderTimes.some((t) => t === null)) {
      setMessage('Enter a valid time for every field.');
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await saveValidationSettings({
        enabled,
        timezone: Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone,
        dayStartMin,
        reminderTimes: reminderTimes as number[],
      });
      if (res.ok === false) {
        setMessage(res.error);
      } else {
        setMessage(
          enabled && deviceCount === 0
            ? 'Saved. Enable notifications on this device (bell icon) to receive reminders.'
            : 'Settings saved.'
        );
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event(VALIDATION_CHANGED_EVENT));
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event(VALIDATION_SETTINGS_EVENT));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setMessage((e as Error).message || 'Could not save settings.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const badge = overview?.enabled && overview.dueCount > 0 ? (overview.dueCount > 9 ? '9+' : String(overview.dueCount)) : null;

  return (
    <>
      <button
        onClick={() => { setMessage(''); setOpen(true); }}
        aria-label={badge ? `Validation settings, ${overview?.dueCount} goals need validation` : 'Validation settings'}
        className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 hover:border-white text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-all flex items-center justify-center"
      >
        <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" />
        {badge && (
          <span className="absolute -top-1.5 -right-1.5 min-w-4 h-4 px-1 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-[9px] font-bold font-mono flex items-center justify-center">
            {badge}
          </span>
        )}
      </button>

      {/* Portal: the header's backdrop-blur would otherwise become the containing block of this fixed overlay */}
      {open && createPortal(
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
          <div
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-label="Validation settings"
            className="w-full max-w-md bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-5 max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-white font-headline">Validation</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} aria-label="Close" className="text-neutral-400 hover:text-white">
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <section className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-white">Goal validation</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
                    Write a short reflection on each goal regularly. Validating a sub-goal validates its parents too.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  role="switch"
                  aria-checked={enabled}
                  aria-label="Goal validation"
                  onClick={() => setEnabled((v) => !v)}
                  className={`w-9 h-5 rounded-full border transition-colors relative shrink-0 ${enabled ? 'bg-white border-white' : 'bg-neutral-900 border-neutral-700'}`}
                >
                  <span className={`absolute top-0.5 w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full transition-all ${enabled ? 'left-[18px] bg-black' : 'left-0.5 bg-neutral-500'}`} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className={`space-y-2 ${enabled ? '' : 'opacity-40'}`}>
              <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 font-mono">Day starts at</p>
              <input
                type="time"
                value={dayStart}
                onChange={(e) => setDayStart(e.target.value)}
                aria-label="Day start time"
                className="bg-black border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-white text-xs font-mono outline-none focus:border-white"
              />
              <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">A validation lasts until this time (times the goal&apos;s period in days).</p>
            </section>

            <section className={`space-y-2 ${enabled ? '' : 'opacity-40'}`}>
              <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 font-mono">Reminder times</p>
              {times.map((t, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <input
                    type="time"
                    value={t}
                    onChange={(e) => setTimes((prev) => prev.map((x, j) => (j === i ? e.target.value : x)))}
                    aria-label={`Reminder time ${i + 1}`}
                    className="bg-black border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-white text-xs font-mono outline-none focus:border-white"
                  />
                  {times.length > 1 && (
                    <button onClick={() => setTimes((prev) => prev.filter((_, j) => j !== i))} aria-label="Remove reminder time" className="text-neutral-500 hover:text-white">
                      <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
              {times.length < MAX_REMINDER_TIMES && (
                <button onClick={() => setTimes((prev) => [...prev, '12:00'])} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-neutral-300 hover:text-white">
                  <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Add time
                </button>
              )}
              <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
                At these times you get one push notification listing the goals that need validating. It uses the devices enabled from the bell icon, and the bell&apos;s
                &quot;All notifications&quot; switch still applies.
              </p>
              {enabled && deviceCount === 0 && (
                <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400">No device is registered for notifications yet. Enable them from the bell icon.</p>
              )}
            </section>

            <button disabled={busy} onClick={save} className="w-full text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50">
              Save
            </button>
            {message && <p role="status" className="text-xs text-neutral-300">{message}</p>}
          </div>
        </div>,
        document.body
      )}
    </>
  );
}
````

// components/CompletedTaskList.tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Check, AlertCircle, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';
import { Task } from '../types';

interface CompletedTaskListProps {
  tasks: Task[];
  onReviewTask: (task: Task) => void;
  onRestoreTask: (task: Task, e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
}

export default function CompletedTaskList({ tasks, onReviewTask, onRestoreTask }: CompletedTaskListProps) {
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(true);

  return (
    <section className="mt-4 pt-6 border-t border-neutral-800/40">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <button onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-neutral-300 hover:text-white transition-colors text-sm font-semibold">
          {isExpanded ? <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />}
          <span className="text-white font-headline">Completed</span>
          <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-neutral-900/80 text-neutral-400 border border-neutral-800/50 font-mono">{tasks.length}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {isExpanded && (
        <div className="space-y-2.5">
          {tasks.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-6 px-4 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-neutral-850/40 text-center text-xs text-neutral-500 bg-neutral-950/40">
              No completed tasks yet.
            </div>
          ) : (
            tasks.map((task) => {
              const isFailed = task.score === 'FAILED';
              return (
                <div key={task.id} onClick={() => onReviewTask(task)} className={`group flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 px-4 rounded-xl hover:bg-neutral-900/80 border cursor-pointer ${isFailed ? 'bg-red-950/20 border-red-900/50' : 'bg-neutral-950/70 border-neutral-800/40'}`}>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 min-w-0 w-full pr-4">
                    <div className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${isFailed ? 'bg-red-500 text-white' : 'bg-white text-black'}`}>
                      {isFailed ? <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />}
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden">
                      <p className={`text-sm font-medium truncate ${isFailed ? 'text-red-200' : 'text-neutral-200'}`}>{task.title}</p>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                        <span className="font-mono shrink-0">Finished {task.completedAt}</span>
                        {task.score && <><span className="text-neutral-600 shrink-0">•</span><span className={`font-mono font-bold text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shrink-0 ${isFailed ? 'bg-red-500 text-white' : 'text-black bg-white'}`}>{task.score}</span></>}
                        {task.reflection && <><span className="text-neutral-600 shrink-0">•</span><span className="text-neutral-400 italic truncate min-w-0">"{task.reflection}"</span></>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                    <button onClick={(e) => onRestoreTask(task, e)} className="text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-neutral-800/40 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" /> Restore
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            })
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

````typescript
import React from 'react';

interface ImportanceLightsHeaderProps {
  level?: number;
  lightColor?: 'white' | 'red' | 'amber' | 'yellow';
  className?: string;
}

export default function ImportanceLightsHeader({
  level = 3,
  lightColor = 'white',
  className = '',
}: ImportanceLightsHeaderProps) {
  
  let activeFill = '#ffffff';
  let activeStroke = '#ffffff';
  let filterStyle = 'drop-shadow(0 0 4px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.85)) drop-shadow(0 0 1px #ffffff)';

  if (lightColor === 'red') {
    activeFill = '#ff283b';
    activeStroke = '#ff4d5e';
    filterStyle = 'drop-shadow(0 0 4px rgba(255, 40, 59, 0.9)) drop-shadow(0 0 1.5px #ff283b)';
  } else if (lightColor === 'amber' || lightColor === 'yellow') {
    activeFill = '#f59e0b';
    activeStroke = '#fbbf24';
    filterStyle = 'drop-shadow(0 0 4px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.85)) drop-shadow(0 0 1.5px #f59e0b)';
  }

  return (
    <div className={`w-full flex items-center gap-1.5 h-[6px] mb-3.5 select-none pointer-events-none ${className}`}>
      {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((index) => {
        const isActive = index < level;
        return (
          <div key={index} className="flex-1 h-full relative">
            <svg
              className="w-full h-full overflow-visible block"
              viewBox="0 0 100 12"
              preserveAspectRatio="none"
            >
              <polygon
                points="6,1 94,1 99,6 94,11 6,11 1,6"
                fill={isActive ? activeFill : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03)'}
                stroke={isActive ? activeStroke : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12)'}
                strokeWidth={isActive ? '0.75' : '1'}
                strokeLinejoin="round"
                strokeLinecap="round"
                vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke"
                style={isActive ? { filter: filterStyle } : undefined}
              />
            </svg>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
