## File: lib/goals/derived.ts
````typescript
import type { GoalSummary, GoalTaskDTO, WorkTime } from '../../types/goals';
import { EMPTY_TIME, rollupTime } from './time';

// Derives rolled-up deep/shallow minutes, counts, and live parent/goal names from base goals + tasks.
// Pure and fast (< 0.1ms), so localized task/goal patches update every view without re-querying Postgres.
export function deriveGoalsAndTasks(
  rawGoals: GoalSummary[],
  rawTasks: GoalTaskDTO[],
): { goals: GoalSummary[]; tasks: GoalTaskDTO[] } {
  const ownTime = new Map<string, WorkTime>();
  const taskCounts = new Map<string, number>();

  for (const t of rawTasks) {
    if (!t.goalId) continue;
    taskCounts.set(t.goalId, (taskCounts.get(t.goalId) ?? 0) + 1);

    if (t.timeSpentMin > 0) {
      const bucket = ownTime.get(t.goalId) ?? { deepMinutes: 0, shallowMinutes: 0 };
      if (t.workType === 'DEEP') bucket.deepMinutes += t.timeSpentMin;
      else bucket.shallowMinutes += t.timeSpentMin;
      ownTime.set(t.goalId, bucket);
    }
  }

  const timeTotals = rollupTime(rawGoals, ownTime);
  const goalNames = new Map<string, string>();
  const subGoalCounts = new Map<string, number>();

  for (const g of rawGoals) {
    goalNames.set(g.id, g.name);
    if (g.parentId) {
      subGoalCounts.set(g.parentId, (subGoalCounts.get(g.parentId) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
  }

  const goals: GoalSummary[] = rawGoals.map(g => {
    const time = timeTotals.get(g.id) ?? EMPTY_TIME;
    return {
      ...g,
      parentName: g.parentId ? (goalNames.get(g.parentId) ?? g.parentName) : null,
      subGoalCount: subGoalCounts.get(g.id) ?? 0,
      taskCount: taskCounts.get(g.id) ?? 0,
      deepMinutes: time.deepMinutes,
      shallowMinutes: time.shallowMinutes,
    };
  });

  const tasks: GoalTaskDTO[] = rawTasks.map(t => ({
    ...t,
    goalName: t.goalId ? (goalNames.get(t.goalId) ?? t.goalName) : null,
  }));

  return { goals, tasks };
}
````

## File: lib/goals/graph.ts
````typescript
import type {
  GoalSummary,
  GoalTaskDTO,
  GoalTree,
  GraphGoalNode,
  GraphTaskNode,
} from '../../types/goals';

// Pure client-side BFS to build the goal tree from GoalsProvider's in-memory goals and tasks.
export function buildGoalTree(
  goals: GoalSummary[],
  tasks: GoalTaskDTO[],
  rootId: string,
  depth: 1 | 2,
  includeTasks: boolean,
): GoalTree | null {
  const maxLevel = depth === 2 ? 2 : 1;
  const byId = new Map(goals.map(g => [g.id, g]));
  const root = byId.get(rootId);
  if (!root) return null;

  const childrenOf = new Map<string, GoalSummary[]>();
  for (const g of goals) {
    if (!g.parentId) continue;
    const list = childrenOf.get(g.parentId) ?? [];
    list.push(g);
    childrenOf.set(g.parentId, list);
  }

  const nodes: (GraphGoalNode | GraphTaskNode)[] = [];
  const edges: GoalTree['edges'] = [];
  const goalLevels = new Map<string, number>();
  const queue: { id: string; level: number }[] = [{ id: root.id, level: 0 }];

  while (queue.length > 0) {
    const { id, level } = queue.shift()!;
    const g = byId.get(id);
    if (!g || goalLevels.has(id)) continue;

    goalLevels.set(id, level);
    nodes.push({
      kind: 'goal',
      id: g.id,
      name: g.name,
      status: g.status,
      level,
      subGoalCount: g.subGoalCount,
      taskCount: g.taskCount,
      deepMinutes: g.deepMinutes,
      shallowMinutes: g.shallowMinutes,
    });

    if (level < maxLevel) {
      for (const child of childrenOf.get(id) ?? []) {
        edges.push({ id: `e-${id}-${child.id}`, source: id, target: child.id });
        queue.push({ id: child.id, level: level + 1 });
      }
    }
  }

  if (includeTasks) {
    const visibleTasks = tasks
      .filter(t => t.goalId && goalLevels.has(t.goalId))
      .sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));

    for (const t of visibleTasks) {
      if (!t.goalId) continue;
      nodes.push({
        kind: 'task',
        id: t.id,
        title: t.title,
        metricType: t.metricType,
        unit: t.unit,
        current: t.current,
        target: t.target,
        goalId: t.goalId,
        level: (goalLevels.get(t.goalId) ?? 0) + 1,
      });
      edges.push({ id: `e-${t.goalId}-${t.id}`, source: t.goalId, target: t.id });
    }
  }

  return { rootId, nodes, edges };
}
````

## File: lib/goals/metrics.ts
````typescript
import type { GoalTaskDTO, MetricType } from '../../types/goals';

export interface MetricConfig {
  label: string;
  example: string;
  steps: number[];
}

export const METRIC_CONFIG: Record<MetricType, MetricConfig> = {
  COUNT: { label: 'Count', example: '12 / 50 pushups', steps: [1, 5, 10] },
  PERCENT: { label: 'Percentage', example: '0 – 100%', steps: [5, 10, 25] },
  DURATION: { label: 'Duration', example: '1h 30m of 5h', steps: [5, 15, 30] },
  YESNO: { label: 'Yes / No', example: 'Done or not done', steps: [1] },
  CUSTOM: { label: 'Custom unit', example: '40 / 300 pages', steps: [1, 5, 10] },
};

export const round2 = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;

// Mirrors the server rule so optimistic updates match what gets saved
export function clampValue(metricType: MetricType, value: number): number {
  const v = Math.max(0, value);
  switch (metricType) {
    case 'YESNO': return v >= 0.5 ? 1 : 0;
    case 'PERCENT': return round2(Math.min(100, v));
    case 'COUNT':
    case 'DURATION': return Math.round(v);
    case 'CUSTOM': return round2(v);
  }
}

export function formatMinutes(total: number): string {
  const m = Math.max(0, Math.round(total));
  const h = Math.floor(m / 60);
  const rest = m % 60;
  if (h === 0) return `${rest}m`;
  return rest === 0 ? `${h}h` : `${h}h ${rest}m`;
}

const trimNumber = (n: number) => String(round2(n));

export function formatValue(task: Pick<GoalTaskDTO, 'metricType' | 'unit'>, value: number): string {
  switch (task.metricType) {
    case 'PERCENT': return `${trimNumber(value)}%`;
    case 'DURATION': return formatMinutes(value);
    case 'YESNO': return value >= 1 ? 'Done' : 'Not done';
    case 'CUSTOM': return `${trimNumber(value)} ${task.unit ?? ''}`.trim();
    default: return trimNumber(value);
  }
}

export function formatTarget(task: Pick<GoalTaskDTO, 'metricType' | 'unit' | 'target'>): string {
  switch (task.metricType) {
    case 'PERCENT': return '100%';
    case 'DURATION': return formatMinutes(task.target);
    case 'YESNO': return '';
    case 'CUSTOM': return `${trimNumber(task.target)} ${task.unit ?? ''}`.trim();
    default: return trimNumber(task.target);
  }
}

export function stepLabel(metricType: MetricType, step: number, sign: '+' | '−' = '+'): string {
  return `${sign}${step}${metricType === 'DURATION' ? 'm' : metricType === 'PERCENT' ? '%' : ''}`;
}

export const progressRatio = (current: number, target: number) =>
  target <= 0 ? 0 : Math.min(1, Math.max(0, current / target));

export const isComplete = (current: number, target: number) => current >= target;
````

## File: lib/goals/streak.ts
````typescript
import type { TaskLogDTO } from '../../types/goals';

// All day math uses the browser's local time so "today" matches what the user sees

export function dayKey(d: Date): string {
  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;
}

function addDays(d: Date, days: number): Date {
  const copy = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate());
  copy.setDate(copy.getDate() + days);
  return copy;
}

export function dayTotals(logs: TaskLogDTO[]): Map<string, number> {
  const totals = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const log of logs) {
    const key = dayKey(new Date(log.createdAt));
    totals.set(key, (totals.get(key) ?? 0) + log.delta);
  }
  return totals;
}

export function todayTotal(logs: TaskLogDTO[], now: Date = new Date()): number {
  return Math.round((dayTotals(logs).get(dayKey(now)) ?? 0) * 100) / 100;
}

// Consecutive local days with net-positive progress. A day that has not been logged yet today does not break the streak.
export function currentStreak(logs: TaskLogDTO[], now: Date = new Date()): number {
  const totals = dayTotals(logs);
  let cursor = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
  if ((totals.get(dayKey(cursor)) ?? 0) <= 0) cursor = addDays(cursor, -1);

  let streak = 0;
  while ((totals.get(dayKey(cursor)) ?? 0) > 0) {
    streak++;
    cursor = addDays(cursor, -1);
  }
  return streak;
}

export function dailySeries(logs: TaskLogDTO[], days = 14, now: Date = new Date()): { key: string; value: number }[] {
  const totals = dayTotals(logs);
  const series: { key: string; value: number }[] = [];
  for (let i = days - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const key = dayKey(addDays(now, -i));
    series.push({ key, value: Math.max(0, totals.get(key) ?? 0) });
  }
  return series;
}
````

## File: lib/goals/time.ts
````typescript
import type { WorkTime } from '../../types/goals';

export const EMPTY_TIME: WorkTime = { deepMinutes: 0, shallowMinutes: 0 };

// "10.5h", "4h", "0h". Decimal hours, at most 2 decimals.
export function formatHours(minutes: number): string {
  const h = Math.round((Math.max(0, minutes) / 60) * 100) / 100;
  return `${h}h`;
}

interface GoalNodeLite {
  id: string;
  parentId: string | null;
}

// Each goal's total is its own tasks plus all descendants. Goals with nothing logged simply contribute 0.
// `own` maps goalId -> time logged directly on that goal's tasks.
export function rollupTime(goals: GoalNodeLite[], own: Map<string, WorkTime>): Map<string, WorkTime> {
  const byId = new Map(goals.map(g => [g.id, g]));
  const totals = new Map<string, WorkTime>();
  for (const g of goals) totals.set(g.id, { ...(own.get(g.id) ?? EMPTY_TIME) });

  // Push each goal's own time up its ancestor chain; the hop cap guards against bad data forming a cycle
  for (const g of goals) {
    const mine = own.get(g.id);
    if (!mine || (mine.deepMinutes === 0 && mine.shallowMinutes === 0)) continue;
    const seen = new Set<string>([g.id]);
    let cursor = g.parentId ? byId.get(g.parentId) : undefined;
    while (cursor && !seen.has(cursor.id)) {
      seen.add(cursor.id);
      const t = totals.get(cursor.id)!;
      t.deepMinutes += mine.deepMinutes;
      t.shallowMinutes += mine.shallowMinutes;
      cursor = cursor.parentId ? byId.get(cursor.parentId) : undefined;
    }
  }
  return totals;
}
````

## File: lib/goals/treeLayout.ts
````typescript
// Pure tidy-tree layout: every parent is centred above its children, leaves never overlap

export interface LayoutNode { id: string; level: number }
export interface LayoutEdge { source: string; target: string }

export const NODE_WIDTH = 220;
export const H_GAP = 36;
export const V_GAP = 150;

export function layoutTree(rootId: string, nodes: LayoutNode[], edges: LayoutEdge[]): Map<string, { x: number; y: number }> {
  const children = new Map<string, string[]>();
  for (const e of edges) {
    const list = children.get(e.source) ?? [];
    list.push(e.target);
    children.set(e.source, list);
  }
  const levelOf = new Map(nodes.map(n => [n.id, n.level]));

  const slot = NODE_WIDTH + H_GAP;
  const widths = new Map<string, number>();
  const measure = (id: string, seen: Set<string>): number => {
    if (seen.has(id)) return slot;
    seen.add(id);
    const kids = children.get(id) ?? [];
    const w = kids.length === 0 ? slot : Math.max(slot, kids.reduce((sum, k) => sum + measure(k, seen), 0));
    widths.set(id, w);
    return w;
  };
  measure(rootId, new Set());

  const positions = new Map<string, { x: number; y: number }>();
  const place = (id: string, left: number) => {
    const w = widths.get(id) ?? slot;
    positions.set(id, { x: left + w / 2 - NODE_WIDTH / 2, y: (levelOf.get(id) ?? 0) * V_GAP });
    let cursor = left;
    for (const k of children.get(id) ?? []) {
      place(k, cursor);
      cursor += widths.get(k) ?? slot;
    }
  };
  place(rootId, 0);

  return positions;
}
````

## File: lib/goalValidation/pause.ts
````typescript
// Transactional pause helpers shared by the goal actions and the resume cron.
//
// Pause root:     status PAUSED, pausedByGoalId null, with pausedAt / pausedUntil / pauseReason.
// Cascade-paused: status PAUSED, pausedByGoalId = the root's id, pausedUntil copied from the root (display only).
// Not paused:     all pause fields null.

import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';

type Db = Prisma.TransactionClient;

export interface TreeNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  parentId: string | null;
  status: string;
  pausedByGoalId: string | null;
  pausedUntil: Date | null;
}

export async function loadTree(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<Map<string, TreeNode>> {
  const rows = await db.goal.findMany({
    where: { userId },
    select: { id: true, name: true, parentId: true, status: true, pausedByGoalId: true, pausedUntil: true },
  });
  return new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
}

export function descendantsOf(map: Map<string, TreeNode>, goalId: string): TreeNode[] {
  const children = new Map<string, TreeNode[]>();
  for (const n of map.values()) {
    if (!n.parentId) continue;
    const list = children.get(n.parentId);
    if (list) list.push(n);
    else children.set(n.parentId, [n]);
  }
  const out: TreeNode[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>([goalId]);
  const queue = [goalId];
  while (queue.length) {
    for (const child of children.get(queue.shift()!) ?? []) {
      if (seen.has(child.id)) continue;
      seen.add(child.id);
      out.push(child);
      queue.push(child.id);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

// Pause root of the nearest paused goal at or above `startId` (null when nothing above is paused)
export function pauseRootFrom(map: Map<string, TreeNode>, startId: string | null): TreeNode | null {
  let cursor = startId ? map.get(startId) : undefined;
  let hops = 0;
  while (cursor && hops < 1000) {
    if (cursor.status === 'PAUSED') {
      return cursor.pausedByGoalId ? map.get(cursor.pausedByGoalId) ?? cursor : cursor;
    }
    cursor = cursor.parentId ? map.get(cursor.parentId) : undefined;
    hops++;
  }
  return null;
}

const CLEARED_PAUSE = { pausedAt: null, pausedUntil: null, pauseReason: null, pausedByGoalId: null } as const;

export async function markCascadePaused(
  tx: Db,
  ids: string[],
  root: { id: string; pausedUntil: Date | null },
  now: Date
) {
  if (ids.length === 0) return;
  await tx.goal.updateMany({
    where: { id: { in: ids } },
    data: { status: 'PAUSED', pausedByGoalId: root.id, pausedAt: now, pausedUntil: root.pausedUntil, pauseReason: null },
  });
}

// Pauses `goal` as a root and cascades to its in-progress descendants. Returns how many were cascaded.
export async function pauseTree(
  tx: Db,
  userId: string,
  map: Map<string, TreeNode>,
  goalId: string,
  reason: string,
  resumeAt: Date,
  now: Date
): Promise<number> {
  const cascadeIds = descendantsOf(map, goalId)
    .filter((d) => d.status === 'IN_PROGRESS')
    .map((d) => d.id);

  await tx.goal.update({
    where: { id: goalId },
    data: { status: 'PAUSED', pausedByGoalId: null, pausedAt: now, pausedUntil: resumeAt, pauseReason: reason },
  });
  await markCascadePaused(tx, cascadeIds, { id: goalId, pausedUntil: resumeAt }, now);
  await tx.goalPause.create({
    data: { userId, goalId, reason, pausedAt: now, resumeAt, cascadedCount: cascadeIds.length },
  });
  return cascadeIds.length;
}

export type ResumeKind = 'AUTO' | 'MANUAL' | 'COMPLETED';

// Resumes a pause root together with the goals it cascaded. Returns null if it was not (or no longer) a pause root.
// If an ancestor is itself paused, the whole group is moved under that ancestor's pause instead of resuming.
export async function resumeTree(
  tx: Db,
  userId: string,
  rootId: string,
  kind: ResumeKind,
  now: Date
): Promise<{ resumed: boolean; cascaded: number } | null> {
  const map = await loadTree(tx, userId);
  const root = map.get(rootId);
  if (!root || root.status !== 'PAUSED' || root.pausedByGoalId) return null;

  const ancestorRoot = pauseRootFrom(map, root.parentId);
  const groupIds = [...map.values()].filter((n) => n.pausedByGoalId === rootId).map((n) => n.id);

  const data = ancestorRoot
    ? { pausedByGoalId: ancestorRoot.id, pausedUntil: ancestorRoot.pausedUntil, pauseReason: null }
    : { status: 'IN_PROGRESS', ...CLEARED_PAUSE, resumedAt: now };

  // The guarded update doubles as a claim, so concurrent resumes of the same goal cannot both win
  const claimed = await tx.goal.updateMany({
    where: { id: rootId, userId, status: 'PAUSED', pausedByGoalId: null },
    data,
  });
  if (claimed.count === 0) return null;

  if (groupIds.length) await tx.goal.updateMany({ where: { id: { in: groupIds } }, data });
  await tx.goalPause.updateMany({
    where: { goalId: rootId, resumedAt: null },
    data: { resumedAt: now, resumeKind: kind },
  });
  return { resumed: !ancestorRoot, cascaded: groupIds.length };
}

// A cascade-paused goal leaves its pause tree (detached or its parent deleted): it and the goals paused
// together with it go back to in-progress.
export async function releaseCascade(tx: Db, map: Map<string, TreeNode>, goalId: string, now: Date): Promise<number> {
  const node = map.get(goalId);
  if (!node || node.status !== 'PAUSED' || !node.pausedByGoalId) return 0;

  const ids = [
    node.id,
    ...descendantsOf(map, node.id)
      .filter((d) => d.status === 'PAUSED' && d.pausedByGoalId === node.pausedByGoalId)
      .map((d) => d.id),
  ];
  await tx.goal.updateMany({
    where: { id: { in: ids } },
    data: { status: 'IN_PROGRESS', ...CLEARED_PAUSE, resumedAt: now },
  });
  return ids.length;
}

// A pause root is being deleted: everything it cascaded goes back to in-progress
export async function releaseGroup(tx: Db, userId: string, rootId: string, now: Date) {
  await tx.goal.updateMany({
    where: { userId, pausedByGoalId: rootId },
    data: { status: 'IN_PROGRESS', ...CLEARED_PAUSE, resumedAt: now },
  });
}

export async function clearPauseFields(tx: Db, goalId: string) {
  await tx.goal.update({ where: { id: goalId }, data: CLEARED_PAUSE });
}
````

## File: lib/goalValidation/resume.ts
````typescript
import { prisma } from '../prisma';
import { notify } from '../notifications/service';
import { resumeTree } from './pause';
import { getValidationConfig } from './settings';

// Generous limits: the database sits behind a remote pooler
const TX_OPTIONS = { maxWait: 10_000, timeout: 30_000 };

interface ResumedGoal {
  userId: string;
  goalId: string;
  name: string;
  reason: string;
  pausedUntil: Date;
  cascaded: number;
}

async function notifyResumed(g: ResumedGoal) {
  const cfg = await getValidationConfig(prisma, g.userId);
  const reason = g.reason.length > 140 ? `${g.reason.slice(0, 140)}…` : g.reason;
  const more = g.cascaded > 0 ? ` (+${g.cascaded} sub-goal${g.cascaded === 1 ? '' : 's'})` : '';
  await notify({
    userId: g.userId,
    type: 'GOAL_RESUMED',
    dedupeKey: `goal-resume:${g.goalId}:${g.pausedUntil.getTime()}`,
    title: `Goal resumed: ${g.name}`,
    body: `Paused for: ${reason}${more}. ${cfg.enabled ? 'It needs validation now.' : 'It is back in progress.'}`,
    url: `/goals?goal=${g.goalId}`,
  });
}

// Resumes every pause root whose resume time has passed. Called by the cron producer and lazily
// (for one user) when goals are loaded, so the UI is right even if the cron lags.
export async function resumeDueGoals(now: Date, userId?: string): Promise<number> {
  const roots = await prisma.goal.findMany({
    where: { status: 'PAUSED', pausedByGoalId: null, pausedUntil: { lte: now }, ...(userId ? { userId } : {}) },
    select: { id: true, userId: true, name: true, pausedUntil: true, pauseReason: true },
    take: 500,
  });

  const resumed: ResumedGoal[] = [];
  for (const r of roots) {
    const result = await prisma.$transaction((tx) => resumeTree(tx, r.userId, r.id, 'AUTO', now), TX_OPTIONS);
    if (result?.resumed && r.pausedUntil) {
      resumed.push({
        userId: r.userId,
        goalId: r.id,
        name: r.name,
        reason: r.pauseReason ?? '',
        pausedUntil: r.pausedUntil,
        cascaded: result.cascaded,
      });
    }
  }

  for (const g of resumed) {
    try {
      await notifyResumed(g);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[goalValidation] resume notification failed', e);
    }
  }
  return resumed.length;
}
````

## File: lib/goalValidation/settings.ts
````typescript
import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { prisma } from '../prisma';
import { DISABLED_CONFIG, needsValidation, type ValidationConfig } from './status';

type Db = Prisma.TransactionClient | typeof prisma;

export const DEFAULT_REMINDER_TIMES = [1260]; // 21:00

// A missing row is treated as "feature off"
export async function getValidationConfig(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<ValidationConfig> {
  const row = await db.validationSettings.findUnique({
    where: { userId },
    select: { enabled: true, timezone: true, dayStartMin: true },
  });
  return row ?? DISABLED_CONFIG;
}

export interface DueGoal {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  rootId: string;
  rootName: string;
  depth: number;
}

// In-progress goals that need validation right now, with their top-level ancestor
export async function findDueGoals(db: Db, userId: string, cfg: ValidationConfig, now: Date): Promise<DueGoal[]> {
  if (!cfg.enabled) return [];
  const rows = await db.goal.findMany({
    where: { userId },
    select: {
      id: true,
      name: true,
      parentId: true,
      status: true,
      validationPeriodDays: true,
      lastValidatedAt: true,
      resumedAt: true,
    },
  });
  const byId = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));

  const due: DueGoal[] = [];
  for (const g of rows) {
    if (!needsValidation(g, cfg, now)) continue;
    let root = g;
    let depth = 0;
    while (root.parentId && byId.has(root.parentId) && depth < 1000) {
      root = byId.get(root.parentId)!;
      depth++;
    }
    due.push({ id: g.id, name: g.name, rootId: root.id, rootName: root.name, depth });
  }
  return due;
}
````

## File: lib/goalValidation/status.ts
````typescript
// Pure validity rules. A validation lasts until the Nth next "day start" boundary in the user's timezone.

import { localParts, zonedToUtc } from '../time/zoned';

export interface ValidationConfig {
  enabled: boolean;
  timezone: string;
  dayStartMin: number;
}

export const DISABLED_CONFIG: ValidationConfig = { enabled: false, timezone: 'UTC', dayStartMin: 420 };

// First day-start boundary strictly after `lastValidatedAt`, plus (periodDays - 1) local days.
// Validated Mon 22:00 with a 07:00 day start and a 1-day period -> valid until Tue 07:00.
export function validUntil(lastValidatedAt: Date, periodDays: number, dayStartMin: number, timeZone: string): Date {
  const p = localParts(lastValidatedAt, timeZone);
  const firstBoundaryDay = p.minuteOfDay < dayStartMin ? p.day : p.day + 1;
  return zonedToUtc(p.year, p.month, firstBoundaryDay + (periodDays - 1), dayStartMin, timeZone);
}

export interface ValidatableGoal {
  status: string;
  validationPeriodDays: number;
  lastValidatedAt: Date | null;
  resumedAt: Date | null;
}

export function goalValidUntil(goal: ValidatableGoal, cfg: ValidationConfig): Date | null {
  if (!goal.lastValidatedAt) return null;
  return validUntil(goal.lastValidatedAt, goal.validationPeriodDays, cfg.dayStartMin, cfg.timezone);
}

// Only in-progress goals ever need validation; paused and completed goals never do.
export function needsValidation(goal: ValidatableGoal, cfg: ValidationConfig, now: Date): boolean {
  if (!cfg.enabled || goal.status !== 'IN_PROGRESS') return false;
  if (!goal.lastValidatedAt) return true;
  if (goal.resumedAt && goal.resumedAt > goal.lastValidatedAt) return true;
  const until = goalValidUntil(goal, cfg);
  return until !== null && now >= until;
}
````

## File: lib/notifications/channels/webpush.ts
````typescript
import * as webpush from 'web-push';
import { prisma } from '../../prisma';
import type { ChannelOutcome, NotificationChannel, NotificationMessage } from '../types';

let configured = false;

function configure() {
  if (configured) return;
  const publicKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY;
  const privateKey = process.env.VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY;
  const subject = process.env.VAPID_SUBJECT;
  if (!publicKey || !privateKey || !subject) {
    throw new Error('VAPID keys are not configured (NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY, VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY, VAPID_SUBJECT)');
  }
  webpush.setVapidDetails(subject, publicKey, privateKey);
  configured = true;
}

// Only same-origin relative paths are allowed so a notification can't send users elsewhere
function safeUrl(url?: string): string {
  return url && url.startsWith('/') && !url.startsWith('//') ? url : '/';
}

export const webPushChannel: NotificationChannel = {
  id: 'webpush',

  async send(userId, message: NotificationMessage & { tag: string }): Promise<ChannelOutcome> {
    try {
      configure();
    } catch (e) {
      return { status: 'failed', error: (e as Error).message };
    }

    const subs = await prisma.pushSubscription.findMany({ where: { userId } });
    if (subs.length === 0) return { status: 'no_target' };

    const payload = JSON.stringify({
      title: message.title,
      body: message.body,
      url: safeUrl(message.url),
      tag: message.tag,
      data: message.data ?? {},
    });

    let delivered = 0;
    let lastError = '';
    const dead: string[] = [];

    await Promise.all(
      subs.map(async (sub) => {
        try {
          await webpush.sendNotification(
            { endpoint: sub.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: sub.p256dh, auth: sub.auth } },
            payload,
            { TTL: 60 * 60, urgency: 'high' }
          );
          delivered++;
        } catch (err) {
          const status = (err as { statusCode?: number }).statusCode;
          // 404/410 mean the browser revoked or expired this subscription
          if (status === 404 || status === 410) dead.push(sub.endpoint);
          else lastError = `push service responded ${status ?? 'with an error'}`;
        }
      })
    );

    if (dead.length) await prisma.pushSubscription.deleteMany({ where: { endpoint: { in: dead } } });
    if (delivered > 0) {
      await prisma.pushSubscription.updateMany({
        where: { userId, endpoint: { notIn: dead } },
        data: { lastSuccessAt: new Date() },
      });
      return { status: 'sent' };
    }
    return lastError ? { status: 'failed', error: lastError } : { status: 'no_target' };
  },
};
````

## File: lib/notifications/producers/goalValidation.ts
````typescript
import { prisma } from '../../prisma';
import { notify } from '../service';
import type { NotificationProducer } from '../types';
import { resumeDueGoals } from '../../goalValidation/resume';
import { findDueGoals } from '../../goalValidation/settings';
import { buildDigest } from '../../goalValidation/digest';
import { localDateKey, localParts, zonedToUtc } from '../../time/zoned';

const SLOT_MS = 60 * 60_000;

// Resumes paused goals whose resume time has passed and announces them
export const goalResumeProducer: NotificationProducer = {
  name: 'goal-resume',

  async run(now) {
    return { created: await resumeDueGoals(now) };
  },
};

// A reminder slot is open for an hour after its time. The dedupe key allows one digest per slot,
// so the every-minute cron cannot send it twice.
export const validationDigestProducer: NotificationProducer = {
  name: 'validation-digest',

  async run(now) {
    const users = await prisma.validationSettings.findMany({
      where: { enabled: true },
      select: { userId: true, timezone: true, dayStartMin: true, reminderTimes: true },
      take: 2000,
    });

    let created = 0;
    for (const u of users) {
      const today = localParts(now, u.timezone);
      // Today's and yesterday's slots, so a late-evening slot still counts just after midnight
      const openSlots = u.reminderTimes.flatMap((t) =>
        [0, -1].map((dayOffset) => ({ t, start: zonedToUtc(today.year, today.month, today.day + dayOffset, t, u.timezone) }))
      ).filter(({ start }) => now >= start && now.getTime() < start.getTime() + SLOT_MS);
      if (openSlots.length === 0) continue;

      const due = await findDueGoals(prisma, u.userId, { enabled: true, timezone: u.timezone, dayStartMin: u.dayStartMin }, now);
      if (due.length === 0) continue;
      const digest = buildDigest(due);

      for (const slot of openSlots) {
        const result = await notify({
          userId: u.userId,
          type: 'VALIDATION_DIGEST',
          dedupeKey: `validation-digest:${u.userId}:${localDateKey(localParts(slot.start, u.timezone))}:${slot.t}`,
          title: `${digest.count} goal${digest.count === 1 ? '' : 's'} need validation`,
          body: digest.body,
          url: '/goals',
          expiresAt: new Date(slot.start.getTime() + SLOT_MS),
        });
        if (result.status !== 'DUPLICATE') created++;
      }
    }
    return { created };
  },
};
````

## File: lib/notifications/producers/taskReminders.ts
````typescript
import { prisma } from '../../prisma';
import { notify } from '../service';
import type { NotificationProducer, NotificationType } from '../types';

export interface ReminderTask {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  title: string;
  deadline: Date;
  warningThresholdMin: number;
  notificationIntervalMin: number | null;
}

export interface PlannedReminder {
  type: NotificationType;
  dedupeKey: string;
  minutesLeft: number;
}

// Decides which reminder (if any) a task needs right now. Pure, so it is easy to test.
//  - Warning: once, as soon as now is inside [deadline - warningThreshold, deadline).
//  - Repeat: one per interval slot, only after the warning, and only the latest slot
//    (no flood after downtime). A warning issued mid-slot also covers that slot.
// `warnIssuedAt` is when the warning was first recorded (null = never).
// The deadline is part of every key, so editing a deadline re-arms the reminders.
export function planReminder(task: ReminderTask, now: Date, warnIssuedAt: Date | null): PlannedReminder | null {
  const deadlineMs = task.deadline.getTime();
  const nowMs = now.getTime();
  if (deadlineMs <= nowMs) return null;

  const windowStart = deadlineMs - task.warningThresholdMin * 60_000;
  if (nowMs < windowStart) return null;

  const minutesLeft = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((deadlineMs - nowMs) / 60_000));

  if (!warnIssuedAt) {
    return { type: 'TASK_WARNING', dedupeKey: `task:${task.id}:warn:${deadlineMs}`, minutesLeft };
  }

  const interval = task.notificationIntervalMin;
  if (interval && interval > 0) {
    const slot = Math.floor((nowMs - windowStart) / (interval * 60_000));
    const slotStart = windowStart + slot * interval * 60_000;
    if (slot >= 1 && warnIssuedAt.getTime() < slotStart) {
      return { type: 'TASK_REPEAT', dedupeKey: `task:${task.id}:repeat:${deadlineMs}:${slot}`, minutesLeft };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function formatTimeLeft(minutes: number): string {
  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes} min`;
  const h = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  const m = minutes % 60;
  if (h < 24) return m ? `${h}h ${m}m` : `${h}h`;
  const d = Math.floor(h / 24);
  return h % 24 ? `${d}d ${h % 24}h` : `${d}d`;
}

export const taskReminderProducer: NotificationProducer = {
  name: 'task-reminders',

  async run(now) {
    const tasks = await prisma.task.findMany({
      where: { status: 'ACTIVE', deadline: { gt: now } },
      select: {
        id: true,
        userId: true,
        title: true,
        deadline: true,
        warningThresholdMin: true,
        notificationIntervalMin: true,
        microStep: true,
      },
      take: 2000,
    });

    // Tasks that already have their warning recorded (any status), to decide warning vs repeat
    const warnKeys = tasks.map((t) => `task:${t.id}:warn:${t.deadline.getTime()}`);
    const warned = await prisma.notificationLog.findMany({
      where: { dedupeKey: { in: warnKeys } },
      select: { dedupeKey: true, createdAt: true },
    });
    const warnIssuedAt = new Map(warned.map((w) => [w.dedupeKey, w.createdAt]));

    let created = 0;
    for (const task of tasks) {
      const plan = planReminder(task, now, warnIssuedAt.get(`task:${task.id}:warn:${task.deadline.getTime()}`) ?? null);
      if (!plan) continue;

      const left = formatTimeLeft(plan.minutesLeft);
      const result = await notify({
        userId: task.userId,
        type: plan.type,
        dedupeKey: plan.dedupeKey,
        title: plan.type === 'TASK_WARNING' ? `Due in ${left}` : `Reminder: ${left} left`,
        body: task.microStep ? `${task.title} - next: ${task.microStep}` : task.title,
        url: '/',
        data: { taskId: task.id },
        expiresAt: task.deadline,
      });
      if (result.status !== 'DUPLICATE') created++;
    }
    return { created };
  },
};
````

## File: lib/notifications/preferences.ts
````typescript
import { prisma } from '../prisma';
import { NOTIFICATION_TYPES, type NotificationType } from './types';
import type { QuietWindow } from './quietHours';

export interface ResolvedSettings extends QuietWindow {
  enabled: boolean;
  typePrefs: Partial<Record<NotificationType, boolean>>;
}

export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: ResolvedSettings = {
  enabled: true,
  timezone: 'UTC',
  quietStart: null,
  quietEnd: null,
  typePrefs: {},
};

export async function getSettings(userId: string): Promise<ResolvedSettings> {
  const row = await prisma.notificationSettings.findUnique({ where: { userId } });
  if (!row) return DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
  const prefs = row.typePrefs && typeof row.typePrefs === 'object' && !Array.isArray(row.typePrefs) ? row.typePrefs : {};
  return {
    enabled: row.enabled,
    timezone: row.timezone,
    quietStart: row.quietStart,
    quietEnd: row.quietEnd,
    typePrefs: prefs as ResolvedSettings['typePrefs'],
  };
}

export function isTypeEnabled(settings: ResolvedSettings, type: NotificationType): boolean {
  if (!settings.enabled) return false;
  return settings.typePrefs[type] ?? NOTIFICATION_TYPES[type].defaultEnabled;
}
````

## File: lib/notifications/quietHours.ts
````typescript
// Pure helpers for quiet hours. Times are minutes from midnight in the user's IANA timezone.

export function isValidTimezone(tz: string): boolean {
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: tz });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function minuteOfDay(date: Date, timeZone: string): number {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
    timeZone,
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
    hourCycle: 'h23',
  }).formatToParts(date);
  const h = Number(parts.find((p) => p.type === 'hour')?.value ?? 0);
  const m = Number(parts.find((p) => p.type === 'minute')?.value ?? 0);
  return h * 60 + m;
}

export interface QuietWindow {
  timezone: string;
  quietStart: number | null;
  quietEnd: number | null;
}

function hasWindow(w: QuietWindow): w is QuietWindow & { quietStart: number; quietEnd: number } {
  return w.quietStart !== null && w.quietEnd !== null && w.quietStart !== w.quietEnd;
}

// Window may wrap past midnight (e.g. 22:00 -> 07:00)
export function isQuietNow(now: Date, w: QuietWindow): boolean {
  if (!hasWindow(w)) return false;
  const cur = minuteOfDay(now, w.timezone);
  return w.quietStart < w.quietEnd
    ? cur >= w.quietStart && cur < w.quietEnd
    : cur >= w.quietStart || cur < w.quietEnd;
}

// The moment quiet hours end, or null if not currently quiet
export function quietEndsAt(now: Date, w: QuietWindow): Date | null {
  if (!isQuietNow(now, w) || !hasWindow(w)) return null;
  const cur = minuteOfDay(now, w.timezone);
  const minutesLeft = (w.quietEnd - cur + 1440) % 1440 || 1440;
  const msIntoMinute = now.getUTCSeconds() * 1000 + now.getUTCMilliseconds();
  return new Date(now.getTime() - msIntoMinute + minutesLeft * 60_000);
}
````

## File: lib/time/zoned.ts
````typescript
// Pure helpers for working with wall-clock time in an IANA timezone.

export interface LocalParts {
  year: number;
  month: number; // 1-12
  day: number;
  minuteOfDay: number;
}

const formatters = new Map<string, Intl.DateTimeFormat>();

function formatter(timeZone: string): Intl.DateTimeFormat {
  let f = formatters.get(timeZone);
  if (!f) {
    f = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
      timeZone,
      year: 'numeric',
      month: '2-digit',
      day: '2-digit',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      second: '2-digit',
      hourCycle: 'h23',
    });
    formatters.set(timeZone, f);
  }
  return f;
}

function readParts(date: Date, timeZone: string) {
  const parts = formatter(timeZone).formatToParts(date);
  const get = (type: string) => Number(parts.find((p) => p.type === type)?.value ?? 0);
  return { year: get('year'), month: get('month'), day: get('day'), hour: get('hour'), minute: get('minute'), second: get('second') };
}

export function localParts(date: Date, timeZone: string): LocalParts {
  const p = readParts(date, timeZone);
  return { year: p.year, month: p.month, day: p.day, minuteOfDay: p.hour * 60 + p.minute };
}

// Offset (ms) of the timezone from UTC at the given instant
function offsetMs(date: Date, timeZone: string): number {
  const p = readParts(date, timeZone);
  const asUtc = Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day, p.hour, p.minute, p.second);
  return asUtc - Math.floor(date.getTime() / 1000) * 1000;
}

// The UTC instant at which the wall clock in `timeZone` reads y-m-d at `minuteOfDay`.
// Day overflow (e.g. day 32) is normalised by Date.UTC. Two passes settle the offset around DST changes.
export function zonedToUtc(year: number, month: number, day: number, minuteOfDay: number, timeZone: string): Date {
  const guess = Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day, 0, minuteOfDay);
  let result = guess - offsetMs(new Date(guess), timeZone);
  result = guess - offsetMs(new Date(result), timeZone);
  return new Date(result);
}

export function localDateKey(parts: LocalParts): string {
  return `${parts.year}-${String(parts.month).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(parts.day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}
````

## File: lib/validation.ts
````typescript
// Shared input limits and validators. Safe to import from client and server code.

export const FIELD_LIMITS = {
  usernameMin: 3,
  usernameMax: 30,
  passwordMin: 10,
  passwordMax: 64,
  passwordMaxBytes: 72, // bcrypt ignores everything past 72 bytes
  title: 120,
  microStep: 200,
  longText: 6000, // roughly 1000 words
  score: 20,
  completedAt: 40,
  dateString: 20,
  metricName: 60,
  maxValue: 1_000_000_000,
  maxMinutes: 10_080, // one week
} as const;

export const TASK_RAILS = ['urgent', 'exploration'] as const;
export const LIGHT_COLORS = ['white', 'red', 'amber', 'yellow'] as const;

const USERNAME_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9_.-]+$/;

export function normalizeUsername(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim().toLowerCase() : '';
}

export function validateUsername(value: unknown): { ok: true; username: string } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  const username = normalizeUsername(value);
  const { usernameMin: min, usernameMax: max } = FIELD_LIMITS;
  if (username.length < min || username.length > max) {
    return { ok: false, error: `Username must be ${min}-${max} characters.` };
  }
  if (!USERNAME_PATTERN.test(username)) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Username may only contain letters, numbers, underscore, dot and hyphen.' };
  }
  return { ok: true, username };
}

export const PASSWORD_RULES = [
  { id: 'length', label: `${FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMin}-${FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMax} characters`, test: (p: string) => p.length >= FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMin && p.length <= FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMax },
  { id: 'lower', label: 'A lowercase letter', test: (p: string) => /[a-z]/.test(p) },
  { id: 'upper', label: 'An uppercase letter', test: (p: string) => /[A-Z]/.test(p) },
  { id: 'digit', label: 'A number', test: (p: string) => /[0-9]/.test(p) },
  { id: 'symbol', label: 'A symbol', test: (p: string) => /[^A-Za-z0-9\s]/.test(p) },
] as const;

// Used on signup only; sign-in just caps the length so older accounts keep working
export function validateNewPassword(value: unknown, username?: string): { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return { ok: false, error: 'Password is required.' };
  if (/\s/.test(value)) return { ok: false, error: 'Password must not contain spaces.' };
  if (new TextEncoder().encode(value).length > FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMaxBytes) {
    return { ok: false, error: `Password is too long (max ${FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMax} characters).` };
  }
  const failed = PASSWORD_RULES.find(r => !r.test(value));
  if (failed) return { ok: false, error: `Password needs: ${failed.label.toLowerCase()}.` };
  if (username && value.toLowerCase().includes(username)) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Password must not contain your username.' };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

export function isPasswordWithinLimit(value: unknown): value is string {
  return (
    typeof value === 'string' &&
    value.length > 0 &&
    value.length <= FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMax &&
    new TextEncoder().encode(value).length <= FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMaxBytes
  );
}

// Trims and bounds a string. Throws so server actions reject tampered input instead of storing it.
export function requireText(value: unknown, max: number, label: string): string {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') throw new Error(`${label} is required`);
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (trimmed.length === 0) throw new Error(`${label} is required`);
  if (trimmed.length > max) throw new Error(`${label} is too long (max ${max} characters)`);
  return trimmed;
}

export function optionalText(value: unknown, max: number, label: string): string | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
  if (typeof value !== 'string') throw new Error(`${label} is invalid`);
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (trimmed.length > max) throw new Error(`${label} is too long (max ${max} characters)`);
  return trimmed;
}

export function requireInt(value: unknown, min: number, max: number, label: string): number {
  const n = typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() !== '' ? Number(value) : value;
  if (typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(n) || n < min || n > max) {
    throw new Error(`${label} must be a whole number between ${min} and ${max}`);
  }
  return n;
}

export function requireOneOf<T extends string>(value: unknown, allowed: readonly T[], label: string): T {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !(allowed as readonly string[]).includes(value)) {
    throw new Error(`${label} is invalid`);
  }
  return value as T;
}

export function requireDate(value: unknown, label: string): Date {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' && !(value instanceof Date)) throw new Error(`${label} is invalid`);
  const d = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) throw new Error(`${label} is invalid`);
  return d;
}
````

## File: public/sw.js
````javascript
// Service worker: receives push messages and shows them even when no tab is open.
self.addEventListener('install', () => self.skipWaiting());
self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => event.waitUntil(self.clients.claim()));

self.addEventListener('push', (event) => {
  let payload = {};
  try {
    payload = event.data ? event.data.json() : {};
  } catch {
    payload = { title: 'FY', body: event.data ? event.data.text() : '' };
  }

  const title = payload.title || 'FY';
  event.waitUntil(
    self.registration.showNotification(title, {
      body: payload.body || '',
      icon: '/icon-192.png',
      badge: '/badge.png',
      tag: payload.tag,
      vibrate: [100, 50, 100],
      data: { url: payload.url || '/' },
    })
  );
});

self.addEventListener('notificationclick', (event) => {
  event.notification.close();

  // Only ever open same-origin URLs
  let target = new URL('/', self.location.origin);
  try {
    const candidate = new URL(event.notification.data?.url || '/', self.location.origin);
    if (candidate.origin === self.location.origin) target = candidate;
  } catch {}

  event.waitUntil(
    self.clients.matchAll({ type: 'window', includeUncontrolled: true }).then((windows) => {
      for (const client of windows) {
        if (new URL(client.url).origin === self.location.origin && 'focus' in client) {
          return client.focus().then((c) => (c && 'navigate' in c ? c.navigate(target.href) : c));
        }
      }
      return self.clients.openWindow(target.href);
    })
  );
});
````

## File: scripts/generate-icons.mjs
````javascript
// Generates the PWA / notification PNG icons into public/. Run: node scripts/generate-icons.mjs
import { deflateSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';

const crcTable = Array.from({ length: 256 }, (_, n) => {
  let c = n;
  for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
  return c >>> 0;
});

function crc32(buf) {
  let c = 0xffffffff;
  for (const b of buf) c = crcTable[(c ^ b) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8);
  return (c ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}

function chunk(type, data) {
  const len = Buffer.alloc(4);
  len.writeUInt32BE(data.length);
  const body = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(type), data]);
  const crc = Buffer.alloc(4);
  crc.writeUInt32BE(crc32(body));
  return Buffer.concat([len, body, crc]);
}

// RGBA PNG: `bg` fills the square (null = transparent), a white dot is drawn in the centre.
function png(size, dotRatio, bg) {
  const raw = Buffer.alloc((size * 4 + 1) * size);
  const c = size / 2;
  const r = size * dotRatio;
  for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
    const row = y * (size * 4 + 1);
    raw[row] = 0;
    for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
      const d = Math.hypot(x + 0.5 - c, y + 0.5 - c);
      const o = row + 1 + x * 4;
      const a = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, r - d + 0.5));
      const [br, bgc, bb, ba] = bg ?? [0, 0, 0, 0];
      raw[o] = Math.round(255 * a + br * (1 - a));
      raw[o + 1] = Math.round(255 * a + bgc * (1 - a));
      raw[o + 2] = Math.round(255 * a + bb * (1 - a));
      raw[o + 3] = Math.round(255 * a + ba * (1 - a));
    }
  }
  const ihdr = Buffer.alloc(13);
  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(size, 0);
  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(size, 4);
  ihdr[8] = 8;
  ihdr[9] = 6;
  return Buffer.concat([
    Buffer.from([137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]),
    chunk('IHDR', ihdr),
    chunk('IDAT', deflateSync(raw)),
    chunk('IEND', Buffer.alloc(0)),
  ]);
}

mkdirSync('public', { recursive: true });
writeFileSync('public/icon-192.png', png(192, 0.2, [0, 0, 0, 255]));
writeFileSync('public/icon-512.png', png(512, 0.2, [0, 0, 0, 255]));
// Android status-bar badge: monochrome on transparent
writeFileSync('public/badge.png', png(96, 0.32, null));
console.log('icons written');
````

## File: scripts/migrate-paused-goals.mjs
````javascript
// One-off: pausing now needs a reason and a resume date, so goals paused before that feature go back to in-progress.
// Run once after `npx prisma db push`:  node scripts/migrate-paused-goals.mjs
// Safe to re-run: goals paused with the new flow always have a resume date and are left alone.
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';

const prisma = new PrismaClient();
try {
  const result = await prisma.goal.updateMany({
    where: { status: 'PAUSED', pausedUntil: null },
    data: { status: 'IN_PROGRESS' },
  });
  console.log(`Moved ${result.count} paused goal(s) back to IN_PROGRESS.`);
} finally {
  await prisma.$disconnect();
}
````

## File: scripts/setup-notification-env.mjs
````javascript
// Adds VAPID + cron secrets to .env if missing. Run: node scripts/setup-notification-env.mjs
// Prints only variable names, never the secret values.
import { readFileSync, appendFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';

const webpush = createRequire(import.meta.url)('web-push');

const file = '.env';
const current = existsSync(file) ? readFileSync(file, 'utf8') : '';
const has = (name) => new RegExp(`^${name}=`, 'm').test(current);

let add = '';
if (!has('NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY') && !has('VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY')) {
  const keys = webpush.generateVAPIDKeys();
  add += `\nNEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY="${keys.publicKey}"\nVAPID_PRIVATE_KEY="${keys.privateKey}"\n`;
}
if (!has('VAPID_SUBJECT')) add += `VAPID_SUBJECT="mailto:admin@example.com"\n`;
if (!has('CRON_SECRET')) add += `CRON_SECRET="${randomBytes(32).toString('hex')}"\n`;

if (add) {
  appendFileSync(file, add);
  console.log('added:', [...add.matchAll(/^([A-Z_]+)=/gm)].map((m) => m[1]).join(', '));
} else {
  console.log('nothing to add');
}
````

// Shared goal-validation / pause constants and DTOs (client + server safe)

export const VALIDATION_MIN_WORDS = 100;
export const PAUSE_MIN_WORDS = 20;
export const LONG_TEXT_MAX = 6000;
export const MAX_PAUSE_DAYS = 30;
export const MIN_PAUSE_LEAD_MIN = 5;
export const MAX_REMINDER_TIMES = 4;
export const PERIOD_MIN = 1;
export const PERIOD_MAX = 30;
export const PERIOD_OPTIONS = [1, 2, 3, 7, 14, 30] as const;

// Window event dispatched whenever goal data changes, so the header badge can refresh
export const VALIDATION_CHANGED_EVENT = 'validation:changed';
// Dispatched after the validation settings are saved, so open goal views re-read what they show
export const VALIDATION_SETTINGS_EVENT = 'validation:settings';

export function countWords(s: string): number {
  const t = s.trim();
  return t === '' ? 0 : t.split(/\s+/u).length;
}

export interface ValidationState {
  enabled: boolean;
  timezone: string;
  dayStartMin: number;
  reminderTimes: number[];
  pushDeviceCount: number;
}

export interface ValidationSettingsInput {
  enabled: boolean;
  timezone: string;
  dayStartMin: number;
  reminderTimes: number[];
}

export interface ValidationOverview {
  enabled: boolean;
  dueCount: number;
  due: { id: string; name: string; rootName: string }[];
}

export interface GoalValidationDTO {
  id: string;
  direct: boolean;
  viaGoalName: string | null;
  text: string;
  wordCount: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface GoalPauseDTO {
  id: string;
  reason: string;
  pausedAt: string;
  resumeAt: string;
  resumedAt: string | null;
  resumeKind: string | null;
  cascadedCount: number;
}

## File: audio.ts
````typescript
// audio.ts

// We declare the context outside, but initialize it ONLY in the browser
let ctx: AudioContext | null = null;

export const playSound = (type: 'alert' | 'focus' | 'success') => {
  // 1. SSR Check: Don't run on the Next.js server
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;

  // 2. Initialize AudioContext lazily on the first interaction
  if (!ctx) {
    const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
    if (!AudioCtx) return; // Failsafe if browser doesn't support Web Audio
    ctx = new AudioCtx();
  }

  // 3. Browser Policy Check: Resume context if the browser suspended it
  if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
    ctx.resume();
  }

  const t = ctx.currentTime;

  if (type === 'alert') {
    // Discord / Slack hybrid: Warm two-tone melodic ping (D5 -> G5)
    [587.33, 783.99].forEach((freq, i) => {
      if (!ctx) return;
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'triangle'; // Warmer harmonic profile
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t + i * 0.08);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, t + i * 0.08);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + i * 0.08 + 0.25);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(t + i * 0.08);
      osc.stop(t + i * 0.08 + 0.26);
    });
  } 
  else if (type === 'focus') {
    /**
     * NEW FOCUS AUDIO SPECIFICATION:
     * - "Warm Water Droplet meets Singing Bowl"
     * - Uses a dual-frequency FM synthesis model to mimic a perfectly hollow, 
     *   deep bamboo resonance with a warm, crystalline fluid transient.
     * - Highly satisfying, grounding, and completely free of jarring plastic/haptic clicks.
     */
    const primaryOsc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const modulatorOsc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const modulationGain = ctx.createGain();
    const mainGain = ctx.createGain();
    
    // Warm, calming baseline fundamental (F#3 - deep & grounding)
    primaryOsc.type = 'sine';
    primaryOsc.frequency.setValueAtTime(185.00, t); 
    // Pitch envelope: subtle slide down for organic fluid quality
    primaryOsc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(174.61, t + 0.15); // Slide down to F3
    
    // Modulator creates the smooth wooden/liquid strike timbre (A#4)
    modulatorOsc.type = 'sine';
    modulatorOsc.frequency.setValueAtTime(466.16, t);
    
    // Modulation depth peaks instantly then drops to zero for clean body
    modulationGain.gain.setValueAtTime(200, t);
    modulationGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, t + 0.04);
    
    // Soft, organic ADSR envelope to protect working focus
    mainGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.45, t);
    mainGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.25); // Gentle, warm decay
    
    // Connections
    modulatorOsc.connect(modulationGain);
    modulationGain.connect(primaryOsc.frequency);
    primaryOsc.connect(mainGain);
    mainGain.connect(ctx.destination);
    
    // Execution
    modulatorOsc.start(t);
    primaryOsc.start(t);
    modulatorOsc.stop(t + 0.25);
    primaryOsc.stop(t + 0.25);
  } 
  else if (type === 'success') {
    // Apple Pay Two-Tone Chime: F#5 (739.99 Hz) -> B5 (987.77 Hz)
    [739.99, 987.77].forEach((freq, i) => {
      if (!ctx) return;
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'sine';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t + i * 0.12);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.35, t + i * 0.12);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + i * 0.12 + 0.45);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(t + i * 0.12);
      osc.stop(t + i * 0.12 + 0.5);
    });
  }
};
````
