## File: app/login/page.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useState, useTransition } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { signInAction, signUpAction } from '../actions';
import { FIELD_LIMITS, PASSWORD_RULES } from '../../lib/validation';

type Mode = 'signin' | 'signup';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('signin');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const router = useRouter();
  const isSignUp = mode === 'signup';

  const switchMode = (next: Mode) => {
    setMode(next);
    setError('');
    setPassword('');
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    const formData = new FormData(e.currentTarget);

    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await (isSignUp ? signUpAction(formData) : signInAction(formData));
        if (res?.error) {
          setError(res.error);
        } else {
          // Success! Redirect to the main app
          router.push('/');
        }
      } catch {
        setError('Something went wrong. Please try again.');
      }
    });
  };

  const inputCls = 'w-full bg-[#0e0e0e] text-white placeholder:text-[#8e9192]/60 text-sm px-4 py-3 rounded-lg focus:bg-[#201f1f] border border-[#2a2a2a] focus:border-white outline-none transition-colors';
  const labelCls = 'text-xs text-[#c4c7c8] uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold font-mono';

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#0e0e0e] text-[#e5e2e1] font-sans antialiased min-h-screen selection:bg-white selection:text-black">
      <header className="fixed top-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 bg-[#0e0e0e]/90 backdrop-blur-xl border-b border-[#2a2a2a]/60">
        <div className="h-16 w-full px-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white"></span>
              <span className="font-headline text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white">GOF</span>
            </div>
            <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-2 pl-2 border-l border-[#444748]/40">
              <span className="text-xs text-[#c4c7c8] uppercase tracking-wider font-mono">SECURE ZONE</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="w-full pt-16 min-h-[calc(100vh-4rem)] flex flex-col relative overflow-hidden">
        {/* Ambient subtle background depth light */}
        <div className="absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-[540px] h-[540px] bg-white/5 rounded-full blur-[120px] pointer-events-none"></div>

        <div className="relative w-full max-w-[440px] mx-auto z-10 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center px-6">
          <div className="bg-[#1c1b1b] rounded-xl p-8 sm:p-10 shadow-2xl relative border border-[#2a2a2a]">
            
            {/* Signature GOF 5-Bar Light Indicator */}
            <div aria-label="System status 4 of 5 bars active" className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-8 w-full">
              <div className="h-1 flex-1 rounded-full bg-white"></div>
              <div className="h-1 flex-1 rounded-full bg-white"></div>
              <div className="h-1 flex-1 rounded-full bg-white"></div>
              <div className="h-1 flex-1 rounded-full bg-white"></div>
              <div className="h-1 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#353534]"></div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 mb-8">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white"></span>
                <span className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white font-headline">GOF Auth</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-[#c4c7c8]">{isSignUp ? 'Create your execution instrument account' : 'Sign in to your execution instrument'}</p>
            </div>

            <div role="tablist" className="grid grid-cols-2 p-1 mb-6 bg-[#0e0e0e] border border-[#2a2a2a] rounded-xl gap-1">
              {([['signin', 'Sign In'], ['signup', 'Sign Up']] as const).map(([m, label]) => (
                <button
                  key={m}
                  type="button"
                  role="tab"
                  aria-selected={mode === m}
                  onClick={() => switchMode(m)}
                  className={`py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${mode === m ? 'bg-white text-black' : 'text-[#c4c7c8] hover:text-white'}`}
                >
                  {label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-950/50 border border-red-900 rounded-lg text-red-400 text-xs font-mono">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="username">Username</label>
                <input
                  id="username"
                  name="username"
                  type="text"
                  required
                  minLength={isSignUp ? FIELD_LIMITS.usernameMin : undefined}
                  maxLength={FIELD_LIMITS.usernameMax}
                  pattern={isSignUp ? '[A-Za-z0-9_.\\-]+' : undefined}
                  title={isSignUp ? 'Letters, numbers, underscore, dot and hyphen only' : undefined}
                  autoComplete="username"
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  spellCheck={false}
                  placeholder={isSignUp ? `${FIELD_LIMITS.usernameMin}-${FIELD_LIMITS.usernameMax} letters, numbers, _ . -` : 'Enter your username'}
                  className={inputCls}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="password">Password</label>
                <input
                  id="password"
                  name="password"
                  type="password"
                  required
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  maxLength={FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMax}
                  autoComplete={isSignUp ? 'new-password' : 'current-password'}
                  placeholder={isSignUp ? 'Create a strong password' : 'Enter your password'}
                  className={inputCls}
                />
              </div>

              {isSignUp && (
                <>
                  <ul className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1 text-[11px] font-mono" aria-label="Password requirements">
                    {PASSWORD_RULES.map(rule => (
                      <li key={rule.id} className={rule.test(password) ? 'text-white' : 'text-neutral-500'}>
                        {rule.test(password) ? '✓' : '○'} {rule.label}
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>

                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                    <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="confirmPassword">Confirm password</label>
                    <input
                      id="confirmPassword"
                      name="confirmPassword"
                      type="password"
                      required
                      maxLength={FIELD_LIMITS.passwordMax}
                      autoComplete="new-password"
                      placeholder="Re-enter your password"
                      className={inputCls}
                    />
                  </div>
                </>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isPending}
                className="w-full mt-4 py-3.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-white text-black text-sm font-bold hover:bg-neutral-200 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <span>{isPending ? 'Verifying...' : isSignUp ? 'Create Account' : 'Sign In'}</span>
              </button>

              <p className="text-center text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-2 font-mono">
                {isSignUp ? 'Already have an account? ' : 'New here? '}
                <button type="button" onClick={() => switchMode(isSignUp ? 'signin' : 'signup')} className="text-white underline underline-offset-2">
                  {isSignUp ? 'Sign in' : 'Create an account'}
                </button>
              </p>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: app/progress/page.tsx
````typescript
// app/progress/page.tsx
"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Plus, Calendar, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import AppHeader from '../../components/AppHeader';
import TimelineNode from '../../components/TimelineNode';
import { 
  getMilestones, 
  createMilestoneAction, 
  createMetricAction, 
  updateMetricValAction, 
  deleteMetricAction,
  deleteMilestoneAction
} from '../actions';

type Metric = { id: string; name: string; val: number };
type TimelineEntry = { id: string; dateString: string; metrics: Metric[] };

export default function ProgressPage() {
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState('');
  const [currentDate, setCurrentDate] = useState('');
  
  const [timelineData, setTimelineData] = useState<TimelineEntry[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  
  // States
  const [activeDateId, setActiveDateId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isPopoverOpen, setIsPopoverOpen] = useState(false);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  // 1. Fetch real data from Database
  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadData() {
      try {
        const milestones = await getMilestones();
        setTimelineData(milestones);
        if (milestones.length > 0 && !activeDateId) {
          setActiveDateId(milestones[0].id); // Auto-open the newest one
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch progress data", error);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }
    loadData();
  }, []);

  // 2. Realtime Clock
  useEffect(() => {
    const updateTime = () => {
      const now = new Date();
      setCurrentTime(now.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: true }));
      setCurrentDate(now.toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }));
    };
    updateTime();
    const interval = setInterval(updateTime, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  // --- Database Interactions ---

  const handleAddNewDate = async (dateString: string) => {
    try {
      const newMilestone = await createMilestoneAction(dateString);
      setTimelineData(prev => [newMilestone, ...prev]); // Add to top
      setActiveDateId(newMilestone.id);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to create milestone", e);
    }
    setIsPopoverOpen(false);
  };

  const handleAddMetric = async (milestoneId: string, name: string, val: number) => {
    try {
      await createMetricAction(milestoneId, name, val);
      // Refresh the specific milestone data (simulated for immediate UI response)
      setTimelineData(prev => prev.map(entry => {
        if (entry.id === milestoneId) {
          return { ...entry, metrics: [...entry.metrics, { id: 'temp_' + Date.now(), name, val }] };
        }
        return entry;
      }));
      // Silently fetch real IDs in background
      const fresh = await getMilestones();
      setTimelineData(fresh);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to add metric", e);
    }
  };

  const handleUpdateMetricVal = async (metricId: string, newVal: number) => {
    // 1. OPTIMISTIC UPDATE: Instantly change the UI state without waiting for the server
    setTimelineData(prev => prev.map(entry => ({
      ...entry,
      metrics: entry.metrics.map(m => m.id === metricId ? { ...m, val: newVal } : m)
    })));

    // 2. BACKGROUND SYNC: Quietly fire the server action to update the database
    try {
      await updateMetricValAction(metricId, newVal);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to sync metric update with database", e);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteMetric = async (metricId: string) => {
    try {
      await deleteMetricAction(metricId);
      setTimelineData(prev => prev.map(entry => ({
        ...entry,
        metrics: entry.metrics.filter(m => m.id !== metricId)
      })));
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to delete metric", e);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteMilestone = async (milestoneId: string) => {
    // 1. Optimistically remove it from the UI
    setTimelineData(prev => prev.filter(entry => entry.id !== milestoneId));
    setActiveDateId(null); 

    // 2. Sync with database
    try {
      await deleteMilestoneAction(milestoneId);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to delete milestone", e);
    }
  };

  const formattedToday = `${new Date().getMonth() + 1}/${new Date().getDate()}/${String(new Date().getFullYear()).slice(-2)}`;

  // Filter based on search query
  const filteredTimeline = timelineData.filter(entry => 
    entry.dateString.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
    entry.metrics.some(m => m.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()))
  );

  return (
    <div className="bg-black text-white font-sans antialiased min-h-screen selection:bg-white selection:text-black">
      <AppHeader currentDate={currentDate} currentTime={currentTime} />

      <main className="w-full pt-6 md:pt-8 pb-16 min-h-[calc(100vh-4rem)]">
        <div className="w-full max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6">
          <div className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-[#0a0a0a] p-6 md:p-10 relative border border-[#222222] shadow-2xl">
            
            {/* Header & Search - MOBILE RESPONSIVE */}
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-[#222222]">
              <h1 className="font-headline text-2xl text-white tracking-tight font-semibold">Progress Engine</h1>
              
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-3 w-full md:w-auto">
                {/* Search Bar - Full width on mobile */}
                <div className="relative flex-1 sm:w-64 w-full">
                  <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-neutral-500" />
                  <input 
                    type="text"
                    value={searchQuery}
                    onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Search milestones..."
                    className="w-full bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] focus:border-white rounded-lg pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 sm:py-1.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none transition-all font-mono"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Add Date Button - Full width on mobile */}
                <div className="relative shrink-0 w-full sm:w-auto">
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setIsPopoverOpen(!isPopoverOpen)}
                    className="w-full sm:w-auto group flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2.5 sm:py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-800 text-white font-mono text-xs transition-all border border-neutral-800"
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400 group-hover:text-white transition-colors" />
                    <span className="font-semibold">Add Date</span>
                  </button>

                  {isPopoverOpen && (
                    <div className="absolute right-0 sm:right-0 left-0 sm:left-auto mt-2 w-full sm:w-56 rounded-xl bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] p-1.5 shadow-xl z-30 backdrop-blur-sm">
                      <div className="px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] text-neutral-500 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">New Milestone</div>
                      <button onClick={() => handleAddNewDate(formattedToday)} className="w-full text-left px-2.5 py-3 sm:py-2 rounded-lg text-xs text-neutral-300 hover:bg-neutral-800 hover:text-white transition-colors flex items-center justify-between font-mono">
                        <span>Today ({formattedToday})</span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-500">AUTO</span>
                      </button>
                      <button onClick={() => {
                        const input = window.prompt("Enter milestone date (MM/DD/YY):", formattedToday);
                        if (input?.trim()) handleAddNewDate(input.trim());
                      }} className="w-full text-left px-2.5 py-3 sm:py-2 rounded-lg text-xs text-neutral-300 hover:bg-neutral-800 hover:text-white transition-colors flex items-center justify-between font-mono">
                        <span>Custom Date...</span>
                        <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-500" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Timeline Area */}
            <div className="relative pt-8 pb-8 min-h-[460px]">
              
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-y-10 relative z-10 pl-2 sm:pl-6 md:pl-8">
                {isLoading ? (
                  <div className="text-xs font-mono text-neutral-500">Syncing telemetry...</div>
                ) : filteredTimeline.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="text-xs font-mono text-neutral-500">No milestones found. Create one.</div>
                ) : (
                  filteredTimeline.map((entry) => (
                    <TimelineNode 
                      key={entry.id}
                      id={entry.id}
                      dateString={entry.dateString}
                      metrics={entry.metrics}
                      isActive={entry.id === activeDateId}
                      onSelect={() => setActiveDateId(entry.id)}
                      onAddMetric={handleAddMetric}
                      onUpdateMetric={handleUpdateMetricVal}
                      onDeleteMetric={handleDeleteMetric}
                      onDeleteMilestone={handleDeleteMilestone}
                    />
                  ))
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/dialog/PlanEditor.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Eye, Pencil, Trash2, Check, LoaderCircle, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react';
import { updatePlan, deletePlan } from '../../../app/goals/actions';
import { useGoals } from '../../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import type { GoalPlanDTO } from '../../../types/goals';
import { LIMITS } from '../../../types/goals';
import Markdown from '../Markdown';

interface PlanEditorProps {
  plan: GoalPlanDTO;
  onBack: () => void;
  onSaved: (plan: GoalPlanDTO) => void;
  onDeleted: () => void;
}

type SaveState = 'saved' | 'dirty' | 'saving' | 'error';
const AUTOSAVE_MS = 800;

export default function PlanEditor({ plan, onBack, onSaved, onDeleted }: PlanEditorProps) {
  const { notify } = useGoals();

  const [title, setTitle] = useState(plan.title);
  const [body, setBody] = useState(plan.body);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'edit' | 'preview'>('edit');
  const [saveState, setSaveState] = useState<SaveState>('saved');
  const [confirmDelete, setConfirmDelete] = useState(false);

  const latest = useRef({ title: plan.title, body: plan.body });
  const savedSnapshot = useRef({ title: plan.title, body: plan.body });
  const timer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const chain = useRef<Promise<void>>(Promise.resolve());
  const deleted = useRef(false);

  const save = useCallback(() => {
    if (timer.current) {
      clearTimeout(timer.current);
      timer.current = null;
    }
    const snap = { ...latest.current };
    if (snap.title === savedSnapshot.current.title && snap.body === savedSnapshot.current.body) return chain.current;

    setSaveState('saving');
    chain.current = chain.current.then(async () => {
      if (deleted.current) return;
      try {
        const res = await updatePlan(plan.id, { title: snap.title.trim() ? snap.title : undefined, body: snap.body });
        if (res.ok === false) {
          setSaveState('error');
          notify(res.error);
          return;
        }
        savedSnapshot.current = { title: res.data.title, body: res.data.body };
        onSaved(res.data);
        const stillDirty = latest.current.title !== res.data.title || latest.current.body !== res.data.body;
        setSaveState(stillDirty ? 'dirty' : 'saved');
      } catch {
        setSaveState('error');
      }
    });
    return chain.current;
  }, [notify, onSaved, plan.id]);

  const schedule = () => {
    setSaveState('dirty');
    if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current);
    timer.current = setTimeout(() => { save(); }, AUTOSAVE_MS);
  };

  // Flush unsaved edits when the editor closes (back, dialog close, Esc)
  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (!deleted.current) save();
  }, [save]);

  const handleBack = async () => {
    await save();
    onBack();
  };

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    deleted.current = true;
    if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current);
    const res = await deletePlan(plan.id);
    if (res.ok === false) {
      deleted.current = false;
      notify(res.error);
      return;
    }
    notify('Plan deleted');
    onDeleted();
  };

  const status = {
    saved: { icon: Check, text: 'Saved' },
    dirty: { icon: LoaderCircle, text: 'Unsaved changes' },
    saving: { icon: LoaderCircle, text: 'Saving…' },
    error: { icon: TriangleAlert, text: 'Could not save' },
  }[saveState];
  const StatusIcon = status.icon;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <button onClick={handleBack} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-colors">
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> All plans
        </button>
        <span className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-mono ${saveState === 'error' ? 'text-white' : 'text-neutral-500'}`} aria-live="polite">
          <StatusIcon className={`w-3 h-3 ${saveState === 'saving' ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} /> {status.text}
        </span>
      </div>

      <input
        value={title}
        onChange={e => { setTitle(e.target.value); latest.current.title = e.target.value; schedule(); }}
        onBlur={() => { if (!title.trim()) { setTitle(savedSnapshot.current.title); latest.current.title = savedSnapshot.current.title; } }}
        maxLength={120}
        aria-label="Plan title"
        placeholder="Plan title"
        className="w-full bg-transparent border-b border-neutral-800 focus:border-white pb-2 font-headline text-lg font-semibold text-white outline-none transition-colors"
      />

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div role="tablist" className="inline-flex p-0.5 rounded-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800">
          {([['edit', Pencil, 'Edit'], ['preview', Eye, 'Preview']] as const).map(([m, Icon, label]) => (
            <button
              key={m}
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={mode === m}
              onClick={() => setMode(m)}
              className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-medium transition-all ${
                mode === m ? 'bg-white text-black font-semibold' : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              <Icon className="w-3 h-3" /> {label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-600">Markdown supported · {body.length} / {LIMITS.planBody}</span>
      </div>

      {mode === 'edit' ? (
        <textarea
          value={body}
          onChange={e => { const v = e.target.value.slice(0, LIMITS.planBody); setBody(v); latest.current.body = v; schedule(); }}
          maxLength={LIMITS.planBody}
          placeholder={'Write your plan…\n\n# Heading\n- bullet\n**bold**, *italic*, `code`'}
          aria-label="Plan body"
          className="w-full min-h-[260px] resize-y rounded-xl bg-[#101010] border border-[#262626] focus:border-white p-4 text-sm text-neutral-200 placeholder-neutral-700 outline-none font-mono leading-relaxed transition-colors"
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="min-h-[260px] rounded-xl bg-[#101010] border border-[#1f1f1f] p-4">
          <Markdown source={body} />
        </div>
      )}

      {confirmDelete ? (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-950 p-3">
          <span className="text-xs text-neutral-300">Delete this plan permanently?</span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(false)} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-white">Cancel</button>
            <button onClick={handleDelete} className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all">Delete</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <button onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(true)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-neutral-500 hover:text-white transition-colors">
          <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Delete plan
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/dialog/TasksSection.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { ListChecks, Plus, Link2, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { attachTask } from '../../../app/goals/actions';
import { useGoals } from '../../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import type { GoalDetail, GoalTaskDTO } from '../../../types/goals';
import { METRIC_CONFIG } from '../../../lib/goals/metrics';
import CreateTaskForm from '../CreateTaskForm';
import TaskProgressCard from '../TaskProgressCard';

interface TasksSectionProps {
  detail: GoalDetail;
}

export default function TasksSection({ detail }: TasksSectionProps) {
  const { tasks, loading, patchTask, notify, openGoal } = useGoals();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'none' | 'create' | 'attach'>('none');
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [confirmMove, setConfirmMove] = useState<GoalTaskDTO | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  // Read live tasks from GoalsProvider; fall back to detail.tasks only during initial direct-URL load
  const goalTasks = useMemo(
    () => (loading && tasks.length === 0 ? detail.tasks : tasks.filter(t => t.goalId === detail.id)),
    [loading, tasks, detail.tasks, detail.id],
  );

  const attachable = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return tasks
      .filter(t => t.goalId !== detail.id)
      .filter(t => !q || t.title.toLowerCase().includes(q))
      .sort((a, b) => Number(!!a.goalId) - Number(!!b.goalId)); // unattached first
  }, [tasks, detail.id, query]);

  const doAttach = async (task: GoalTaskDTO, move: boolean) => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await attachTask(detail.id, task.id, { move });
      if (res.ok === false) {
        if (res.needsConfirm) setConfirmMove(task);
        else notify(res.error);
        return;
      }
      patchTask(res.data);
      setConfirmMove(null);
      setMode('none');
      setQuery('');
      notify(move ? 'Task moved to this goal' : 'Task attached');
    } catch {
      notify('Could not attach the task.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="tasks-heading">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
        <h3 id="tasks-heading" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400 whitespace-nowrap">
          <ListChecks className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Tasks <span className="text-neutral-600">{goalTasks.length}</span>
        </h3>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button onClick={() => setMode(m => (m === 'attach' ? 'none' : 'attach'))} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white whitespace-nowrap hover:border-white active:scale-95 transition-all">
            <Link2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Attach existing
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => setMode(m => (m === 'create' ? 'none' : 'create'))} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all">
            <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> New task
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {mode === 'create' && (
        <div className="mb-4">
          <CreateTaskForm
            goalId={detail.id}
            onCancel={() => setMode('none')}
            onCreated={t => {
              patchTask(t);
              setMode('none');
              notify('Task created');
            }}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {mode === 'attach' && (
        <div className="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-[#262626] bg-[#101010]">
          <div className="flex items-center border-b border-[#1f1f1f]">
            <input
              autoFocus
              value={query}
              onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search tasks…"
              aria-label="Search tasks"
              className="flex-1 bg-transparent px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none font-mono"
            />
            <button onClick={() => { setMode('none'); setConfirmMove(null); }} aria-label="Close" className="w-8 h-8 mr-1 text-neutral-500 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
          </div>
          {confirmMove && (
            <div className="m-2 rounded-lg border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-950 p-3">
              <p className="text-xs text-neutral-300">
                “{confirmMove.title}” is attached to “{confirmMove.goalName}”. A task can only belong to one goal. Move it here?
              </p>
              <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end gap-2">
                <button onClick={() => setConfirmMove(null)} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-white">Cancel</button>
                <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => doAttach(confirmMove, true)} className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50">Move here</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          <ul className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto p-1">
            {attachable.length === 0 ? (
              <li className="px-3 py-4 text-center text-xs text-neutral-600">{query ? 'No matches.' : 'No other tasks yet. Create a new one instead.'}</li>
            ) : (
              attachable.map(t => (
                <li key={t.id}>
                  <button
                    disabled={busy}
                    onClick={() => (t.goalId ? setConfirmMove(t) : doAttach(t, false))}
                    className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-left hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    <span className="min-w-0">
                      <span className="block text-xs text-white truncate">{t.title}</span>
                      <span className="block text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-500">{METRIC_CONFIG[t.metricType].label}</span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-500">{t.goalId ? `on ${t.goalName}` : 'Unattached'}</span>
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))
            )}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}

      {goalTasks.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-neutral-800 py-5 text-center text-xs text-neutral-600">No tasks yet. Create one or attach an existing task.</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {goalTasks.map(t => (
            <TaskProgressCard key={t.id} task={t} showGoalChip={false} onOpenGoal={id => openGoal(id, 'push')} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/CreateTaskForm.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { createGoalTask } from '../../app/goals/actions';
import type { GoalTaskDTO, MetricType, WorkType } from '../../types/goals';
import MetricTypePicker from './MetricTypePicker';
import WorkTypeToggle from './WorkTypeToggle';

interface CreateTaskFormProps {
  goalId?: string | null;
  onCreated: (task: GoalTaskDTO) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

const inputCls = 'w-full bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] focus:border-white rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-white placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none transition-all';
const labelCls = 'block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 mb-1.5';

export default function CreateTaskForm({ goalId, onCreated, onCancel }: CreateTaskFormProps) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [metricType, setMetricType] = useState<MetricType>('COUNT');
  const [target, setTarget] = useState('');
  const [hours, setHours] = useState('');
  const [minutes, setMinutes] = useState('');
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState('');
  const [start, setStart] = useState('');
  const [workType, setWorkType] = useState<WorkType>('SHALLOW');
  const [timeHours, setTimeHours] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const durationTarget = (Number(hours) || 0) * 60 + (Number(minutes) || 0);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (saving) return;
    setError(null);

    if (!title.trim()) return setError('Give the task a name.');

    const timeValue = Number(timeHours) || 0;
    if (timeValue < 0) return setError('Time spent must be 0 or more.');

    let targetValue: number | undefined;
    if (metricType === 'DURATION') {
      if (durationTarget < 1) return setError('Set a target of at least 1 minute.');
      targetValue = durationTarget;
    } else if (metricType === 'COUNT' || metricType === 'CUSTOM') {
      targetValue = Number(target);
      if (!target || !(targetValue > 0)) return setError('Set a target greater than 0.');
      if (metricType === 'CUSTOM' && !unit.trim()) return setError('Add a unit, for example pages or km.');
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await createGoalTask({
        title,
        metricType,
        target: targetValue,
        unit: metricType === 'CUSTOM' ? unit : undefined,
        start: start ? Number(start) : 0,
        goalId: goalId ?? null,
        workType,
        timeSpentMin: Math.round(timeValue * 60),
      });
      if (res.ok === false) {
        setError(res.error);
        return;
      }
      onCreated(res.data);
    } catch {
      setError('Could not create the task. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
      <div>
        <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="gt-title">Task name</label>
        <input id="gt-title" autoFocus value={title} onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)} maxLength={120} placeholder="e.g. Pushups" className={inputCls} />
      </div>

      <div>
        <span className={labelCls}>Metric</span>
        <MetricTypePicker value={metricType} onChange={setMetricType} />
      </div>

      {metricType === 'DURATION' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div>
            <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="gt-h">Target hours</label>
            <input id="gt-h" type="number" min={0} inputMode="numeric" value={hours} onChange={e => setHours(e.target.value)} placeholder="0" className={inputCls} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="gt-m">Target minutes</label>
            <input id="gt-m" type="number" min={0} inputMode="numeric" value={minutes} onChange={e => setMinutes(e.target.value)} placeholder="0" className={inputCls} />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {(metricType === 'COUNT' || metricType === 'CUSTOM') && (
        <div className={`grid gap-3 ${metricType === 'CUSTOM' ? 'grid-cols-2' : 'grid-cols-1'}`}>
          <div>
            <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="gt-target">Target</label>
            <input id="gt-target" type="number" min={1} step={metricType === 'COUNT' ? 1 : 'any'} inputMode="decimal" value={target} onChange={e => setTarget(e.target.value)} placeholder="50" className={inputCls} />
          </div>
          {metricType === 'CUSTOM' && (
            <div>
              <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="gt-unit">Unit</label>
              <input id="gt-unit" value={unit} onChange={e => setUnit(e.target.value)} maxLength={20} placeholder="pages, km, glasses…" className={inputCls} />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {metricType !== 'YESNO' && (
        <div>
          <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="gt-start">
            Starting value <span className="normal-case text-neutral-600">(optional{metricType === 'DURATION' ? ', in minutes' : ''})</span>
          </label>
          <input id="gt-start" type="number" min={0} inputMode="decimal" value={start} onChange={e => setStart(e.target.value)} placeholder="0" className={inputCls} />
        </div>
      )}

      <div>
        <span className={labelCls}>Work type</span>
        <WorkTypeToggle value={workType} onChange={setWorkType} />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className={labelCls} htmlFor="gt-time">
          Time spent so far <span className="normal-case text-neutral-600">(optional, in hours)</span>
        </label>
        <input id="gt-time" type="number" min={0} step="0.25" inputMode="decimal" value={timeHours} onChange={e => setTimeHours(e.target.value)} placeholder="0" className={inputCls} />
      </div>

      {error && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-neutral-300 border border-neutral-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 bg-neutral-900">{error}</p>}

      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-1">
        <button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-colors">Cancel</button>
        <button type="submit" disabled={saving} className="px-5 py-2 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50">
          {saving ? 'Creating…' : 'Create task'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
````

````typescript
import React from 'react';
import { GOAL_STATUSES, type GoalStatus } from '../../types/goals';
import { STATUS_LABEL } from './StatusBadge';

interface StatusSelectProps {
  value: GoalStatus;
  onChange: (status: GoalStatus) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  lockedStatuses?: GoalStatus[]; // options that cannot be picked right now
  lockedTitle?: string;
}

export default function StatusSelect({ value, onChange, disabled, lockedStatuses = [], lockedTitle }: StatusSelectProps) {
  return (
    <div role="radiogroup" aria-label="Goal status" className="inline-flex p-0.5 rounded-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800">
      {GOAL_STATUSES.map(s => {
        const active = s === value;
        const locked = lockedStatuses.includes(s);
        return (
          <button
            key={s}
            type="button"
            role="radio"
            aria-checked={active}
            disabled={disabled || locked}
            title={locked ? lockedTitle : undefined}
            onClick={() => !active && onChange(s)}
            className={`px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] font-medium transition-all disabled:opacity-50 ${
              active ? 'bg-white text-black font-semibold' : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            {STATUS_LABEL[s]}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
````

````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Plus, Search, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { attachTask } from '../../app/goals/actions';
import { useGoals } from '../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import type { GoalSummary, GoalTaskDTO } from '../../types/goals';
import { isComplete } from '../../lib/goals/metrics';
import { currentStreak } from '../../lib/goals/streak';
import CreateTaskForm from './CreateTaskForm';
import GoalPicker from './GoalPicker';
import TaskProgressCard from './TaskProgressCard';

type Filter = 'ALL' | 'UNATTACHED' | 'ATTACHED';

export default function TasksTab() {
  const { tasks, goals, logs, loading, openGoal, patchTask, notify } = useGoals();
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Filter>('ALL');
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [attachFor, setAttachFor] = useState<GoalTaskDTO | null>(null);
  const [pendingGoal, setPendingGoal] = useState<GoalSummary | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const visible = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return tasks.filter(t =>
      (filter === 'ALL' || (filter === 'UNATTACHED' ? !t.goalId : !!t.goalId)) &&
      (!q || t.title.toLowerCase().includes(q)),
    );
  }, [tasks, filter, query]);

  const summary = useMemo(() => ({
    total: tasks.length,
    complete: tasks.filter(t => isComplete(t.current, t.target)).length,
    bestStreak: tasks.reduce((best, t) => Math.max(best, currentStreak(logs[t.id] ?? [])), 0),
  }), [tasks, logs]);

  const closeAttach = () => { setAttachFor(null); setPendingGoal(null); };

  const doAttach = async (task: GoalTaskDTO, goal: GoalSummary) => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await attachTask(goal.id, task.id, { move: true });
      if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
      patchTask(res.data);
      notify(`Attached to “${goal.name}”`);
      closeAttach();
    } catch {
      notify('Could not attach the task.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const pickGoal = (goal: GoalSummary) => {
    if (!attachFor) return;
    if (attachFor.goalId && attachFor.goalId !== goal.id) setPendingGoal(goal);
    else doAttach(attachFor, goal);
  };

  const counts: Record<Filter, number> = {
    ALL: tasks.length,
    UNATTACHED: tasks.filter(t => !t.goalId).length,
    ATTACHED: tasks.filter(t => !!t.goalId).length,
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:gap-3">
        {[
          ['Tasks', summary.total],
          ['At target', summary.complete],
          ['Best streak', `${summary.bestStreak}d`],
        ].map(([label, val]) => (
          <div key={label} className="rounded-xl border border-[#222222] bg-[#0f0f0f] px-3 py-3 sm:px-4">
            <div className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">{label}</div>
            <div className="mt-1 font-headline text-xl font-semibold text-white tabular-nums">{val}</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div className="relative flex-1 sm:max-w-xs">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-neutral-500" />
          <input
            value={query}
            onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search tasks…"
            aria-label="Search tasks"
            className="w-full bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] focus:border-white rounded-lg pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-white placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none transition-all font-mono"
          />
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => setCreating(c => !c)} className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all">
          <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" /> New task
        </button>
      </div>

      {creating && (
        <div className="mt-3">
          <CreateTaskForm
            onCancel={() => setCreating(false)}
            onCreated={t => {
              patchTask(t);
              setCreating(false);
              notify('Task created');
            }}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Filter tasks">
        {([['ALL', 'All'], ['UNATTACHED', 'Unattached'], ['ATTACHED', 'Attached']] as [Filter, string][]).map(([f, label]) => (
          <button
            key={f}
            role="radio"
            aria-checked={filter === f}
            onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-medium border transition-all ${
              filter === f ? 'bg-white text-black border-white font-semibold' : 'border-neutral-800 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:border-neutral-600'
            }`}
          >
            {label} <span className="font-mono opacity-60">{counts[f]}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 animate-pulse" aria-busy="true">
            {[0, 1].map(i => <div key={i} className="h-56 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-900" />)}
          </div>
        ) : tasks.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-neutral-800 py-14 text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-neutral-300">No tasks yet</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-600">Create a task with a metric, then attach it to a goal.</p>
          </div>
        ) : visible.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="py-10 text-center text-xs font-mono text-neutral-600">No tasks match your filters.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
            {visible.map(t => (
              <TaskProgressCard key={t.id} task={t} onOpenGoal={id => openGoal(id)} onAttach={setAttachFor} />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {attachFor && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm" onMouseDown={e => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) closeAttach(); }}>
          <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Attach task to a goal" className="w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-[#222222] bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-2xl motion-safe:animate-[goal-sheet_0.2s_ease-out]">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
              <h3 className="font-headline text-base font-semibold text-white">
                {attachFor.goalId ? 'Move' : 'Attach'} “{attachFor.title}”
              </h3>
              <button onClick={closeAttach} aria-label="Close" className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full text-neutral-500 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
            </div>
            {pendingGoal ? (
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-950 p-3">
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-300">
                  This task is on “{attachFor.goalName}”. A task can only belong to one goal. Move it to “{pendingGoal.name}”?
                </p>
                <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => setPendingGoal(null)} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-white">Back</button>
                  <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => doAttach(attachFor, pendingGoal)} className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50">Move task</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <GoalPicker
                goals={goals.filter(g => g.id !== attachFor.goalId)}
                onPick={pickGoal}
                emptyText="No goals yet. Create one in the Goals tab."
              />
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/TaskTimeRow.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Clock, Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { logTaskTime, updateGoalTask } from '../../app/goals/actions';
import { useGoals } from '../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import { formatHours } from '../../lib/goals/time';
import { LIMITS, type GoalTaskDTO, type WorkType } from '../../types/goals';
import WorkTypeToggle from './WorkTypeToggle';

const QUICK_ADD: { label: string; minutes: number }[] = [
  { label: '+15m', minutes: 15 },
  { label: '+30m', minutes: 30 },
  { label: '+1h', minutes: 60 },
  { label: '+2h', minutes: 120 },
];

// Deep/shallow type and manually logged time for a task; goal rollups recompute automatically via GoalsProvider
export default function TaskTimeRow({ task }: { task: GoalTaskDTO }) {
  const { patchTask, notify } = useGoals();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [hours, setHours] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const changeType = async (workType: WorkType) => {
    if (busy) return;
    const previous = task;
    patchTask({ ...task, workType });
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await updateGoalTask(task.id, { workType });
      if (res.ok === false) {
        patchTask(previous);
        return notify(res.error);
      }
      patchTask(res.data);
    } catch {
      patchTask(previous);
      notify('Could not change the work type.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const log = async (deltaMin: number) => {
    if (busy || deltaMin === 0) return;
    const previous = task;
    const optimisticMin = Math.min(LIMITS.maxTimeMin, Math.max(0, Math.round(task.timeSpentMin + deltaMin)));
    patchTask({ ...task, timeSpentMin: optimisticMin });
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await logTaskTime(task.id, { deltaMin });
      if (res.ok === false) {
        patchTask(previous);
        return notify(res.error);
      }
      patchTask(res.data);
      setHours('');
    } catch {
      patchTask(previous);
      notify('Could not log time. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const customMinutes = Math.round((Number(hours) || 0) * 60);

  return (
    <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-[#1a1a1a]">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <WorkTypeToggle value={task.workType} onChange={changeType} disabled={busy} />
        <button
          onClick={() => setOpen(o => !o)}
          aria-expanded={open}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-300 hover:text-white hover:border-neutral-600 transition-colors"
        >
          <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {formatHours(task.timeSpentMin)} spent
        </button>
      </div>

      {open && (
        <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-950 p-3 space-y-3">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5" aria-label="Quick add time">
            {QUICK_ADD.map(q => (
              <button
                key={q.minutes}
                onClick={() => log(q.minutes)}
                disabled={busy}
                className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-200 hover:border-white hover:text-white active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {q.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <input
              type="number"
              min={0}
              step="0.25"
              inputMode="decimal"
              value={hours}
              onChange={e => setHours(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); log(customMinutes); } }}
              placeholder="Hours, e.g. 1.5"
              aria-label="Hours to add or remove"
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] focus:border-white rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none transition-all"
            />
            <button
              onClick={() => log(-customMinutes)}
              disabled={busy || customMinutes <= 0}
              aria-label="Remove time"
              className="shrink-0 w-9 h-9 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 text-white flex items-center justify-center hover:border-white active:scale-90 transition-all disabled:opacity-30"
            >
              <Minus className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => log(customMinutes)}
              disabled={busy || customMinutes <= 0}
              aria-label="Add time"
              className="shrink-0 w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-white text-black flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-90 transition-all disabled:opacity-30"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.5]" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/FocusOverlay.tsx
````typescript
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { CheckCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { Task } from '../types';

interface FocusOverlayProps {
  task: Task;
  onDismiss: () => void;
  onComplete: (task: Task) => void;
}

export default function FocusOverlay({ task, onDismiss, onComplete }: FocusOverlayProps) {
  const [focusPhase, setFocusPhase] = useState<'2min' | 'deep'>('2min');
  const [isFocusRunning, setIsFocusRunning] = useState(true);
  
  // UI Display states
  const [displaySeconds, setDisplaySeconds] = useState(120);

  // ABSOLUTE TIME TRACKING
  const startTimeRef = useRef<number>(Date.now());
  const totalPausedTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const pauseStartRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  // Handle phase switching manually (when user clicks "Enter Deep Flow")
  const switchToDeepFlow = () => {
    setFocusPhase('deep');
    startTimeRef.current = Date.now(); // Reset the absolute clock for the new phase
    totalPausedTimeRef.current = 0;
    pauseStartRef.current = null;
    setDisplaySeconds(0);
  };

  // Handle Pause/Resume
  const togglePause = () => {
    if (isFocusRunning) {
      // We are pausing. Record exactly when we paused.
      pauseStartRef.current = Date.now();
    } else {
      // We are resuming. Add the time spent paused to our total paused tally.
      if (pauseStartRef.current) {
        totalPausedTimeRef.current += (Date.now() - pauseStartRef.current);
        pauseStartRef.current = null;
      }
    }
    setIsFocusRunning(!isFocusRunning);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    let animationFrameId: number;

    const tick = () => {
      if (isFocusRunning) {
        const now = Date.now();
        // Calculate exact elapsed time: (Now - Start) minus (Time spent paused)
        const elapsedMs = now - startTimeRef.current - totalPausedTimeRef.current;
        const elapsedSecs = Math.floor(elapsedMs / 1000);

        if (focusPhase === '2min') {
          const remaining = Math.max(0, 120 - elapsedSecs);
          setDisplaySeconds(remaining);
          
          if (remaining === 0) {
            switchToDeepFlow(); // Auto-switch when 2 minutes are up
          }
        } else {
          setDisplaySeconds(elapsedSecs);
        }
      }
      // Request next frame (runs at ~60fps while tab is active, throttles gracefully when hidden but math stays accurate!)
      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };

    animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);
  }, [isFocusRunning, focusPhase]);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-black/95 backdrop-blur-lg flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-6 md:p-12 select-none">
      <div className="w-full max-w-xl flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-neutral-400 border-b border-neutral-850 pb-3.5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white animate-pulse"></span>
          <span className="font-semibold text-white font-headline">Focus Session</span>
        </div>
        <button onClick={onDismiss} className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 hover:border-white text-neutral-300 hover:text-white text-xs font-medium">
          Dismiss
        </button>
      </div>
      
      <div className="max-w-md w-full flex flex-col items-center text-center my-auto">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-400 mb-2 font-mono">
          {focusPhase === '2min' ? '2-Minute Starter' : 'Deep Flow Session'}
        </span>
        <div className="relative w-52 h-52 flex items-center justify-center my-5">
          <svg className="w-full h-full transform -rotate-90" viewBox="0 0 100 100">
            <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="44" className="text-neutral-800" strokeWidth="2.5" stroke="currentColor" fill="transparent" />
            <circle 
              cx="50" cy="50" r="44" className="text-white" strokeWidth="3.5" strokeLinecap="round" 
              strokeDasharray={276.46} 
              strokeDashoffset={focusPhase === '2min' ? 276.46 - (displaySeconds / 120) * 276.46 : 0} 
              stroke="currentColor" fill="transparent" 
            />
          </svg>
          <div className="absolute flex flex-col items-center">
            <span className="text-4xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight tabular-nums font-headline">
              {`${Math.floor(displaySeconds / 60)}:${String(displaySeconds % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400 mt-1 font-mono">
              {focusPhase === '2min' ? 'Initial step' : 'Elapsed'}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="mt-3 p-5 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 text-center w-full">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold block mb-1 text-white font-mono">Initial step</span>
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">"{task.microStep || task.title}"</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400 mt-2">{task.title}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
        {focusPhase === '2min' ? (
          <button onClick={switchToDeepFlow} className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 hover:border-white text-white text-xs font-medium transition-all font-mono">
            Enter Deep Flow
          </button>
        ) : (
          <button onClick={togglePause} className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 hover:border-white text-white text-xs font-medium transition-all font-mono">
            {isFocusRunning ? 'Pause' : 'Resume'}
          </button>
        )}
        <button onClick={() => onComplete(task)} className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 font-semibold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all">
          <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-black" /><span>Mark Finished</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/TaskCard.tsx
````typescript
import React from 'react';
import { Check, Pencil } from 'lucide-react';
import { Task } from '../types';
import ImportanceLightsHeader from './ImportanceLightsHeader';
import { playSound } from '../audio'; // Added sound import

interface TaskCardProps {
  task: Task;
  currentTime: Date; // Passed down from page to trigger re-renders
  onStart: (task: Task) => void;
  onComplete: (task: Task, e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  onEdit: (task: Task, e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
}

export default function TaskCard({ task, currentTime, onStart, onComplete, onEdit }: TaskCardProps) {
  const deadlineDate = new Date(task.deadline);
  const timeDiffMs = deadlineDate.getTime() - currentTime.getTime();
  
  // Calculate remaining time string
  let remainingText = '';
  const hoursLeft = Math.floor(timeDiffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60));
  const minutesLeft = Math.floor((timeDiffMs % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60));
  
  if (timeDiffMs < 0) {
    remainingText = 'OVERDUE';
  } else if (hoursLeft > 24) {
    const days = Math.floor(hoursLeft / 24);
    remainingText = `${days} day${days > 1 ? 's' : ''} left`;
  } else if (hoursLeft > 0) {
    remainingText = `${hoursLeft}h ${minutesLeft}m left`;
  } else {
    remainingText = `${minutesLeft}m left`;
  }

  // BUG FIX: Use custom threshold instead of hardcoded 2 hours
  const thresholdMin = task.warningThresholdMin ?? 120;
  const thresholdMs = thresholdMin * 60 * 1000;

  // SMART LOGIC: If it's a mandatory task AND time left is below the CUSTOM threshold, FORCE CRITICAL RED STATE
  const isDanger = task.rail === 'urgent' && timeDiffMs <= thresholdMs && timeDiffMs > 0;

  // Override visuals if in danger
  const finalLevel = isDanger ? 5 : task.importanceLevel;
  // FIX: Added TS cast to prevent strict-type error
  const finalLightColor = (isDanger ? 'red' : task.lightColor) as 'white' | 'red' | 'amber' | 'yellow';
  
  const borderClasses = isDanger 
    ? 'border-red-500 shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(239,68,68,0.4)]'
    : 'border-[#262626] hover:border-neutral-400';

  // NEW: Handler to play focus sound before starting
  const handleStart = () => {
    playSound('focus');
    onStart(task);
  };

  return (
    <div
      onClick={handleStart} // Used new handler here
      tabIndex={0}
      role="button"
      className={`group relative text-left p-5 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer transition-all duration-200 select-none bg-[#141414] border ${borderClasses}`}
    >
      <ImportanceLightsHeader level={finalLevel} lightColor={finalLightColor} />
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mt-3.5">
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pr-2">
          <p className={`text-xs font-mono mb-1.5 ${isDanger ? 'text-red-400 font-bold' : 'text-neutral-400'}`}>
            Target: {deadlineDate.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })} • {remainingText}
          </p>
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white tracking-tight leading-snug">{task.title}</h3>
          {task.microStep && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed font-sans">{task.microStep}</p>}
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 pt-0.5" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
          <button onClick={(e) => onComplete(task, e)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 hover:border-white hover:bg-white hover:text-black text-neutral-400 flex items-center justify-center transition-all group/btn" title="Mark Complete">
            <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5] group-hover/btn:scale-110 transition-transform" />
          </button>
          <button onClick={(e) => onEdit(task, e)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 hover:border-white hover:bg-white/10 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-all" title="Edit task">
            <Pencil className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: lib/notifications/service.ts
````typescript
import { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { prisma } from '../prisma';
import { channels } from './channels';
import { getSettings, isTypeEnabled } from './preferences';
import { isQuietNow, quietEndsAt } from './quietHours';
import {
  NOTIFICATION_TYPES,
  isNotificationType,
  type NotifyInput,
  type NotifyResult,
} from './types';

const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 4;
const LEASE_MS = 5 * 60_000;

// ==========================================================
// PUBLIC API - the only function other features need to call.
//
//   await notify({
//     userId, type: 'TASK_WARNING', dedupeKey: `task:${id}:warn`,
//     title: 'Due soon', body: '...', url: '/',
//   });
//
// It applies the user's preferences and quiet hours, makes the call idempotent via
// dedupeKey, and delivers through every registered channel.
// ==========================================================
export async function notify(input: NotifyInput): Promise<NotifyResult> {
  if (!isNotificationType(input.type)) throw new Error(`Unknown notification type: ${input.type}`);
  const def = NOTIFICATION_TYPES[input.type];
  const now = new Date();
  const settings = await getSettings(input.userId);

  let status: 'PENDING' | 'SKIPPED' = 'PENDING';
  let reason: string | undefined;
  let sendAfter = now;

  if (!isTypeEnabled(settings, input.type)) {
    status = 'SKIPPED';
    reason = 'disabled';
  } else if (isQuietNow(now, settings) && def.quietHours !== 'ignore') {
    if (def.quietHours === 'skip') {
      status = 'SKIPPED';
      reason = 'quiet_hours';
    } else {
      sendAfter = quietEndsAt(now, settings) ?? now;
    }
  }

  let logId: string;
  try {
    const log = await prisma.notificationLog.create({
      data: {
        userId: input.userId,
        type: input.type,
        dedupeKey: input.dedupeKey,
        title: input.title.slice(0, 200),
        body: input.body.slice(0, 1000),
        url: input.url?.slice(0, 2048),
        data: input.data as Prisma.InputJsonValue | undefined,
        status,
        error: reason,
        sendAfter,
        expiresAt: input.expiresAt,
      },
    });
    logId = log.id;
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError && e.code === 'P2002') {
      return { status: 'DUPLICATE' };
    }
    throw e;
  }

  if (status === 'SKIPPED') return { status: 'SKIPPED', reason };
  if (sendAfter > now) return { status: 'DEFERRED', reason: 'quiet_hours' };
  return dispatch(logId);
}

// Sends one stored notification. Safe against concurrent runners: a short lease is taken first.
async function dispatch(id: string): Promise<NotifyResult> {
  const now = new Date();
  const claimed = await prisma.notificationLog.updateMany({
    where: { id, status: 'PENDING', sendAfter: { lte: now } },
    data: { sendAfter: new Date(now.getTime() + LEASE_MS), attempts: { increment: 1 } },
  });
  if (claimed.count === 0) return { status: 'DUPLICATE', reason: 'already handled' };

  const log = await prisma.notificationLog.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } });
  const finish = (status: 'SENT' | 'SKIPPED' | 'FAILED', error?: string) =>
    prisma.notificationLog.update({
      where: { id },
      data: { status, error: error ? error.slice(0, 1000) : null, sentAt: status === 'SENT' ? new Date() : null },
    });

  if (log.expiresAt && log.expiresAt < now) {
    await finish('SKIPPED', 'expired');
    return { status: 'SKIPPED', reason: 'expired' };
  }

  // Preferences may have changed while a deferred notification was waiting
  if (isNotificationType(log.type) && !isTypeEnabled(await getSettings(log.userId), log.type)) {
    await finish('SKIPPED', 'disabled');
    return { status: 'SKIPPED', reason: 'disabled' };
  }

  const message = {
    title: log.title,
    body: log.body,
    url: log.url ?? undefined,
    data: (log.data as Record<string, unknown> | null) ?? undefined,
    tag: log.dedupeKey,
  };
  const outcomes = await Promise.all(
    channels.map((c) => c.send(log.userId, message).catch((e) => ({ status: 'failed' as const, error: String(e?.message ?? e) })))
  );

  if (outcomes.some((o) => o.status === 'sent')) {
    await finish('SENT');
    return { status: 'SENT' };
  }

  const failure = outcomes.find((o) => o.status === 'failed');
  if (failure && failure.status === 'failed') {
    if (log.attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
      await finish('FAILED', failure.error);
      return { status: 'FAILED', reason: failure.error };
    }
    await prisma.notificationLog.update({
      where: { id },
      data: { error: failure.error.slice(0, 1000), sendAfter: new Date(Date.now() + log.attempts * 2 * 60_000) },
    });
    return { status: 'RETRY', reason: failure.error };
  }

  await finish('SKIPPED', 'no_devices');
  return { status: 'SKIPPED', reason: 'no_devices' };
}

// Sends everything that is due: deferred (quiet hours) and retries. Called by the cron route.
export async function flushPending(limit = 100): Promise<{ processed: number }> {
  const due = await prisma.notificationLog.findMany({
    where: { status: 'PENDING', sendAfter: { lte: new Date() } },
    orderBy: { sendAfter: 'asc' },
    take: limit,
    select: { id: true },
  });
  for (const { id } of due) await dispatch(id);
  return { processed: due.length };
}
````

## File: lib/notifications/types.ts
````typescript
// Central registry of notification types. To add a new kind of notification, add one entry here
// and call notify() with that type. Nothing else needs to change.

export type QuietHoursPolicy =
  | 'skip' // drop it if it falls inside quiet hours
  | 'defer' // hold it and send when quiet hours end
  | 'ignore'; // always send immediately

export interface NotificationTypeDef {
  label: string;
  description: string;
  defaultEnabled: boolean;
  quietHours: QuietHoursPolicy;
}

export const NOTIFICATION_TYPES = {
  TASK_WARNING: {
    label: 'Deadline warnings',
    description: 'One alert when a task enters its warning window before the deadline.',
    defaultEnabled: true,
    quietHours: 'defer',
  },
  TASK_REPEAT: {
    label: 'Repeating reminders',
    description: 'Follow-up reminders at the interval set on a task until it is due.',
    defaultEnabled: true,
    quietHours: 'skip',
  },
  TEST: {
    label: 'Test notification',
    description: 'Manually triggered from the settings panel.',
    defaultEnabled: true,
    quietHours: 'ignore',
  },
  // The two below are controlled from the Validation panel, not the bell panel's toggles
  VALIDATION_DIGEST: {
    label: 'Validation reminders',
    description: 'Goals that need validating, at the times chosen in the Validation panel.',
    defaultEnabled: true,
    quietHours: 'ignore', // the user picked these times themselves
  },
  GOAL_RESUMED: {
    label: 'Goal resumed',
    description: 'A paused goal reached its resume date and needs validating.',
    defaultEnabled: true,
    quietHours: 'defer',
  },
} as const satisfies Record<string, NotificationTypeDef>;

export type NotificationType = keyof typeof NOTIFICATION_TYPES;

// Types the user can switch on/off in the UI
export const USER_TOGGLEABLE_TYPES: NotificationType[] = ['TASK_WARNING', 'TASK_REPEAT'];

export function isNotificationType(value: string): value is NotificationType {
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(NOTIFICATION_TYPES, value);
}

// What a channel actually delivers
export interface NotificationMessage {
  title: string;
  body: string;
  url?: string; // same-origin path, e.g. "/progress"
  data?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export interface NotifyInput extends NotificationMessage {
  userId: string;
  type: NotificationType;
  // Unique per logical notification. Re-sending the same key is a no-op.
  dedupeKey: string;
  // Drop instead of sending if still unsent after this moment
  expiresAt?: Date;
}

export type NotifyStatus = 'SENT' | 'DEFERRED' | 'SKIPPED' | 'DUPLICATE' | 'RETRY' | 'FAILED';

export interface NotifyResult {
  status: NotifyStatus;
  reason?: string;
}

// A delivery mechanism (web push now; email/SMS later). Implement and register in channels/index.ts.
export type ChannelOutcome =
  | { status: 'sent' }
  | { status: 'no_target' } // the user has nowhere to receive this on this channel
  | { status: 'failed'; error: string };

export interface NotificationChannel {
  id: string;
  send(userId: string, message: NotificationMessage & { tag: string }): Promise<ChannelOutcome>;
}

// Anything that creates notifications on a schedule. Implement and register in producers/index.ts.
export interface NotificationProducer {
  name: string;
  run(now: Date): Promise<{ created: number }>;
}
````

## File: lib/auth.ts
````typescript
// lib/auth.ts
import { SignJWT, jwtVerify } from 'jose';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';

const secretKey = process.env.JWT_SECRET;
if (!secretKey || secretKey.length < 32) {
  throw new Error('JWT_SECRET must be set to a random string of at least 32 characters');
}
const key = new TextEncoder().encode(secretKey);

// Create a 72-hour token
export async function encrypt(payload: any) {
  return await new SignJWT(payload)
    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'HS256' })
    .setIssuedAt()
    .setExpirationTime('72h')
    .sign(key);
}

// Read the token
export async function decrypt(input: string): Promise<any> {
  try {
    const { payload } = await jwtVerify(input, key, { algorithms: ['HS256'] });
    return payload;
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
}

// Helper to get the current user ID inside Server Actions
export async function getSession() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const session = cookieStore.get('gof_session')?.value;
  if (!session) return null;
  return await decrypt(session);
}

// Log out
export async function destroySession() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  cookieStore.set('gof_session', '', { expires: new Date(0) });
}
````
