## File: components/goals/dialog/SubGoalsSection.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { GitBranch, Plus, Link2, Unlink, ChevronRight, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { createGoal, attachSubGoal, detachSubGoal } from '../../../app/goals/actions';
import { useGoals } from '../../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import type { GoalDetail } from '../../../types/goals';
import StatusBadge from '../StatusBadge';
import WorkTimeBadge from '../WorkTimeBadge';
import GoalPicker from '../GoalPicker';

interface SubGoalsSectionProps {
  detail: GoalDetail;
}

export default function SubGoalsSection({ detail }: SubGoalsSectionProps) {
  const { goals, notify, refresh, openGoal } = useGoals();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'none' | 'create' | 'link'>('none');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  // Merge live metrics (time rollups, task counts, renames) from GoalsProvider onto child rows
  const liveChildren = useMemo(() => {
    const byId = new Map(goals.map(g => [g.id, g]));
    return detail.children.map(c => byId.get(c.id) ?? c);
  }, [detail.children, goals]);

  // Only parentless goals can be linked, and never this goal or one of its ancestors
  const linkable = useMemo(() => {
    const blocked = new Set([detail.id, ...detail.ancestors.map(a => a.id)]);
    return goals.filter(g => !g.parentId && !blocked.has(g.id));
  }, [goals, detail.id, detail.ancestors]);

  const handleCreate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (busy || !name.trim()) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await createGoal({ name, parentId: detail.id });
      if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
      setName('');
      setMode('none');
      notify(`Created and linked “${res.data.name}”`);
      await refresh();
    } catch {
      notify('Could not create the sub-goal.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLink = async (childId: string) => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await attachSubGoal(detail.id, childId);
      if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
      setMode('none');
      notify(`Linked “${res.data.name}” as a sub-goal`);
      await refresh();
    } catch {
      notify('Could not link the goal.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDetach = async (childId: string, childName: string) => {
    const res = await detachSubGoal(childId);
    if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
    notify(`Detached “${childName}”. It is now a top-level goal.`);
    await refresh();
  };

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="subgoals-heading">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
        <h3 id="subgoals-heading" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400 whitespace-nowrap">
          <GitBranch className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Sub-goals <span className="text-neutral-600">{liveChildren.length}</span>
        </h3>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button onClick={() => setMode(m => (m === 'link' ? 'none' : 'link'))} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white whitespace-nowrap hover:border-white active:scale-95 transition-all">
            <Link2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Link existing
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => setMode(m => (m === 'create' ? 'none' : 'create'))} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all">
            <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Create &amp; link
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {mode === 'create' && (
        <form onSubmit={handleCreate} className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <input
            autoFocus
            value={name}
            onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
            maxLength={120}
            placeholder="Sub-goal name"
            aria-label="Sub-goal name"
            className="flex-1 bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] focus:border-white rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none transition-all"
          />
          <button type="submit" disabled={busy || !name.trim()} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-40 active:scale-95 transition-all">Add</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('none')} aria-label="Cancel" className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full text-neutral-500 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
        </form>
      )}

      {mode === 'link' && (
        <GoalPicker
          goals={linkable}
          onPick={g => handleLink(g.id)}
          placeholder="Search top-level goals…"
          emptyText="No unlinked goals. Create a goal first, or detach one from its parent."
          className="mb-3"
        />
      )}

      {liveChildren.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-neutral-800 py-5 text-center text-xs text-neutral-600">No sub-goals attached.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-2">
          {liveChildren.map(c => (
            <li key={c.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#222222] bg-[#0f0f0f] pl-4 pr-2 py-2 hover:border-neutral-600 transition-colors">
              <button onClick={() => openGoal(c.id, 'push')} className="flex-1 min-w-0 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-1 text-left group">
                <span className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="block text-sm font-medium text-white truncate">{c.name}</span>
                  <span className="block text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">{c.subGoalCount} sub-goals · {c.taskCount} tasks</span>
                  <WorkTimeBadge deepMinutes={c.deepMinutes} shallowMinutes={c.shallowMinutes} compact className="mt-1" />
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                  <StatusBadge status={c.status} />
                  <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-600 group-hover:text-white transition-colors" />
                </span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleDetach(c.id, c.name)}
                aria-label={`Detach ${c.name}`}
                title="Detach from this goal"
                className="shrink-0 w-8 h-8 rounded-full text-neutral-500 hover:text-white hover:bg-neutral-800 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
              >
                <Unlink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/GoalNode.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { Handle, Position, type Node, type NodeProps } from '@xyflow/react';
import { GitBranch, ListChecks } from 'lucide-react';
import type { GoalStatus, MetricType } from '../../types/goals';
import { formatTarget, formatValue, progressRatio } from '../../lib/goals/metrics';
import StatusBadge from './StatusBadge';
import WorkTimeBadge from './WorkTimeBadge';

export type GoalNodeData = {
  name: string;
  status: GoalStatus;
  subGoalCount: number;
  taskCount: number;
  deepMinutes: number;
  shallowMinutes: number;
  isRoot: boolean;
};

export type GoalFlowNode = Node<GoalNodeData, 'goal'>;

export type TaskNodeData = {
  title: string;
  metricType: MetricType;
  unit: string | null;
  current: number;
  target: number;
  goalId: string;
};

export type TaskFlowNode = Node<TaskNodeData, 'task'>;

const hidden = '!opacity-0 !pointer-events-none !w-1 !h-1';

export function GoalNode({ data }: NodeProps<GoalFlowNode>) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`w-[220px] rounded-2xl border bg-[#0a0a0a] p-3.5 cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:border-white ${
        data.isRoot ? 'border-white shadow-[0_0_24px_rgba(255,255,255,0.12)]' : 'border-[#333333]'
      } ${data.status === 'COMPLETED' ? 'opacity-70' : ''}`}
    >
      <Handle type="target" position={Position.Top} className={hidden} />
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="font-headline text-sm font-semibold text-white leading-snug break-words line-clamp-2">{data.name}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2"><StatusBadge status={data.status} /></div>
      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3 text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-500">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><GitBranch className="w-3 h-3" /> {data.subGoalCount}</span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><ListChecks className="w-3 h-3" /> {data.taskCount}</span>
      </div>
      <WorkTimeBadge deepMinutes={data.deepMinutes} shallowMinutes={data.shallowMinutes} compact className="mt-2" />
      <Handle type="source" position={Position.Bottom} className={hidden} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function TaskLeafNode({ data }: NodeProps<TaskFlowNode>) {
  const ratio = progressRatio(data.current, data.target);
  const label = data.metricType === 'YESNO'
    ? (data.current >= 1 ? 'Done' : 'Not done')
    : `${formatValue(data, data.current)} / ${formatTarget({ metricType: data.metricType, unit: data.unit, target: data.target })}`;

  return (
    <div className="w-[220px] rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[#2a2a2a] bg-[#0d0d0d] px-3 py-2.5 cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:border-neutral-400">
      <Handle type="target" position={Position.Top} className={hidden} />
      <div className="text-xs text-neutral-200 truncate">{data.title}</div>
      <div className="mt-1.5 h-1 rounded-full bg-neutral-900 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-white transition-all" style={{ width: `${ratio * 100}%` }} />
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-500 truncate">{label}</div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/GraphTab.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Background, Controls, ReactFlow, ReactFlowProvider, useNodesState, useReactFlow,
  type Edge, type Node, type NodeMouseHandler,
} from '@xyflow/react';
import '@xyflow/react/dist/style.css';
import { ChevronDown, Network } from 'lucide-react';
import { useGoals } from '../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import type { GoalTree } from '../../types/goals';
import { buildGoalTree } from '../../lib/goals/graph';
import { layoutTree } from '../../lib/goals/treeLayout';
import GoalPicker from './GoalPicker';
import { GoalNode, TaskLeafNode, type GoalNodeData, type TaskNodeData } from './GoalNode';
import StatusBadge from './StatusBadge';

// Defined outside the component so React Flow does not remount nodes on every render
const nodeTypes = { goal: GoalNode, task: TaskLeafNode };

function buildNodeData(tree: GoalTree): Map<string, GoalNodeData | TaskNodeData> {
  const map = new Map<string, GoalNodeData | TaskNodeData>();
  for (const n of tree.nodes) {
    if (n.kind === 'goal') {
      map.set(n.id, {
        name: n.name,
        status: n.status,
        subGoalCount: n.subGoalCount,
        taskCount: n.taskCount,
        isRoot: n.id === tree.rootId,
        deepMinutes: n.deepMinutes,
        shallowMinutes: n.shallowMinutes,
      });
    } else {
      map.set(n.id, {
        title: n.title,
        metricType: n.metricType,
        unit: n.unit,
        current: n.current,
        target: n.target,
        goalId: n.goalId,
      });
    }
  }
  return map;
}

function buildFlowNodes(tree: GoalTree): Node[] {
  const positions = layoutTree(tree.rootId, tree.nodes.map(n => ({ id: n.id, level: n.level })), tree.edges);
  const dataById = buildNodeData(tree);

  return tree.nodes.map(n => {
    const position = positions.get(n.id) ?? { x: 0, y: 0 };
    const data = dataById.get(n.id)!;
    if (n.kind === 'goal') {
      return { id: n.id, type: 'goal', position, data };
    }
    return { id: n.id, type: 'task', position, data, draggable: true };
  });
}

function getTopologyKey(tree: GoalTree): string {
  const nodeIds = tree.nodes.map(n => `${n.kind}:${n.id}:${n.level}`).sort().join(',');
  const edgeIds = tree.edges.map(e => `${e.source}->${e.target}`).sort().join(',');
  return `${tree.rootId}|${nodeIds}|${edgeIds}`;
}

interface FlowProps {
  tree: GoalTree;
  onGoalClick: (goalId: string) => void;
}

function Flow({ tree, onGoalClick }: FlowProps) {
  const { fitView } = useReactFlow();
  const topologyKey = useMemo(() => getTopologyKey(tree), [tree]);

  const edges = useMemo<Edge[]>(() => {
    const taskIds = new Set(tree.nodes.filter(n => n.kind === 'task').map(n => n.id));
    return tree.edges.map(e => ({
      id: e.id,
      source: e.source,
      target: e.target,
      type: 'smoothstep',
      style: taskIds.has(e.target)
        ? { stroke: '#404040', strokeDasharray: '4 4' }
        : { stroke: '#737373', strokeWidth: 1.5 },
    }));
  }, [tree.nodes, tree.edges]);

  const [nodes, setNodes, onNodesChange] = useNodesState<Node>([]);
  const lastTopology = useRef<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (lastTopology.current !== topologyKey) {
      // Structure changed (or initial mount): compute layout and frame the graph
      lastTopology.current = topologyKey;
      setNodes(buildFlowNodes(tree));
      const t = setTimeout(() => fitView({ padding: 0.25, duration: 300, maxZoom: 1.1 }), 60);
      return () => clearTimeout(t);
    }
    // Data-only change (e.g., +15m or rename): skip layoutTree() and preserve dragged positions + camera
    const dataById = buildNodeData(tree);
    setNodes(prev => prev.map(n => {
      const nextData = dataById.get(n.id);
      return nextData ? { ...n, data: nextData } : n;
    }));
  }, [tree, topologyKey, setNodes, fitView]);

  const handleNodeClick: NodeMouseHandler = useCallback((_, node) => {
    if (node.type === 'goal') onGoalClick(node.id);
    else {
      const data = node.data as TaskNodeData;
      if (data?.goalId) onGoalClick(data.goalId);
    }
  }, [onGoalClick]);

  return (
    <ReactFlow
      nodes={nodes}
      edges={edges}
      onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
      onNodeClick={handleNodeClick}
      nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
      colorMode="dark"
      nodesConnectable={false}
      elementsSelectable={false}
      minZoom={0.2}
      maxZoom={1.6}
      fitView
      fitViewOptions={{ padding: 0.25, maxZoom: 1.1 }}
      style={{ background: '#000000' }}
    >
      <Background color="#1f1f1f" gap={24} />
      <Controls showInteractive={false} />
    </ReactFlow>
  );
}

export default function GraphTab() {
  const { goals, tasks, loading, openGoal } = useGoals();
  const [chosenRootId, setChosenRootId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [depth, setDepth] = useState<1 | 2>(1);
  const [showTasks, setShowTasks] = useState(false);
  const [showCompleted, setShowCompleted] = useState(false);
  const [pickerOpen, setPickerOpen] = useState(false);

  // The user's pick wins while it still exists; otherwise fall back to a sensible default
  const rootId = useMemo(() => {
    if (chosenRootId && goals.some(g => g.id === chosenRootId)) return chosenRootId;
    const candidate =
      goals.find(g => !g.parentId && g.status !== 'COMPLETED' && g.subGoalCount > 0) ??
      goals.find(g => !g.parentId && g.status !== 'COMPLETED') ??
      goals[0] ?? null;
    return candidate?.id ?? null;
  }, [chosenRootId, goals]);

  const visibleTree = useMemo(
    () => (rootId ? buildGoalTree(goals, tasks, rootId, depth, showTasks) : null),
    [goals, tasks, rootId, depth, showTasks],
  );

  const root = goals.find(g => g.id === rootId) ?? null;

  const pickable = useMemo(
    () => goals.filter(g => showCompleted || g.status !== 'COMPLETED' || g.id === rootId),
    [goals, showCompleted, rootId],
  );

  if (!loading && goals.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-neutral-800 py-14 text-center">
        <Network className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto text-neutral-700" />
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-300">Nothing to show yet</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-600">Create goals and link sub-goals to see them as a graph.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const hasChildren = !!visibleTree && visibleTree.nodes.some(n => n.kind === 'goal' && n.id !== visibleTree.rootId);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-3 lg:items-center">
        {/* Root selector */}
        <div className="relative lg:w-80">
          <button
            onClick={() => setPickerOpen(o => !o)}
            aria-haspopup="listbox"
            aria-expanded={pickerOpen}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[#262626] bg-[#141414] px-3.5 py-2.5 text-left hover:border-neutral-500 transition-colors"
          >
            <span className="min-w-0">
              <span className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">Parent goal</span>
              <span className="block text-sm text-white truncate">{root?.name ?? 'Select a goal'}</span>
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
              {root && <StatusBadge status={root.status} />}
              <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 text-neutral-500 transition-transform ${pickerOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
            </span>
          </button>

          {pickerOpen && (
            <>
              <button aria-label="Close" className="fixed inset-0 z-20 cursor-default" onClick={() => setPickerOpen(false)} />
              <div className="absolute z-30 mt-2 w-full shadow-2xl">
                <GoalPicker
                  goals={pickable}
                  onPick={g => { setChosenRootId(g.id); setPickerOpen(false); }}
                  placeholder="Search goals…"
                />
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Depth */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">Branching</span>
          <div role="radiogroup" aria-label="Branching level" className="inline-flex p-0.5 rounded-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800">
            {([1, 2] as const).map(d => (
              <button
                key={d}
                role="radio"
                aria-checked={depth === d}
                onClick={() => setDepth(d)}
                className={`w-10 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${depth === d ? 'bg-white text-black' : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'}`}
              >
                {d}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 lg:ml-auto text-xs text-neutral-400">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-white transition-colors">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={showTasks} onChange={e => setShowTasks(e.target.checked)} className="accent-white w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Show tasks
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-white transition-colors">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={showCompleted} onChange={e => setShowCompleted(e.target.checked)} className="accent-white w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Show completed
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative mt-4 h-[62vh] min-h-[380px] rounded-2xl border border-[#222222] overflow-hidden bg-black">
        {visibleTree && rootId ? (
          <>
            <ReactFlowProvider>
              <Flow tree={visibleTree} onGoalClick={id => openGoal(id)} />
            </ReactFlowProvider>
            {!hasChildren && !showTasks && (
              <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-10 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 bg-black/90 px-4 py-2 text-[11px] text-neutral-400 text-center">
                This goal has no sub-goals yet. Open it to create or link one.
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-mono text-neutral-600">
            {loading ? 'Building graph…' : 'Select a parent goal to see its branches.'}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/TaskProgressCard.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Minus, Plus, Flame, Undo2, Ellipsis, Target, Check, X, Pencil, Unlink, Trash2, Link2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { adjustTaskValue, undoLastChange, updateGoalTask, detachTask, deleteTask } from '../../app/goals/actions';
import { useGoals } from '../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import type { GoalTaskDTO } from '../../types/goals';
import {
  METRIC_CONFIG, clampValue, formatTarget, formatValue, isComplete, progressRatio, round2, stepLabel,
} from '../../lib/goals/metrics';
import { currentStreak, dailySeries, todayTotal } from '../../lib/goals/streak';
import { METRIC_ICON } from './MetricTypePicker';
import Sparkline from './Sparkline';
import TaskTimeRow from './TaskTimeRow';

interface TaskProgressCardProps {
  task: GoalTaskDTO;
  showGoalChip?: boolean;
  onOpenGoal?: (goalId: string) => void;
  onAttach?: (task: GoalTaskDTO) => void;
  onStructuralChange?: () => void;
}

const RING_R = 50;
const RING_C = 2 * Math.PI * RING_R;
const FLUSH_DELAY_MS = 500;
const HOLD_DELAY_MS = 380;

export default function TaskProgressCard({ task, showGoalChip = true, onOpenGoal, onAttach, onStructuralChange }: TaskProgressCardProps) {
  const { patchTask, removeTask, logs, requestLogs, addLog, popLog, notify } = useGoals();
  const taskRef = useRef(task);
  taskRef.current = task;

  const config = METRIC_CONFIG[task.metricType];
  const Icon = METRIC_ICON[task.metricType];

  const [value, setValue] = useState(task.current);
  const [step, setStep] = useState(config.steps[0]);
  const [celebrate, setCelebrate] = useState(false);
  const [typing, setTyping] = useState(false);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [confirmDelete, setConfirmDelete] = useState(false);

  // valueRef is the optimistic value; lastServer is the last value the server confirmed
  const valueRef = useRef(task.current);
  const lastServer = useRef(task.current);
  const pendingRef = useRef(0);
  const inflight = useRef(0);
  const chain = useRef<Promise<void>>(Promise.resolve());
  const flushTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const holdTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const celebrateTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const didRepeat = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => { requestLogs([task.id]); }, [task.id, requestLogs]);

  // Accept outside changes (undo elsewhere, refresh) only when nothing local is in flight
  const serverCurrent = task.current;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (pendingRef.current === 0 && inflight.current === 0) {
      valueRef.current = serverCurrent;
      lastServer.current = serverCurrent;
      setValue(serverCurrent);
    }
  }, [serverCurrent]);

  const enqueue = useCallback((fn: () => Promise<void>) => {
    inflight.current++;
    chain.current = chain.current.then(fn).catch(() => undefined).then(() => {
      inflight.current--;
      if (pendingRef.current === 0 && inflight.current === 0) {
        valueRef.current = lastServer.current;
        setValue(lastServer.current);
      }
    });
    return chain.current;
  }, []);

  const commit = useCallback(async (input: { delta: number } | { set: number }) => {
    try {
      const res = await adjustTaskValue(taskRef.current.id, input);
      if (res.ok === false) {
        notify(res.error);
        return;
      }
      lastServer.current = res.data.task.current;
      patchTask(res.data.task);
      if (res.data.log) addLog(res.data.log);
    } catch {
      notify('Could not save your change. Reverted.');
    }
  }, [addLog, notify, patchTask]);

  const flush = useCallback(() => {
    if (flushTimer.current) {
      clearTimeout(flushTimer.current);
      flushTimer.current = null;
    }
    const delta = round2(pendingRef.current);
    if (delta === 0) return chain.current;
    pendingRef.current = 0;
    return enqueue(() => commit({ delta }));
  }, [commit, enqueue]);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (holdTimer.current) clearTimeout(holdTimer.current);
    if (celebrateTimer.current) clearTimeout(celebrateTimer.current);
    flush();
  }, [flush]);

  const applyDelta = useCallback((delta: number) => {
    const t = taskRef.current;
    const prev = valueRef.current;
    const next = clampValue(t.metricType, prev + delta);
    if (next === prev) return false;

    valueRef.current = next;
    setValue(next);
    pendingRef.current = round2(pendingRef.current + (next - prev));

    if (prev < t.target && next >= t.target) {
      setCelebrate(true);
      if (celebrateTimer.current) clearTimeout(celebrateTimer.current);
      celebrateTimer.current = setTimeout(() => setCelebrate(false), 1300);
    }

    if (flushTimer.current) clearTimeout(flushTimer.current);
    flushTimer.current = setTimeout(() => { flush(); }, FLUSH_DELAY_MS);
    return true;
  }, [flush]);

  // Press and hold repeats the step with acceleration; the whole hold is saved as one history entry
  const startHold = (sign: 1 | -1) => {
    didRepeat.current = false;
    applyDelta(sign * step);
    holdTimer.current = setTimeout(function tick() {
      didRepeat.current = true;
      applyDelta(sign * step);
      holdSpeed.current = Math.max(45, holdSpeed.current - 8);
      holdTimer.current = setTimeout(tick, holdSpeed.current);
    }, HOLD_DELAY_MS);
  };
  const holdSpeed = useRef(140);

  const stopHold = () => {
    if (holdTimer.current) {
      clearTimeout(holdTimer.current);
      holdTimer.current = null;
    }
    holdSpeed.current = 140;
    if (didRepeat.current) {
      didRepeat.current = false;
      flush();
    }
  };

  const holdProps = (sign: 1 | -1) => ({
    onPointerDown: (e: React.PointerEvent) => { if (e.button === 0) startHold(sign); },
    onPointerUp: stopHold,
    onPointerLeave: stopHold,
    onPointerCancel: stopHold,
    onKeyDown: (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
        e.preventDefault();
        applyDelta(sign * step);
      }
    },
    onContextMenu: (e: React.MouseEvent) => e.preventDefault(),
  });

  const setExact = (n: number) => {
    flush();
    const next = clampValue(taskRef.current.metricType, n);
    const prev = valueRef.current;
    if (next === prev) return;
    valueRef.current = next;
    setValue(next);
    if (prev < taskRef.current.target && next >= taskRef.current.target) {
      setCelebrate(true);
      if (celebrateTimer.current) clearTimeout(celebrateTimer.current);
      celebrateTimer.current = setTimeout(() => setCelebrate(false), 1300);
    }
    enqueue(() => commit({ set: next }));
  };

  const startTyping = () => {
    if (task.metricType === 'YESNO') return;
    setDraft(String(value));
    setTyping(true);
  };

  const commitTyping = () => {
    setTyping(false);
    const n = Number(draft);
    if (draft.trim() !== '' && Number.isFinite(n)) setExact(n);
  };

  const handleUndo = () => {
    flush();
    enqueue(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await undoLastChange(taskRef.current.id);
        if (res.ok === false) {
          notify(res.error);
          return;
        }
        lastServer.current = res.data.task.current;
        valueRef.current = res.data.task.current;
        setValue(res.data.task.current);
        patchTask(res.data.task);
        popLog(taskRef.current.id);
      } catch {
        notify('Could not undo. Please try again.');
      }
    });
  };

  const handleDetach = async () => {
    setMenuOpen(false);
    await flush();
    const res = await detachTask(task.id);
    if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
    patchTask(res.data);
    notify('Task detached from goal');
    onStructuralChange?.();
  };

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    setConfirmDelete(false);
    if (flushTimer.current) clearTimeout(flushTimer.current);
    pendingRef.current = 0;
    const res = await deleteTask(task.id);
    if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
    removeTask(task.id);
    notify('Task deleted');
    onStructuralChange?.();
  };

  const taskLogs = useMemo(() => logs[task.id] ?? [], [logs, task.id]);
  const series = useMemo(() => dailySeries(taskLogs, 14).map(d => d.value), [taskLogs]);
  const streak = useMemo(() => currentStreak(taskLogs), [taskLogs]);
  const today = useMemo(() => todayTotal(taskLogs), [taskLogs]);

  const ratio = progressRatio(value, task.target);
  const done = isComplete(value, task.target);
  const over = task.metricType !== 'YESNO' && task.metricType !== 'PERCENT' && value > task.target;
  const isYesNo = task.metricType === 'YESNO';

  return (
    <div className={`relative rounded-2xl border bg-[#0a0a0a] p-4 sm:p-5 transition-colors duration-300 ${
      done ? 'border-white/70' : 'border-[#222222] hover:border-neutral-700'
    } ${celebrate ? 'motion-safe:animate-[goal-glow_1.2s_ease-out]' : ''}`}>
      {/* Title row */}
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-neutral-500">
            <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider">{config.label}</span>
            {done && <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-white"><Check className="w-3 h-3 stroke-[3]" /> Done</span>}
          </div>
          <h3 className="mt-1 font-headline text-base font-semibold text-white leading-snug break-words">{task.title}</h3>
        </div>

        <div className="relative shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={() => setMenuOpen(o => !o)}
            aria-label="Task options"
            aria-expanded={menuOpen}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:border-neutral-600 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
          >
            <Ellipsis className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
          {menuOpen && (
            <>
              <button aria-label="Close menu" className="fixed inset-0 z-10 cursor-default" onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)} />
              <div className="absolute right-0 mt-1 w-48 z-20 rounded-xl bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] p-1 shadow-xl">
                <MenuItem icon={Pencil} label="Edit" onClick={() => { setMenuOpen(false); setEditing(true); }} />
                {onAttach && <MenuItem icon={Link2} label={task.goalId ? 'Move to goal…' : 'Attach to goal…'} onClick={() => { setMenuOpen(false); onAttach(task); }} />}
                {task.goalId && <MenuItem icon={Unlink} label="Detach from goal" onClick={handleDetach} />}
                <MenuItem icon={Trash2} label="Delete" onClick={() => { setMenuOpen(false); setConfirmDelete(true); }} />
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Goal chip */}
      {showGoalChip && (
        <div className="mt-2">
          {task.goalId ? (
            <button
              onClick={() => task.goalId && onOpenGoal?.(task.goalId)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 max-w-full rounded-full border border-neutral-700 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-neutral-300 hover:border-white hover:text-white transition-colors"
            >
              <Target className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
              <span className="truncate">{task.goalName ?? 'Goal'}</span>
            </button>
          ) : (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-dashed border-neutral-800 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-neutral-600">Unattached</span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {editing ? (
        <EditPanel task={task} onClose={() => setEditing(false)} onSaved={t => { patchTask(t); setEditing(false); }} />
      ) : confirmDelete ? (
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-950 p-3">
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-300">Delete this task and all of its history? This cannot be undone.</p>
          <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(false)} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-white">Cancel</button>
            <button onClick={handleDelete} className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all">Delete</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : isYesNo ? (
        <button
          onClick={() => setExact(value >= 1 ? 0 : 1)}
          aria-pressed={value >= 1}
          className={`mt-4 w-full rounded-xl py-5 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold transition-all active:scale-[0.98] ${
            value >= 1 ? 'bg-white text-black' : 'border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 hover:border-white hover:text-white'
          }`}
        >
          <Check className={`w-5 h-5 ${value >= 1 ? 'stroke-[3]' : 'opacity-40'}`} />
          {value >= 1 ? 'Done' : 'Mark as done'}
        </button>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-4 sm:gap-5">
          {/* Progress ring */}
          <div className={`relative w-[112px] h-[112px] shrink-0 ${celebrate ? 'motion-safe:animate-[goal-pop_0.6s_ease-out]' : ''}`}>
            <svg viewBox="0 0 120 120" className="w-full h-full -rotate-90" aria-hidden="true">
              <circle cx="60" cy="60" r={RING_R} fill="none" stroke="#1c1c1c" strokeWidth="8" />
              <circle
                cx="60" cy="60" r={RING_R} fill="none" stroke="#ffffff" strokeWidth="8" strokeLinecap="round"
                strokeDasharray={RING_C}
                strokeDashoffset={RING_C * (1 - ratio)}
                className="transition-[stroke-dashoffset] duration-500 ease-out motion-reduce:transition-none"
              />
            </svg>
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-3 text-center">
              {typing ? (
                <input
                  autoFocus
                  type="number"
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  value={draft}
                  onChange={e => setDraft(e.target.value)}
                  onBlur={commitTyping}
                  onKeyDown={e => {
                    if (e.key === 'Enter') commitTyping();
                    if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.stopPropagation(); setTyping(false); }
                  }}
                  aria-label={task.metricType === 'DURATION' ? 'Exact value in minutes' : 'Exact value'}
                  className="w-16 bg-transparent border-b border-white text-center font-mono text-lg text-white outline-none"
                />
              ) : (
                <button onClick={startTyping} aria-label="Type an exact value" title="Tap to type an exact value" className="font-mono text-lg font-bold text-white leading-tight tabular-nums hover:text-neutral-300 transition-colors">
                  {formatValue(task, value)}
                </button>
              )}
              <span className="mt-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-500 leading-tight">
                {typing && task.metricType === 'DURATION' ? 'minutes' : task.metricType === 'PERCENT' ? 'of 100%' : `of ${formatTarget(task)}`}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Controls */}
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 flex flex-col items-center sm:items-start gap-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button
                {...holdProps(-1)}
                aria-label={`Decrease by ${step}`}
                className="w-12 h-12 sm:w-14 sm:h-14 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 text-white flex items-center justify-center hover:border-white active:scale-90 transition-all select-none touch-none"
              >
                <Minus className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
              <button
                {...holdProps(1)}
                aria-label={`Increase by ${step}`}
                className="w-12 h-12 sm:w-14 sm:h-14 rounded-full bg-white text-black flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-90 transition-all select-none touch-none"
              >
                <Plus className="w-5 h-5 stroke-[2.5]" />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Step size">
              {config.steps.map(s => (
                <button
                  key={s}
                  role="radio"
                  aria-checked={s === step}
                  onClick={() => setStep(s)}
                  className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-mono transition-colors ${
                    s === step ? 'bg-neutral-800 text-white border border-neutral-600' : 'text-neutral-500 border border-transparent hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  {stepLabel(task.metricType, s)}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            {over && <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-400">+{formatValue(task, round2(value - task.target))} over target</span>}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {!editing && !confirmDelete && <TaskTimeRow task={task} />}

      {!editing && !confirmDelete && (
        <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-[#1a1a1a] flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
            <Sparkline values={series} />
            <div className="min-w-0 text-[11px] font-mono leading-tight">
              <div className={today > 0 ? 'text-neutral-200' : 'text-neutral-600'}>
                {today > 0 ? (isYesNo ? 'Done today' : `Today +${formatValue(task, today)}`) : 'Nothing yet today'}
              </div>
              {streak > 0 && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-white">
                  <Flame className="w-3 h-3" /> {streak} day streak
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={handleUndo}
            disabled={taskLogs.length === 0}
            aria-label="Undo last change"
            className="shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 text-[11px] text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:border-neutral-600 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:hover:text-neutral-400 disabled:hover:border-neutral-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <Undo2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Undo
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      <span className="sr-only" aria-live="polite">{task.title}: {formatValue(task, value)}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function MenuItem({ icon: Icon, label, onClick }: { icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>; label: string; onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick} className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-2.5 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs text-neutral-300 hover:bg-neutral-800 hover:text-white transition-colors text-left">
      <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-500" /> {label}
    </button>
  );
}

function EditPanel({ task, onClose, onSaved }: { task: GoalTaskDTO; onClose: () => void; onSaved: (task: GoalTaskDTO) => void }) {
  const { notify } = useGoals();
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(task.title);
  const [target, setTarget] = useState(String(task.target));
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState(task.unit ?? '');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const hasTarget = task.metricType === 'COUNT' || task.metricType === 'DURATION' || task.metricType === 'CUSTOM';
  const inputCls = 'w-full bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] focus:border-white rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white outline-none transition-all';

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await updateGoalTask(task.id, {
        title,
        ...(hasTarget ? { target: Number(target) } : {}),
        ...(task.metricType === 'CUSTOM' ? { unit } : {}),
      });
      if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
      onSaved(res.data);
    } catch {
      notify('Could not save changes.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={save} className="mt-4 space-y-3">
      <input autoFocus value={title} onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)} maxLength={120} aria-label="Task name" className={inputCls} />
      {hasTarget && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <label className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
            Target{task.metricType === 'DURATION' ? ' (minutes)' : ''}
            <input type="number" min={1} value={target} onChange={e => setTarget(e.target.value)} className={`${inputCls} mt-1.5`} />
          </label>
          {task.metricType === 'CUSTOM' && (
            <label className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
              Unit
              <input value={unit} onChange={e => setUnit(e.target.value)} maxLength={20} className={`${inputCls} mt-1.5`} />
            </label>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
        <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs text-neutral-400 hover:text-white flex items-center gap-1"><X className="w-3 h-3" /> Cancel</button>
        <button type="submit" disabled={saving} className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50">Save</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
````

## File: components/CriticalLockdownModal.tsx
````typescript
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { Task } from '../types';
import ImportanceLightsHeader from './ImportanceLightsHeader';
import { FIELD_LIMITS } from '../lib/validation';

// Leaves room for the "MISSED DEADLINE: " prefix added before saving
const MAX_EXCUSE = FIELD_LIMITS.longText - 'MISSED DEADLINE: '.length;

interface CriticalLockdownModalProps {
  task: Task;
  onSubmitExcuse: (task: Task, excuse: string) => void;
}

export default function CriticalLockdownModal({ task, onSubmitExcuse }: CriticalLockdownModalProps) {
  const [excuse, setExcuse] = useState('');

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (excuse.trim().length < 10) return; // Force them to write at least a few words!
    onSubmitExcuse(task, excuse);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-red-950/90 backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center justify-center p-4 selection:bg-red-500 selection:text-white animate-in fade-in duration-300">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(ellipse_at_center,_var(--tw-gradient-stops))] from-red-900/20 via-black/80 to-black pointer-events-none"></div>
      
      <div className="bg-black border-2 border-red-500/50 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-xl max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain p-5 sm:p-8 relative shadow-[0_0_100px_rgba(239,68,68,0.2)] flex flex-col gap-4 sm:gap-6">
        
        {/* Warning Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center gap-3 border-b border-red-900/30 pb-4 sm:pb-6">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 sm:w-16 sm:h-16 rounded-full bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 flex items-center justify-center text-red-500 animate-pulse">
            <Lock className="w-6 h-6 sm:w-8 sm:h-8" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg sm:text-2xl font-bold text-red-500 font-headline uppercase tracking-wider sm:tracking-widest">Critical Deadline Missed</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-red-400/80 mt-1">Portal locked. Accountability required to proceed.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* The Missed Task */}
        <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 bg-red-950/20 border border-red-900/50 rounded-xl flex flex-col">
          <ImportanceLightsHeader level={5} lightColor="red" />
          <p className="text-xs font-mono text-red-400/60 mb-1.5 mt-2 flex items-center gap-2">
            <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            {/* BUG FIX: Converted the Date object into a readable string string */}
            Target was: {new Date(task.deadline).toLocaleString([], { dateStyle: 'short', timeStyle: 'short' })}
          </p>
          <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-white tracking-tight leading-snug">{task.title}</h4>
        </div>

        {/* The Mandatory Form */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-200 flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <span>Why was this task not completed on time?</span>
            </label>
            <div className="w-full bg-black border border-red-900/50 focus-within:border-red-500 rounded-xl p-3.5 transition-all">
              <textarea
                rows={4}
                required
                maxLength={MAX_EXCUSE}
                value={excuse}
                onChange={(e) => setExcuse(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Provide a detailed reason. Minimum 10 characters required..."
                className="w-full bg-transparent text-sm text-white placeholder-red-900/50 outline-none leading-relaxed resize-none font-sans"
              />
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-red-500/60 mt-2 font-mono text-right">
              {excuse.length < 10 ? `Need ${10 - excuse.length} more characters` : 'Ready to submit'}
            </p>
          </div>

          <button 
            type="submit" 
            disabled={excuse.length < 10}
            className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 disabled:bg-neutral-900 disabled:text-neutral-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-bold text-sm transition-all shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(239,68,68,0.2)] hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(239,68,68,0.4)]"
          >
            Submit Explanation & Unlock Portal
          </button>
        </form>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/NotificationSettings.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { Bell, X } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  getNotificationState,
  sendTestNotification,
  subscribePush,
  unsubscribePush,
  updateNotificationSettings,
  type NotificationState,
} from '../app/notifications/actions';

const VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY ?? '';

function urlBase64ToUint8Array(base64: string): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> {
  const padded = (base64 + '='.repeat((4 - (base64.length % 4)) % 4)).replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
  const raw = atob(padded);
  const out = new Uint8Array(new ArrayBuffer(raw.length));
  for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) out[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}

const toTime = (min: number | null) =>
  min === null ? '' : `${String(Math.floor(min / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(min % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;

const toMinutes = (value: string): number | null => {
  const m = /^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(value);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) * 60 + Number(m[2]) : null;
};

export default function NotificationSettings() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [supported, setSupported] = useState(true);
  const [permission, setPermission] = useState<NotificationPermission>('default');
  const [deviceSubscribed, setDeviceSubscribed] = useState(false);
  const [state, setState] = useState<NotificationState | null>(null);
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(true);
  const [typePrefs, setTypePrefs] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [quietOn, setQuietOn] = useState(false);
  const [quietStart, setQuietStart] = useState('22:00');
  const [quietEnd, setQuietEnd] = useState('07:00');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [diag, setDiag] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [syncCount, setSyncCount] = useState(0);

  const applyState = useCallback((s: NotificationState) => {
    setState(s);
    setEnabled(s.enabled);
    setTypePrefs(Object.fromEntries(s.types.map((t) => [t.type, t.enabled])));
    setQuietOn(s.quietStart !== null && s.quietEnd !== null);
    if (s.quietStart !== null) setQuietStart(toTime(s.quietStart));
    if (s.quietEnd !== null) setQuietEnd(toTime(s.quietEnd));
  }, []);

  const loadState = useCallback(async () => applyState(await getNotificationState()), [applyState]);

  // Registers the service worker and reports what this browser supports / has subscribed.
  // Every step is recorded in `diag` so problems are visible in the panel instead of swallowed.
  useEffect(() => {
    const detect = async () => {
      const lines: string[] = [];
      lines.push(`secure context: ${window.isSecureContext}`);
      lines.push(`serviceWorker: ${'serviceWorker' in navigator}, PushManager: ${'PushManager' in window}, Notification: ${'Notification' in window}`);
      lines.push(`VAPID public key in client bundle: ${VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY ? `yes (${VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY.length} chars)` : 'NO - restart the dev server / redeploy'}`);
      const ok = 'serviceWorker' in navigator && 'PushManager' in window && 'Notification' in window;
      if (!ok) return { supported: false, permission: 'default' as NotificationPermission, subscribed: false, synced: false, lines };

      lines.push(`permission: ${Notification.permission}`);
      let subscribed = false;
      let synced = false;
      try {
        const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js', { scope: '/', updateViaCache: 'none' });
        await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
        lines.push(`service worker active, scope ${reg.scope}`);
        const sub = await reg.pushManager.getSubscription();
        subscribed = Boolean(sub);
        lines.push(sub ? `browser subscription: yes (${new URL(sub.endpoint).host})` : 'browser subscription: none');

        // Keep the server copy fresh (also repairs a subscription the server lost)
        if (sub && Notification.permission === 'granted') {
          const json = sub.toJSON();
          if (json.endpoint && json.keys?.p256dh && json.keys?.auth) {
            try {
              const res = await subscribePush({ endpoint: json.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: json.keys.p256dh, auth: json.keys.auth } }, navigator.userAgent);
              synced = res.ok;
              lines.push(res.ok ? 'server sync: ok' : `server sync FAILED: ${res.error}`);
            } catch (e) {
              console.error('[notifications] server sync failed', e);
              lines.push(`server sync FAILED: ${(e as Error).message}`);
            }
          } else {
            lines.push('browser subscription is missing keys');
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('[notifications] setup failed', e);
        lines.push(`setup FAILED: ${(e as Error).name}: ${(e as Error).message}`);
      }
      return { supported: true, permission: Notification.permission, subscribed, synced, lines };
    };

    let cancelled = false;
    void detect().then((r) => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      setSupported(r.supported);
      setPermission(r.permission);
      setDeviceSubscribed(r.subscribed);
      setDiag(r.lines);
      if (r.synced) setSyncCount((n) => n + 1);
    });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    getNotificationState()
      .then((s) => {
        if (!cancelled) applyState(s);
      })
      .catch((e) => {
        console.error('[notifications] could not load state', e);
        if (!cancelled) setMessage(`Could not load settings: ${(e as Error).message}`);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [open, syncCount, applyState]);

  const enableDevice = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage('');
    try {
      if (!VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY) throw new Error('Push is not configured: the public key is missing from the client bundle. Restart the dev server or redeploy.');
      const result = await Notification.requestPermission();
      setPermission(result);
      if (result !== 'granted') throw new Error('Permission was not granted. Allow notifications in your browser settings.');

      const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
      const sub =
        (await reg.pushManager.getSubscription()) ??
        (await reg.pushManager.subscribe({
          userVisibleOnly: true,
          applicationServerKey: urlBase64ToUint8Array(VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY),
        }));
      const json = sub.toJSON();
      const res = await subscribePush(
        { endpoint: json.endpoint!, keys: { p256dh: json.keys!.p256dh, auth: json.keys!.auth } },
        navigator.userAgent
      );
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(res.error);
      setDeviceSubscribed(true);
      await loadState();
      setMessage('This device will now receive notifications.');
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[notifications] enable failed', e);
      const err = e as Error;
      setMessage(
        err.name === 'AbortError'
          ? 'The browser could not reach its push service (AbortError). Brave needs "Use Google services for push messaging" turned on; some networks/VPNs also block it.'
          : `${err.name !== 'Error' ? `${err.name}: ` : ''}${err.message || 'Could not enable notifications.'}`
      );
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const disableDevice = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage('');
    try {
      const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
      const sub = await reg.pushManager.getSubscription();
      if (sub) {
        await unsubscribePush(sub.endpoint);
        await sub.unsubscribe();
      }
      setDeviceSubscribed(false);
      await loadState();
      setMessage('Notifications disabled on this device.');
    } catch {
      setMessage('Could not disable notifications.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const save = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    const start = quietOn ? toMinutes(quietStart) : null;
    const end = quietOn ? toMinutes(quietEnd) : null;
    const res = await updateNotificationSettings({
      enabled,
      timezone: Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone,
      quietStart: start,
      quietEnd: end,
      typePrefs,
    });
    setMessage(res.ok ? 'Settings saved.' : res.error);
    setBusy(false);
  };

  const sendTest = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    const res = await sendTestNotification();
    setMessage(res.message);
    setBusy(false);
  };

  const toggle = (checked: boolean, onChange: (v: boolean) => void, label: string) => (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
      className={`w-9 h-5 rounded-full border transition-colors relative shrink-0 ${checked ? 'bg-white border-white' : 'bg-neutral-900 border-neutral-700'}`}
    >
      <span className={`absolute top-0.5 w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full transition-all ${checked ? 'left-[18px] bg-black' : 'left-0.5 bg-neutral-500'}`} />
    </button>
  );

  return (
    <>
      <button
        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
        aria-label="Notification settings"
        className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full border border-neutral-700 hover:border-white text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-all flex items-center justify-center"
      >
        <Bell className="w-4 h-4" />
        {deviceSubscribed && <span className="absolute -top-0.5 -right-0.5 w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white" />}
      </button>

      {/* Rendered in a portal: the header's backdrop-blur would otherwise become the containing block of this fixed overlay */}
      {open && createPortal(
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
          <div
            className="w-full max-w-md bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-white font-headline">Notifications</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} aria-label="Close" className="text-neutral-400 hover:text-white">
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {!supported ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400">This browser does not support push notifications.</p>
            ) : (
              <>
                <section className="space-y-2">
                  <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 font-mono">This device</p>
                  {permission === 'denied' ? (
                    <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400">Notifications are blocked. Allow them in your browser site settings, then reload.</p>
                  ) : deviceSubscribed ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <span className="text-xs text-neutral-300">Enabled on this device</span>
                      <button disabled={busy} onClick={disableDevice} className="text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 hover:border-white hover:text-white disabled:opacity-50">
                        Turn off
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <button disabled={busy} onClick={enableDevice} className="w-full text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50">
                      Enable notifications on this device
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {state && <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">{state.deviceCount} device(s) registered</p>}
                </section>

                {state && (
                  <>
                    <section className="space-y-3">
                      <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 font-mono">Preferences</p>
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <span className="text-xs text-white">All notifications</span>
                        {toggle(enabled, setEnabled, 'All notifications')}
                      </div>
                      {state.types.map((t) => (
                        <div key={t.type} className={`flex items-start justify-between gap-3 ${enabled ? '' : 'opacity-40'}`}>
                          <div>
                            <p className="text-xs text-white">{t.label}</p>
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">{t.description}</p>
                          </div>
                          {toggle(typePrefs[t.type] ?? true, (v) => setTypePrefs((p) => ({ ...p, [t.type]: v })), t.label)}
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </section>

                    <section className="space-y-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs text-white">Quiet hours</p>
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">Repeats are skipped; deadline warnings wait until they end.</p>
                        </div>
                        {toggle(quietOn, setQuietOn, 'Quiet hours')}
                      </div>
                      {quietOn && (
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <input type="time" value={quietStart} onChange={(e) => setQuietStart(e.target.value)} className="bg-black border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-white text-xs font-mono outline-none focus:border-white" />
                          <span className="text-neutral-500 text-xs">to</span>
                          <input type="time" value={quietEnd} onChange={(e) => setQuietEnd(e.target.value)} className="bg-black border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-white text-xs font-mono outline-none focus:border-white" />
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </section>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <button disabled={busy} onClick={save} className="flex-1 text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50">
                        Save
                      </button>
                      <button disabled={busy || !deviceSubscribed} onClick={sendTest} title={deviceSubscribed ? undefined : 'Enable notifications on this device first'} className="flex-1 text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 hover:border-white hover:text-white disabled:opacity-50">
                        Send test
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </>
                )}
              </>
            )}

            {message && <p role="status" className="text-xs text-neutral-300">{message}</p>}

            <details className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
              <summary className="cursor-pointer hover:text-neutral-300">Diagnostics</summary>
              <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 font-mono break-words">
                {diag.map((line) => (
                  <li key={line}>{line}</li>
                ))}
                {state && <li>server: {state.deviceCount} device(s) saved for your account</li>}
              </ul>
            </details>
          </div>
        </div>,
        document.body
      )}
    </>
  );
}
````
