## File: components/TimelineNode.tsx
````typescript
// components/TimelineNode.tsx
import React, { useState, useTransition } from 'react';
import { Plus, Minus, X, ChevronRight, ChevronDown, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { FIELD_LIMITS } from '../lib/validation';

const MAX_METRIC_VALUE = FIELD_LIMITS.maxValue;

interface Metric {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  val: number;
}

interface TimelineNodeProps {
  id: string;
  dateString: string;
  metrics: Metric[];
  isActive: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onAddMetric: (id: string, name: string, val: number) => void;
  onUpdateMetric: (metricId: string, newVal: number) => void;
  onDeleteMetric: (metricId: string) => void;
  onDeleteMilestone: (id: string) => void;
}

export default function TimelineNode({ 
  id, dateString, metrics, isActive, onSelect, onAddMetric, onUpdateMetric, onDeleteMetric, onDeleteMilestone 
}: TimelineNodeProps) {
  const [isAddMetricFormOpen, setIsAddMetricFormOpen] = useState(false);
  const [newMetricName, setNewMetricName] = useState('');
  const [newMetricVal, setNewMetricVal] = useState('20');
  
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  const handleAddSubmit = () => {
    const parsed = newMetricVal.trim() === '' ? 20 : parseInt(newMetricVal, 10);
    const val = Number.isFinite(parsed) ? Math.min(MAX_METRIC_VALUE, Math.max(-MAX_METRIC_VALUE, parsed)) : 20;
    const name = newMetricName.trim() || 'Custom Metric';
    
    onAddMetric(id, name, val);
    
    setNewMetricName('');
    setNewMetricVal('20');
    setIsAddMetricFormOpen(false);
  };

  const handleOptimisticUpdate = (metricId: string, newVal: number) => {
    onUpdateMetric(metricId, newVal);
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative flex flex-col group cursor-pointer" onClick={() => { onSelect(); setIsAddMetricFormOpen(false); }}>
      
      {/* Header Row */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4 select-none py-1">
        {/* Glowing Active Node */}
        <div className={`-ml-2 sm:-ml-[11px] md:-ml-[13px] w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-300 z-20 shrink-0
          ${isActive 
            ? 'bg-white text-black shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)] scale-110' 
            : 'bg-[#0a0a0a] border-2 border-[#222222] text-neutral-600 group-hover:border-neutral-500 group-hover:text-neutral-300'
          }`}
        >
          {isActive ? <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[3]" /> : <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[3]" />}
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-1">
          <span className={`text-sm tracking-wide font-mono transition-colors duration-300 
            ${isActive ? 'font-bold text-white' : 'text-neutral-500 group-hover:text-neutral-300'} 
          `}>
            {dateString}
          </span>
          {/* Subtle Trash Icon */}
          {isActive && (
            <button 
              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onDeleteMilestone(id); }}
              className="w-8 h-8 sm:w-6 sm:h-6 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full text-neutral-600 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/10 transition-colors animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200"
              title="Delete Milestone"
            >
              <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 sm:w-3.5 sm:h-3.5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Active Detail Panel - Smooth sliding animation */}
      {isActive && (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 sm:gap-4 md:gap-6 pt-3 pl-1 sm:pl-2 md:pl-5 cursor-default animate-in slide-in-from-top-4 fade-in duration-300 fill-mode-both" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
          
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 w-full max-w-xl pb-2 ml-1 sm:ml-4">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 w-full">
              {metrics.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="px-4 py-6 sm:py-4 rounded-xl bg-[#141414]/50 border border-[#262626] border-dashed text-neutral-500 text-xs font-mono flex items-center justify-center text-center">
                  NO METRICS RECORDED
                </div>
              ) : (
                metrics.map(metric => (
                  // Hover lifts and Glassmorphism
                  <div key={metric.id} className="relative group/card flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 bg-[#141414] hover:bg-[#1a1a1a] px-4 sm:px-5 py-4 rounded-xl border border-[#262626] hover:border-neutral-700 hover:shadow-lg sm:hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition-all duration-300 overflow-hidden w-full">
                    
                    <div className="flex flex-col relative z-10 w-full sm:w-auto">
                      
                      <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white tracking-tight break-words">{metric.name}</span>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3 relative z-10 bg-[#0a0a0a] p-1.5 sm:p-1 rounded-lg border border-[#222222] self-start sm:self-auto w-full sm:w-auto justify-between sm:justify-start">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
                        <button 
                          disabled={isPending}
                          onClick={() => handleOptimisticUpdate(metric.id, Math.max(0, metric.val - 1))} 
                          className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-7 sm:h-7 rounded-md bg-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-800 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
                        >
                          <Minus className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                        <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1 min-w-[50px] justify-center select-none">
                          <span className="text-lg font-bold text-white tracking-tighter">{metric.val}</span>
                        </div>
                        <button 
                          disabled={isPending}
                          onClick={() => handleOptimisticUpdate(metric.id, Math.min(MAX_METRIC_VALUE, metric.val + 1))} 
                          className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-7 sm:h-7 rounded-md bg-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-800 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95 transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
                        >
                          <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center">
                        <div className="w-[1px] h-6 sm:h-4 bg-neutral-800 mx-2 sm:mx-1"></div>
                        <button onClick={() => onDeleteMetric(metric.id)} className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-7 sm:h-7 rounded-md text-neutral-600 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/10 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors">
                          <X className="w-5 h-5 sm:w-4 sm:h-4" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Add Metric Form */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-1 w-full">
              {!isAddMetricFormOpen ? (
                <button onClick={() => setIsAddMetricFormOpen(true)} className="group w-full sm:w-fit flex items-center justify-center sm:justify-start gap-2 px-4 py-3 sm:py-2 rounded-lg bg-neutral-900/50 hover:bg-white hover:text-black text-neutral-400 font-mono text-xs transition-all border border-neutral-800 border-dashed hover:border-solid hover:shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(255,255,255,0.2)] active:scale-95">
                  <Plus className="w-4 h-4 sm:w-3.5 sm:h-3.5" />
                  <span className="font-semibold tracking-wide">ADD METRIC</span>
                </button>
              ) : (
                // MOBILE RESPONSIVE FORM: flex-col on mobile, flex-row on desktop
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-end gap-3 bg-[#111111] p-4 rounded-xl border border-neutral-700 shadow-xl animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2 w-full">
                  <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 w-full min-w-0">
                    <label className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold pb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white animate-pulse"></span>
                      Metric Name
                    </label>
                    <input 
                      autoFocus
                      type="text" 
                      value={newMetricName} 
                      maxLength={FIELD_LIMITS.metricName}
                      onChange={e => setNewMetricName(e.target.value)}
                      onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleAddSubmit(); if (e.key === 'Escape') setIsAddMetricFormOpen(false); }}
                      placeholder="e.g. Focus Hours, Code Output" 
                      className="bg-black border border-neutral-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-xs text-white placeholder-neutral-700 focus:outline-none focus:border-white w-full transition-colors"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-col w-full sm:w-24">
                    <label className="text-[10px] text-neutral-400 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold pb-2 hidden sm:block">Initial</label>
                    <input 
                      type="number" 
                      value={newMetricVal} 
                      min={-MAX_METRIC_VALUE}
                      max={MAX_METRIC_VALUE}
                      onChange={e => setNewMetricVal(e.target.value)}
                      onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleAddSubmit(); }}
                      className="bg-black border border-neutral-700 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-white w-full sm:text-center transition-colors"
                      placeholder="Pts"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-2 sm:pt-0 w-full sm:w-auto">
                    <button onClick={handleAddSubmit} className="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-5 py-3 sm:py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-white text-black text-xs font-bold hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all shadow-sm">Save</button>
                    <button onClick={() => setIsAddMetricFormOpen(false)} className="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-4 py-3 sm:py-2.5 rounded-lg text-neutral-400 bg-neutral-900 sm:bg-transparent hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors">Cancel</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: lib/goalValidation/digest.ts
````typescript
import type { DueGoal } from './settings';

const MAX_BODY_CHARS = 900;

// One bullet per goal, using just the goal name, sorted alphabetically.
export function buildDigest(due: DueGoal[]): { count: number; body: string } {
  const lines = due.map((g) => `• ${g.name}`).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));

  const kept: string[] = [];
  let used = 0;
  for (const line of lines) {
    if (kept.length > 0 && used + line.length > MAX_BODY_CHARS) break;
    kept.push(line.slice(0, MAX_BODY_CHARS));
    used += line.length + 1;
  }
  if (kept.length < due.length) kept.push(`+${due.length - kept.length} more`);

  return { count: due.length, body: kept.join('\n') };
}
````

## File: middleware.ts
````typescript
// middleware.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { decrypt } from './lib/auth';

const protectedRoutes = ['/', '/progress', '/goals']; // Add any other private routes here
const publicRoutes = ['/login'];

export async function middleware(req: NextRequest) {
  const path = req.nextUrl.pathname;
  const isProtectedRoute = protectedRoutes.includes(path);
  const isPublicRoute = publicRoutes.includes(path);

  const cookie = req.cookies.get('gof_session')?.value;
  const session = await decrypt(cookie || '');

  // 1. Not logged in + trying to access app = Kick to login
  if (isProtectedRoute && !session?.userId) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/login', req.nextUrl));
  }

  // 2. Already logged in + trying to view login page = Send to app
  if (isPublicRoute && session?.userId) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/', req.nextUrl));
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

// Ensure middleware only runs on routes, not static files/images/api or the PWA/push assets
// (the service worker and manifest must be reachable without a session)
export const config = {
  matcher: ['/((?!api|_next/static|_next/image|sounds|favicon.ico|sw\\.js|manifest\\.webmanifest|icon-192\\.png|icon-512\\.png|badge\\.png).*)'],
};
````

## File: app/actions.ts
````typescript
"use server";

import { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { revalidatePath, unstable_noStore as noStore } from 'next/cache';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import { encrypt, getSession } from '../lib/auth';
import { prisma } from '../lib/prisma';
import {
  FIELD_LIMITS,
  LIGHT_COLORS,
  TASK_RAILS,
  isPasswordWithinLimit,
  normalizeUsername,
  optionalText,
  requireDate,
  requireInt,
  requireOneOf,
  requireText,
  validateNewPassword,
  validateUsername,
} from '../lib/validation';

// ==========================================
// 1. AUTHENTICATION ACTIONS
// ==========================================

const BCRYPT_COST = 12;
const MAX_FAILED_LOGINS = 5;
const LOCK_MINUTES = 15;
const INVALID_CREDENTIALS = 'Invalid username or password';

// Compared against when the user does not exist so that response time does not reveal which usernames are registered
let dummyHash: Promise<string> | undefined;
const getDummyHash = () => (dummyHash ??= bcrypt.hash('not-a-real-password', BCRYPT_COST));

async function startSession(userId: string) {
  const session = await encrypt({ userId });
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  cookieStore.set('gof_session', session, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
    sameSite: 'lax',
    maxAge: 72 * 60 * 60, // 72 hours
    path: '/',
  });
}

function readField(formData: FormData, name: string): string {
  const value = formData?.get(name);
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : '';
}

export async function signInAction(formData: FormData) {
  const rawUsername = readField(formData, 'username');
  const password = readField(formData, 'password');

  if (!rawUsername || !password) {
    return { error: 'Username and password are required' };
  }
  // Oversized input is rejected before it reaches bcrypt or the database
  const username = normalizeUsername(rawUsername);
  if (username.length > FIELD_LIMITS.usernameMax || !isPasswordWithinLimit(password)) {
    return { error: INVALID_CREDENTIALS };
  }

  // Insensitive so accounts created before usernames were normalised can still sign in
  const user = await prisma.user.findFirst({
    where: { username: { equals: username, mode: 'insensitive' } },
  });

  if (!user) {
    await bcrypt.compare(password, await getDummyHash());
    return { error: INVALID_CREDENTIALS };
  }

  if (user.lockedUntil && user.lockedUntil > new Date()) {
    return { error: 'Too many failed attempts. Try again in a few minutes.' };
  }

  const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password);
  if (!isValid) {
    const { failedLoginCount } = await prisma.user.update({
      where: { id: user.id },
      data: { failedLoginCount: { increment: 1 } },
      select: { failedLoginCount: true },
    });
    if (failedLoginCount >= MAX_FAILED_LOGINS) {
      await prisma.user.update({
        where: { id: user.id },
        data: { failedLoginCount: 0, lockedUntil: new Date(Date.now() + LOCK_MINUTES * 60_000) },
      });
    }
    return { error: INVALID_CREDENTIALS };
  }

  if (user.failedLoginCount > 0 || user.lockedUntil) {
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { failedLoginCount: 0, lockedUntil: null } });
  }

  await startSession(user.id);
  return { success: true };
}

export async function signUpAction(formData: FormData) {
  const rawUsername = readField(formData, 'username');
  const password = readField(formData, 'password');
  const confirm = readField(formData, 'confirmPassword');

  const usernameCheck = validateUsername(rawUsername);
  if (usernameCheck.ok === false) return { error: usernameCheck.error };
  const { username } = usernameCheck;

  const passwordCheck = validateNewPassword(password, username);
  if (passwordCheck.ok === false) return { error: passwordCheck.error };
  if (password !== confirm) return { error: 'Passwords do not match' };

  const taken = await prisma.user.findFirst({
    where: { username: { equals: username, mode: 'insensitive' } },
    select: { id: true },
  });
  if (taken) return { error: 'That username is already taken' };

  const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(password, BCRYPT_COST);
  let userId: string;
  try {
    const user = await prisma.user.create({ data: { username, password: hashedPassword }, select: { id: true } });
    userId = user.id;
  } catch (e) {
    // Two signups for the same name racing past the check above
    if (e instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError && e.code === 'P2002') {
      return { error: 'That username is already taken' };
    }
    throw e;
  }

  await startSession(userId);
  return { success: true };
}

export async function logoutAction() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  cookieStore.delete('gof_session');
}

// ==========================================
// HELPER: ENSURE AUTHENTICATION
// ==========================================

async function requireAuth() {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session || !session.userId) {
    throw new Error('Unauthorized');
  }
  return session.userId;
}

// ==========================================
// 2. TASK ACTIONS (SECURED)
// ==========================================

export async function getTasks() {
  noStore(); 
  const userId = await requireAuth(); 

  return await prisma.task.findMany({
    where: { userId }, 
    orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' }
  });
}

function parseTaskInput(data: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined) {
  const rail = requireOneOf(data?.rail, TASK_RAILS, 'Rail');
  const notificationIntervalMin =
    data?.notificationIntervalMin === null || data?.notificationIntervalMin === undefined || data?.notificationIntervalMin === ''
      ? null
      : requireInt(data.notificationIntervalMin, 1, FIELD_LIMITS.maxMinutes, 'Notification interval');
  const warningRaw = Number(data?.warningThresholdMin);

  return {
    rail,
    title: requireText(data?.title, FIELD_LIMITS.title, 'Title'),
    deadline: requireDate(data?.deadline, 'Deadline'),
    importanceLevel: requireInt(data?.importanceLevel, 1, 5, 'Importance level'),
    lightColor: requireOneOf(data?.lightColor, LIGHT_COLORS, 'Light color'),
    microStep: requireText(data?.microStep, FIELD_LIMITS.microStep, 'Starter step'),
    warningThresholdMin: warningRaw ? requireInt(warningRaw, 1, FIELD_LIMITS.maxMinutes, 'Warning threshold') : 120,
    notificationIntervalMin,
  };
}

export async function createTaskAction(data: any) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();

  const newTask = await prisma.task.create({
    data: { userId, ...parseTaskInput(data) }
  });
  revalidatePath('/');
  return newTask;
}

export async function updateTaskAction(id: string, data: any) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();

  const updatedTask = await prisma.task.update({
    where: { id, userId }, 
    data: parseTaskInput(data)
  });
  revalidatePath('/');
  return updatedTask;
}

export async function deleteTaskAction(id: string) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();
  await prisma.task.delete({ where: { id, userId } });
  return true;
}

export async function completeTaskAction(id: string, score: string, reflection: string, completedAt: string, isFailed: boolean = false) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();
  return await prisma.task.update({
    where: { id, userId },
    data: {
      status: isFailed ? 'FAILED' : 'COMPLETED',
      score: requireText(score, FIELD_LIMITS.score, 'Score'),
      reflection: optionalText(reflection, FIELD_LIMITS.longText, 'Reflection') ?? '',
      completedAt: optionalText(completedAt, FIELD_LIMITS.completedAt, 'Completion time') ?? '',
    }
  });
}

export async function restoreTaskAction(id: string) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();
  return await prisma.task.update({
    where: { id, userId },
    data: {
      status: 'ACTIVE',
      score: null,
      reflection: null,
      completedAt: null,
    }
  });
}

// ==========================================
// 3. PROGRESS ENGINE ACTIONS (SECURED)
// ==========================================

export async function getMilestones() {
  noStore();
  const userId = await requireAuth();

  return await prisma.milestone.findMany({
    where: { userId },
    include: {
      metrics: { orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' } }
    },
    orderBy: { date: 'desc' } // Chronological sorting (newest first)
  });
}

export async function createMilestoneAction(dateString: string) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();
  const label = requireText(dateString, FIELD_LIMITS.dateString, 'Date');
  const dateObj = new Date(label);

  const newMilestone = await prisma.milestone.create({
    data: {
      userId,
      dateString: label,
      date: isNaN(dateObj.getTime()) ? new Date() : dateObj,
    },
    include: { metrics: true }
  });
  revalidatePath('/progress');
  return newMilestone;
}

export async function createMetricAction(milestoneId: string, name: string, val: number) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();
  const cleanName = requireText(name, FIELD_LIMITS.metricName, 'Metric name');
  const cleanVal = requireInt(val, -FIELD_LIMITS.maxValue, FIELD_LIMITS.maxValue, 'Metric value');
  
  // Verify ownership before modifying
  const milestone = await prisma.milestone.findUnique({ where: { id: milestoneId, userId } });
  if (!milestone) throw new Error('Unauthorized');

  await prisma.metric.create({
    data: { milestoneId, name: cleanName, val: cleanVal }
  });
  revalidatePath('/progress');
}

export async function updateMetricValAction(metricId: string, newVal: number) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();
  const cleanVal = requireInt(newVal, -FIELD_LIMITS.maxValue, FIELD_LIMITS.maxValue, 'Metric value');
  
  // Verify ownership
  const metric = await prisma.metric.findUnique({ where: { id: metricId }, include: { milestone: true } });
  if (!metric || metric.milestone.userId !== userId) throw new Error('Unauthorized');

  await prisma.metric.update({
    where: { id: metricId },
    data: { val: cleanVal }
  });
  revalidatePath('/progress');
}

export async function deleteMetricAction(metricId: string) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();
  
  // Verify ownership
  const metric = await prisma.metric.findUnique({ where: { id: metricId }, include: { milestone: true } });
  if (!metric || metric.milestone.userId !== userId) throw new Error('Unauthorized');

  await prisma.metric.delete({ where: { id: metricId } });
  revalidatePath('/progress');
}

export async function deleteMilestoneAction(milestoneId: string) {
  const userId = await requireAuth();
  
  // Verify ownership before deleting
  const milestone = await prisma.milestone.findUnique({ where: { id: milestoneId, userId } });
  if (!milestone) throw new Error('Unauthorized');

  // Because of onDelete: Cascade in the schema, this safely deletes all attached metrics too!
  await prisma.milestone.delete({ where: { id: milestoneId } });
  revalidatePath('/progress');
}
````

## File: components/goals/GoalDialog.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, ChevronRight, Ellipsis, Trash2, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { getGoal, renameGoal, setGoalStatus, deleteGoal, resumeGoal, setValidationPeriod } from '../../app/goals/actions';
import { useGoals } from '../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import type { GoalDetail, GoalPlanDTO, GoalStatus } from '../../types/goals';
import { VALIDATION_CHANGED_EVENT } from '../../types/validation';
import StatusSelect from './StatusSelect';
import WorkTimeBadge from './WorkTimeBadge';
import PlansSection from './dialog/PlansSection';
import PlanEditor from './dialog/PlanEditor';
import SubGoalsSection from './dialog/SubGoalsSection';
import TasksSection from './dialog/TasksSection';
import DeleteGoalConfirm from './dialog/DeleteGoalConfirm';
import ValidationBar from './dialog/ValidationBar';
import ValidateGoalDialog from './dialog/ValidateGoalDialog';
import PauseGoalDialog from './dialog/PauseGoalDialog';
import PauseBanner from './dialog/PauseBanner';
import HistorySection from './dialog/HistorySection';

const FOCUSABLE = 'a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';

// The body is keyed by goal id, so moving to another goal (or closing) resets all local state
export default function GoalDialog() {
  const { activeGoalId } = useGoals();
  if (!activeGoalId) return null;
  return <DialogBody key={activeGoalId} goalId={activeGoalId} />;
}

function DialogBody({ goalId }: { goalId: string }) {
  const { goals, patchGoal, canGoBack, goBack, closeDialog, openGoal, version, refresh, notify } = useGoals();
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<GoalDetail | null>(null);
  const [missing, setMissing] = useState(false);
  const [nameDraft, setNameDraft] = useState<string | null>(null); // null = not editing
  const [editingPlanId, setEditingPlanId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [confirmDelete, setConfirmDelete] = useState(false);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);
  const [modal, setModal] = useState<'validate' | 'pause' | 'pause-edit' | null>(null);
  const [resuming, setResuming] = useState(false);

  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const requestId = useRef(0);

  // Live rolled-up time from GoalsProvider so +15m updates the header instantly without refetching getGoal
  const liveGoal = useMemo(() => goals.find(g => g.id === goalId) ?? null, [goals, goalId]);

  // Load, and reload after every shared refresh (version bump)
  useEffect(() => {
    const myRequest = ++requestId.current;
    getGoal(goalId)
      .then(result => {
        if (myRequest !== requestId.current) return;
        if (!result) setMissing(true);
        else setDetail(result);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (myRequest === requestId.current) setMissing(true);
      });
  }, [goalId, version]);

  // Focus management and scroll lock while open
  useEffect(() => {
    const returnFocus = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    panelRef.current?.focus();
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
      returnFocus?.focus?.();
    };
  }, []);

  // Esc peels back one layer at a time: validate/pause modal -> confirm -> menu -> plan editor -> dialog
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key !== 'Escape') return;
      e.preventDefault();
      if (modal) setModal(null);
      else if (confirmDelete) setConfirmDelete(false);
      else if (menuOpen) setMenuOpen(false);
      else if (editingPlanId) setEditingPlanId(null);
      else closeDialog();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [modal, confirmDelete, menuOpen, editingPlanId, closeDialog]);

  const trapTab = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key !== 'Tab' || !panelRef.current) return;
    const items = Array.from(panelRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE)).filter(el => el.offsetParent !== null);
    if (items.length === 0) return;
    const first = items[0];
    const last = items[items.length - 1];
    const active = document.activeElement;
    if (e.shiftKey && (active === first || active === panelRef.current)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      last.focus();
    } else if (!e.shiftKey && active === last) {
      e.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    }
  };

  const commitName = async () => {
    if (!detail || nameDraft === null) return;
    const next = nameDraft.trim();
    setNameDraft(null);
    if (!next || next === detail.name) return;
    const res = await renameGoal(detail.id, next);
    if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
    setDetail(d => (d ? { ...d, name: res.data.name } : d));
    patchGoal(detail.id, { name: res.data.name });
  };

  const handleStatus = async (status: GoalStatus) => {
    if (!detail) return;
    // Pausing needs a reason and a resume date, collected in its own dialog
    if (status === 'PAUSED') return setModal('pause');
    const previous = detail.status;
    setDetail({ ...detail, status });
    const res = await setGoalStatus(detail.id, status);
    if (res.ok === false) {
      setDetail(d => (d ? { ...d, status: previous } : d));
      notify(res.error);
      return;
    }
    await refresh();
  };

  const handleResume = async () => {
    if (!detail) return;
    setResuming(true);
    try {
      const res = await resumeGoal(detail.id);
      if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
      notify('Goal resumed');
      await refresh();
    } catch {
      notify('Could not resume the goal.');
    } finally {
      setResuming(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePeriod = async (days: number) => {
    if (!detail) return;
    const res = await setValidationPeriod(detail.id, days);
    if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
    const patch = {
      validationPeriodDays: res.data.validationPeriodDays,
      validUntil: res.data.validUntil,
      needsValidation: res.data.needsValidation,
    };
    patchGoal(detail.id, patch);
    setDetail(d => (d ? { ...d, ...patch } : d));
    // Changing the period can flip whether this goal is due, so refresh the header shield badge
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event(VALIDATION_CHANGED_EVENT));
  };

  const handleModalDone = async (message: string) => {
    setModal(null);
    notify(message);
    await refresh();
  };

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!detail) return;
    setDeleting(true);
    try {
      const res = await deleteGoal(detail.id);
      if (res.ok === false) return notify(res.error);
      const { orphanedSubGoals, unattachedTasks } = res.data;
      const parts = [
        orphanedSubGoals ? `${orphanedSubGoals} sub-goal${orphanedSubGoals === 1 ? '' : 's'} now top-level` : '',
        unattachedTasks ? `${unattachedTasks} task${unattachedTasks === 1 ? '' : 's'} unattached` : '',
      ].filter(Boolean);
      notify(parts.length ? `Goal deleted · ${parts.join(', ')}` : 'Goal deleted');
      closeDialog();
      await refresh();
    } catch {
      notify('Could not delete the goal.');
    } finally {
      setDeleting(false);
    }
  };

  const patchPlan = useCallback((plan: GoalPlanDTO) => {
    setDetail(d => (d ? { ...d, plans: d.plans.map(p => (p.id === plan.id ? plan : p)) } : d));
  }, []);

  const handlePlanDeleted = useCallback(() => {
    setEditingPlanId(null);
    refresh();
  }, [refresh]);

  const editingPlan = detail?.plans.find(p => p.id === editingPlanId) ?? null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm"
      onMouseDown={e => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) closeDialog(); }}
    >
      <div
        ref={panelRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={detail ? `Goal: ${detail.name}` : 'Goal'}
        tabIndex={-1}
        onKeyDown={trapTab}
        className="w-full sm:max-w-3xl h-[94dvh] sm:h-auto sm:max-h-[88vh] flex flex-col rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-2xl border border-[#222222] bg-[#0a0a0a] shadow-2xl outline-none motion-safe:animate-[goal-sheet_0.22s_ease-out]"
      >
        <div className="sm:hidden mx-auto mt-2 h-1 w-10 rounded-full bg-neutral-700 shrink-0" />

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="shrink-0 px-4 sm:px-6 pt-3 sm:pt-5 pb-4 border-b border-[#1a1a1a]">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0 text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-500 overflow-x-auto">
              {canGoBack && (
                <button onClick={goBack} aria-label="Back" className="shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1 mr-2 px-2 py-1 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 text-neutral-300 hover:text-white hover:border-neutral-600 transition-colors">
                  <ArrowLeft className="w-3 h-3" /> Back
                </button>
              )}
              {detail?.ancestors.map(a => (
                <React.Fragment key={a.id}>
                  <button onClick={() => openGoal(a.id)} className="shrink-0 max-w-[140px] truncate hover:text-white transition-colors">{a.name}</button>
                  <ChevronRight className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0 text-neutral-700" />
                </React.Fragment>
              ))}
              <span className="shrink-0 text-neutral-400">{detail ? 'Goal' : 'Loading…'}</span>
            </div>
            <button onClick={closeDialog} aria-label="Close" className="shrink-0 w-8 h-8 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:border-neutral-600 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors">
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {detail && (
            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-3">
              <input
                value={nameDraft ?? detail.name}
                onFocus={() => setNameDraft(detail.name)}
                onChange={e => setNameDraft(e.target.value)}
                onBlur={commitName}
                onKeyDown={e => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter') (e.target as HTMLInputElement).blur();
                  if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.stopPropagation(); setNameDraft(null); (e.target as HTMLInputElement).blur(); }
                }}
                maxLength={120}
                aria-label="Goal name"
                className="flex-1 min-w-0 bg-transparent border-b border-transparent hover:border-neutral-800 focus:border-white font-headline text-xl sm:text-2xl font-semibold text-white outline-none transition-colors py-1"
              />
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 justify-between sm:justify-end">
                <StatusSelect
                  value={detail.status}
                  onChange={handleStatus}
                  lockedStatuses={detail.pausedByGoalId ? ['IN_PROGRESS'] : []}
                  lockedTitle={`Paused together with ${detail.pausedByGoalName ?? 'another goal'}. Resume that goal instead.`}
                />
                <div className="relative">
                  <button onClick={() => setMenuOpen(o => !o)} aria-label="Goal options" aria-expanded={menuOpen} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:border-neutral-600 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors">
                    <Ellipsis className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                  {menuOpen && (
                    <>
                      <button aria-label="Close menu" className="fixed inset-0 z-10 cursor-default" onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)} />
                      <div className="absolute right-0 mt-1 w-44 z-20 rounded-xl bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] p-1 shadow-xl">
                        <button onClick={() => { setMenuOpen(false); setConfirmDelete(true); }} className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-2.5 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs text-neutral-300 hover:bg-neutral-800 hover:text-white transition-colors text-left">
                          <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-500" /> Delete goal
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {detail && (
            <WorkTimeBadge
              deepMinutes={liveGoal?.deepMinutes ?? detail.deepMinutes}
              shallowMinutes={liveGoal?.shallowMinutes ?? detail.shallowMinutes}
              className="mt-2"
            />
          )}

          {detail && <PauseBanner detail={detail} busy={resuming} onResume={handleResume} onChangeDate={() => setModal('pause-edit')} onOpenRoot={id => openGoal(id, 'push')} />}
          {detail && <ValidationBar detail={detail} onValidate={() => setModal('validate')} onPeriodChange={handlePeriod} />}
        </div>

        {/* Body */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-5 space-y-8">
          {missing ? (
            <div className="py-16 text-center">
              <p className="text-sm text-neutral-300">This goal no longer exists.</p>
              <button onClick={closeDialog} className="mt-4 px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold">Close</button>
            </div>
          ) : !detail ? (
            <div className="space-y-4 animate-pulse" aria-busy="true">
              <div className="h-5 w-1/3 rounded bg-neutral-900" />
              <div className="h-16 rounded-xl bg-neutral-900" />
              <div className="h-5 w-1/4 rounded bg-neutral-900" />
              <div className="h-16 rounded-xl bg-neutral-900" />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              {confirmDelete && (
                <DeleteGoalConfirm
                  goalName={detail.name}
                  subGoals={detail.children.length}
                  tasks={liveGoal?.taskCount ?? detail.tasks.length}
                  plans={detail.plans.length}
                  busy={deleting}
                  onCancel={() => setConfirmDelete(false)}
                  onConfirm={handleDelete}
                />
              )}
              {editingPlanId ? (
                editingPlan ? (
                  <PlanEditor
                    key={editingPlan.id}
                    plan={editingPlan}
                    onBack={() => setEditingPlanId(null)}
                    onSaved={patchPlan}
                    onDeleted={handlePlanDeleted}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono text-neutral-500">
                    Loading plan…
                    <button onClick={() => setEditingPlanId(null)} className="text-neutral-300 hover:text-white">Back to plans</button>
                  </div>
                )
              ) : (
                <>
                  <PlansSection detail={detail} onOpenPlan={setEditingPlanId} />
                  <SubGoalsSection detail={detail} />
                  <TasksSection detail={detail} />
                  <HistorySection detail={detail} />
                </>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {detail && modal === 'validate' && (
        <ValidateGoalDialog
          goalId={detail.id}
          goalName={detail.name}
          ancestors={detail.ancestors}
          onClose={() => setModal(null)}
          onError={notify}
          onValidated={n => handleModalDone(`Validated ${n} goal${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`)}
        />
      )}

      {detail && (modal === 'pause' || modal === 'pause-edit') && (
        <PauseGoalDialog
          goalId={detail.id}
          goalName={detail.name}
          mode={modal === 'pause' ? 'create' : 'edit'}
          currentResumeAt={detail.pausedUntil}
          onClose={() => setModal(null)}
          onError={notify}
          onDone={handleModalDone}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/goals/GoalsTab.tsx
````typescript
"use client";

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Background, Controls, ReactFlow, ReactFlowProvider, useNodesState, useReactFlow,
  type Edge, type Node, type NodeMouseHandler,
} from '@xyflow/react';
import '@xyflow/react/dist/style.css';
import { ChevronDown, Network } from 'lucide-react';
import { useGoals } from '../../app/goals/GoalsProvider';
import type { GoalTree } from '../../types/goals';
import { buildGoalTree } from '../../lib/goals/graph';
import { layoutTree } from '../../lib/goals/treeLayout';
import GoalPicker from './GoalPicker';
import { GoalNode, TaskLeafNode, type GoalNodeData, type TaskNodeData } from './GoalNode';
import StatusBadge from './StatusBadge';

// Defined outside the component so React Flow does not remount nodes on every render
const nodeTypes = { goal: GoalNode, task: TaskLeafNode };

function buildNodeData(tree: GoalTree): Map<string, GoalNodeData | TaskNodeData> {
  const map = new Map<string, GoalNodeData | TaskNodeData>();
  for (const n of tree.nodes) {
    if (n.kind === 'goal') {
      map.set(n.id, {
        name: n.name,
        status: n.status,
        subGoalCount: n.subGoalCount,
        taskCount: n.taskCount,
        isRoot: n.id === tree.rootId,
        deepMinutes: n.deepMinutes,
        shallowMinutes: n.shallowMinutes,
      });
    } else {
      map.set(n.id, {
        title: n.title,
        metricType: n.metricType,
        unit: n.unit,
        current: n.current,
        target: n.target,
        goalId: n.goalId,
      });
    }
  }
  return map;
}

function buildFlow(tree: GoalTree): { nodes: Node[]; edges: Edge[] } {
  const positions = layoutTree(tree.rootId, tree.nodes.map(n => ({ id: n.id, level: n.level })), tree.edges);
  const dataById = buildNodeData(tree);

  const nodes: Node[] = tree.nodes.map(n => {
    const position = positions.get(n.id) ?? { x: 0, y: 0 };
    const data = dataById.get(n.id)!;
    if (n.kind === 'goal') {
      return { id: n.id, type: 'goal', position, data };
    }
    return { id: n.id, type: 'task', position, data, draggable: true };
  });

  const taskIds = new Set(tree.nodes.filter(n => n.kind === 'task').map(n => n.id));
  const edges: Edge[] = tree.edges.map(e => ({
    id: e.id,
    source: e.source,
    target: e.target,
    type: 'smoothstep',
    style: taskIds.has(e.target)
      ? { stroke: '#404040', strokeDasharray: '4 4' }
      : { stroke: '#737373', strokeWidth: 1.5 },
  }));

  return { nodes, edges };
}

function getTopologyKey(tree: GoalTree): string {
  const nodeIds = tree.nodes.map(n => `${n.kind}:${n.id}:${n.level}`).sort().join(',');
  const edgeIds = tree.edges.map(e => `${e.source}->${e.target}`).sort().join(',');
  return `${tree.rootId}|${nodeIds}|${edgeIds}`;
}

interface FlowProps {
  tree: GoalTree;
  onGoalClick: (goalId: string) => void;
}

function Flow({ tree, onGoalClick }: FlowProps) {
  const { fitView } = useReactFlow();
  const topologyKey = useMemo(() => getTopologyKey(tree), [tree]);
  const built = useMemo(() => buildFlow(tree), [tree]);
  const dataById = useMemo(() => buildNodeData(tree), [tree]);

  const [nodes, setNodes, onNodesChange] = useNodesState<Node>(built.nodes);
  const lastTopology = useRef<string>(topologyKey);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (lastTopology.current !== topologyKey) {
      // Structure changed: apply fresh layout and re-center camera
      lastTopology.current = topologyKey;
      setNodes(built.nodes);
      const t = setTimeout(() => fitView({ padding: 0.25, duration: 300, maxZoom: 1.1 }), 60);
      return () => clearTimeout(t);
    }
    // Data-only change (e.g., +15m or rename): patch node.data in place without resetting dragged positions or camera
    setNodes(prev => prev.map(n => {
      const nextData = dataById.get(n.id);
      return nextData ? { ...n, data: nextData } : n;
    }));
  }, [topologyKey, built.nodes, dataById, setNodes, fitView]);

  const handleNodeClick: NodeMouseHandler = useCallback((_, node) => {
    if (node.type === 'goal') onGoalClick(node.id);
    else {
      const data = node.data as TaskNodeData;
      if (data?.goalId) onGoalClick(data.goalId);
    }
  }, [onGoalClick]);

  return (
    <ReactFlow
      nodes={nodes}
      edges={built.edges}
      onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
      onNodeClick={handleNodeClick}
      nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
      colorMode="dark"
      nodesConnectable={false}
      elementsSelectable={false}
      minZoom={0.2}
      maxZoom={1.6}
      fitView
      style={{ background: '#000000' }}
    >
      <Background color="#1f1f1f" gap={24} />
      <Controls showInteractive={false} />
    </ReactFlow>
  );
}

export default function GraphTab() {
  const { goals, tasks, loading, openGoal } = useGoals();
  const [chosenRootId, setChosenRootId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [depth, setDepth] = useState<1 | 2>(1);
  const [showTasks, setShowTasks] = useState(false);
  const [showCompleted, setShowCompleted] = useState(false);
  const [pickerOpen, setPickerOpen] = useState(false);

  // The user's pick wins while it still exists; otherwise fall back to a sensible default
  const rootId = useMemo(() => {
    if (chosenRootId && goals.some(g => g.id === chosenRootId)) return chosenRootId;
    const candidate =
      goals.find(g => !g.parentId && g.status !== 'COMPLETED' && g.subGoalCount > 0) ??
      goals.find(g => !g.parentId && g.status !== 'COMPLETED') ??
      goals[0] ?? null;
    return candidate?.id ?? null;
  }, [chosenRootId, goals]);

  const visibleTree = useMemo(
    () => (rootId ? buildGoalTree(goals, tasks, rootId, depth, showTasks) : null),
    [goals, tasks, rootId, depth, showTasks],
  );

  const root = goals.find(g => g.id === rootId) ?? null;

  const pickable = useMemo(
    () => goals.filter(g => showCompleted || g.status !== 'COMPLETED' || g.id === rootId),
    [goals, showCompleted, rootId],
  );

  if (!loading && goals.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-neutral-800 py-14 text-center">
        <Network className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto text-neutral-700" />
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-300">Nothing to show yet</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-600">Create goals and link sub-goals to see them as a graph.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const hasChildren = !!visibleTree && visibleTree.nodes.some(n => n.kind === 'goal' && n.id !== visibleTree.rootId);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-3 lg:items-center">
        {/* Root selector */}
        <div className="relative lg:w-80">
          <button
            onClick={() => setPickerOpen(o => !o)}
            aria-haspopup="listbox"
            aria-expanded={pickerOpen}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[#262626] bg-[#141414] px-3.5 py-2.5 text-left hover:border-neutral-500 transition-colors"
          >
            <span className="min-w-0">
              <span className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">Parent goal</span>
              <span className="block text-sm text-white truncate">{root?.name ?? 'Select a goal'}</span>
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
              {root && <StatusBadge status={root.status} />}
              <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 text-neutral-500 transition-transform ${pickerOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
            </span>
          </button>

          {pickerOpen && (
            <>
              <button aria-label="Close" className="fixed inset-0 z-20 cursor-default" onClick={() => setPickerOpen(false)} />
              <div className="absolute z-30 mt-2 w-full shadow-2xl">
                <GoalPicker
                  goals={pickable}
                  onPick={g => { setChosenRootId(g.id); setPickerOpen(false); }}
                  placeholder="Search goals…"
                />
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Depth */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">Branching</span>
          <div role="radiogroup" aria-label="Branching level" className="inline-flex p-0.5 rounded-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800">
            {([1, 2] as const).map(d => (
              <button
                key={d}
                role="radio"
                aria-checked={depth === d}
                onClick={() => setDepth(d)}
                className={`w-10 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${depth === d ? 'bg-white text-black' : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'}`}
              >
                {d}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 lg:ml-auto text-xs text-neutral-400">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-white transition-colors">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={showTasks} onChange={e => setShowTasks(e.target.checked)} className="accent-white w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Show tasks
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-white transition-colors">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={showCompleted} onChange={e => setShowCompleted(e.target.checked)} className="accent-white w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Show completed
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative mt-4 h-[62vh] min-h-[380px] rounded-2xl border border-[#222222] overflow-hidden bg-black">
        {visibleTree && rootId ? (
          <>
            <ReactFlowProvider>
              <Flow tree={visibleTree} onGoalClick={id => openGoal(id)} />
            </ReactFlowProvider>
            {!hasChildren && !showTasks && (
              <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-10 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 bg-black/90 px-4 py-2 text-[11px] text-neutral-400 text-center">
                This goal has no sub-goals yet. Open it to create or link one.
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-mono text-neutral-600">
            {loading ? 'Building graph…' : 'Select a parent goal to see its branches.'}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

## File: components/AccomplishmentModal.tsx
````typescript
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Target, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { Task } from '../types';
import ImportanceLightsHeader from './ImportanceLightsHeader';
import { playSound } from '../audio'; // Added sound import
import { FIELD_LIMITS } from '../lib/validation';

interface AccomplishmentModalProps {
  task: Task;
  mode: 'complete' | 'review';
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (task: Task, text: string, score: string, mode: 'complete' | 'review') => void;
}

export default function AccomplishmentModal({ task, mode, onClose, onSave }: AccomplishmentModalProps) {
  // FIX: Removed 'presetAccomplishment' since it's not in the DB schema
  const [text, setText] = useState(
    mode === 'review' 
      ? (task.reflection || '') 
      : (task.microStep ? `Finished: ${task.microStep}` : '')
  );
  
  // FIX: Removed 'presetScore' since it's not in the DB schema
  const [score, setScore] = useState(
    mode === 'review' 
      ? (task.score ? task.score.replace('%', '') : '100')
      : '100'
  );

  // FIX: Explicitly cast lightColor to satisfy TypeScript
  const safeLightColor = (task.lightColor || 'white') as 'white' | 'red' | 'amber' | 'yellow';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-[#0e0e0e] border border-white/80 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-lg max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain p-4 sm:p-7 relative shadow-2xl flex flex-col gap-4 sm:gap-5 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between pb-3.5 border-b border-neutral-800">
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white tracking-tight font-headline">Task Accomplishment</h3>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border border-neutral-800 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:border-white transition-colors">
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
        
        <div className="p-4 bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] rounded-xl flex flex-col">
          <ImportanceLightsHeader level={task.importanceLevel || 4} lightColor={safeLightColor} />
          
          <p className="text-xs font-mono text-neutral-400 mb-1">
            Target: {new Date(task.deadline).toLocaleString([], { dateStyle: 'short', timeStyle: 'short' })}
            {task.completedAt && ` • Logged ${task.completedAt}`}
          </p>

          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white tracking-tight leading-snug">{task.title}</h4>
          {task.microStep && <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed font-sans">{task.microStep}</p>}
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-200 flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2"><span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white"></span><span>What was accomplished?</span></label>
          <div className="w-full bg-[#141414] border border-[#262626] focus-within:border-white rounded-xl p-3.5 transition-all">
            <textarea rows={3} value={text} maxLength={FIELD_LIMITS.longText} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} placeholder="Document outcome, breakthroughs, key findings or links..." className="w-full bg-transparent text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-100 placeholder-neutral-600 outline-none leading-relaxed resize-none font-sans" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-600 text-right mt-1">{text.length} / {FIELD_LIMITS.longText}</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-200 flex items-center gap-1.5"><span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white"></span><span>Expectation Score</span></label>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold font-mono text-white bg-neutral-900 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-neutral-700">Goal Met: {score ? (score.includes('%') ? score : `${score}%`) : '0%'}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="relative flex items-center bg-[#111111] border border-[#262626] focus-within:border-white rounded-xl transition-all overflow-hidden group">
            <div className="pl-4 pr-2 text-white flex items-center pointer-events-none"><Target className="w-4 h-4" /></div>
            <input type="number" min="0" max="300" value={score} onChange={(e) => setScore(e.target.value.slice(0, 6))} placeholder="100" className="w-full bg-transparent py-3 px-2 text-white font-headline text-base font-semibold tracking-wide outline-none placeholder-neutral-600" />
            <div className="pr-4 pl-2 flex items-center gap-1.5"><span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-white text-black select-none">%</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2 sm:gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-neutral-800 mt-1">
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-neutral-900 border border-transparent transition-all">Cancel</button>
          <button 
            type="button" 
            onClick={() => {
              // Play success sound before saving
              if (mode === 'complete') playSound('success');
              onSave(task, text, score, mode);
            }} 
            className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-white text-black font-bold text-xs hover:bg-neutral-200 active:scale-95 transition-all flex items-center gap-2 shadow-sm"
          >
            <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-black stroke-[3]" /><span>Save & Close</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````
